// Commands.SelfTest.FileOps.cpp
// Included from Commands.SelfTest.cpp — NOT compiled standalone.
// FileOps test family: 19 test functions.

[[nodiscard]] auto ResetFileOperationsIssuesPaneViewStateForTest(FolderWindow::FileOperationState* fileOps) noexcept
{
    std::wstring savedSortColumnId;
    bool savedSortDescending = false;
    std::vector<Common::Settings::GridColumnLayoutEntry> savedGridLayout;
    const bool hadSavedViewState = fileOps && fileOps->TryGetIssuesPaneViewState(savedSortColumnId, savedSortDescending, savedGridLayout);
    if (fileOps)
    {
        fileOps->DebugResetIssuesPaneForSelfTest();
    }

    return wil::scope_exit([fileOps,
                            hadSavedViewState,
                            savedSortColumnId = std::move(savedSortColumnId),
                            savedSortDescending,
                            savedGridLayout = std::move(savedGridLayout)]() mutable noexcept
    {
        if (! fileOps)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (hadSavedViewState)
        {
            fileOps->SaveIssuesPaneViewState(savedSortColumnId, savedSortDescending, savedGridLayout);
            return;
        }

        fileOps->SaveIssuesPaneViewState(L"", false, {});
    });
}

[[nodiscard]] HWND FindVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuWindowForFileOps(HWND ownerHwnd) noexcept
{
    const HWND rootOwner = ownerHwnd ? GetAncestor(ownerHwnd, GA_ROOT) : nullptr;
    for (HWND popup = FindWindowW(L"DxUi_ContextMenu", nullptr); popup != nullptr; popup = FindWindowExW(nullptr, popup, L"DxUi_ContextMenu", nullptr))
    {
        if (IsWindowVisible(popup) == FALSE)
        {
            continue;
        }

        if (ownerHwnd)
        {
            const HWND popupOwner = GetWindow(popup, GW_OWNER);
            if (popupOwner != ownerHwnd && popupOwner != rootOwner)
            {
                continue;
            }
        }

        return popup;
    }

    return nullptr;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool DismissVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuForFileOps(HWND ownerHwnd, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
    {
        const HWND menu = FindVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuWindowForFileOps(ownerHwnd);
        if (! menu)
        {
            return true;
        }

        PostMessageW(menu, WM_KEYDOWN, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
        PostMessageW(menu, WM_KEYUP, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }

    return FindVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuWindowForFileOps(ownerHwnd) == nullptr;
}

[[nodiscard]] std::wstring DescribeFileOpsValuePatternState(const std::optional<UiaValuePatternState>& state) noexcept
{
    if (! state.has_value())
    {
        return L"<missing>";
    }

    return std::format(L"name='{}' value='{}' readOnly={} controlType={}", state->name, state->value, state->isReadOnly ? 1 : 0, state->controlType);
}

[[nodiscard]] std::wstring DescribeFileOpsControlValueStates(const std::vector<UiaControlValueState>& states) noexcept
{
    if (states.empty())
    {
        return L"<none>";
    }

    std::wstring result;
    for (size_t index = 0; index < states.size(); ++index)
    {
        const auto& state = states[index];
        if (! result.empty())
        {
            result.append(L"; ");
        }

        result.append(std::format(L"#{} name='{}' value='{}' readOnly={} hasValuePattern={} hasValueProperty={} controlType={}",
                                  index,
                                  state.name,
                                  state.value,
                                  state.isReadOnly ? 1 : 0,
                                  state.hasValuePattern ? 1 : 0,
                                  state.hasValueProperty ? 1 : 0,
                                  state.controlType));
    }

    return result;
}

[[nodiscard]] std::wstring DescribeFileOpsPatternStats(const std::optional<UiaDescendantPatternStats>& stats) noexcept
{
    if (! stats.has_value())
    {
        return L"<missing>";
    }

    return std::format(L"visible={} edits={} values={} buttons={} invokes={} text={} toggles={} ranges={}",
                       stats->visibleElementCount,
                       stats->editControlCount,
                       stats->valuePatternCount,
                       stats->buttonControlCount,
                       stats->invokePatternCount,
                       stats->textPatternCount,
                       stats->togglePatternCount,
                       stats->rangeValuePatternCount);
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneUsesDxUiHostWithNoVisibleChildControls(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    if (! mainWindow || IsWindow(mainWindow) == FALSE)
    {
        state.Require(false, L"Main window handle invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable for issues-pane DX host baseline validation.");
    if (! fileOps)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const AppTheme previousTheme = g_folderWindow.GetTheme();
    const auto restoreTheme      = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(previousTheme); });
    const AppTheme backdropTheme = MakeWindowBackdropSelfTestTheme(Common::Settings::WindowBackdropMode::Acrylic, L"fileops-issues-backdrop-selftest");
    g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(backdropTheme);
    const Common::WindowBackdrop::Kind expectedToolBackdropKind =
        Common::WindowBackdrop::Resolve(Common::Settings::WindowBackdropMode::Acrylic, Common::WindowBackdrop::Target::Tool, false);

    const auto getVisibleIssuesPane = [fileOps]() noexcept -> HWND
    {
        const HWND pane = fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest();
        // This legacy fixture exercises technical-column interactions explicitly.
        if (pane)
            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane, true);
        return (pane && IsActuallyVisibleChildWindow(pane)) ? pane : nullptr;
    };

    const bool wasVisible       = g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible();
    const auto setIssuesVisible = [&](bool visible, std::wstring_view label) noexcept -> bool
    {
        if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
        {
            return true;
        }

        SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILEOPS_FAILED_ITEMS, 0), 0);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
            {
                return true;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        state.Require(false, std::format(L"File-operations issues pane did not switch to {} during {}.", visible ? L"visible" : L"hidden", label));
        return false;
    };
    const auto restoreVisibility = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept { static_cast<void>(setIssuesVisible(wasVisible, L"restore")); });

    constexpr size_t kTaskCount   = 2u;
    const uint64_t taskBase       = 0xF250000000000000ull + static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64() & 0x0000FFFFFFFFFFFFull);
    const auto cleanupDiagnostics = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept
    {
        for (size_t index = 0; index < kTaskCount; ++index)
        {
            fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(taskBase + index);
        }

        if (const HWND pane = getVisibleIssuesPane(); pane && IsWindow(pane) != FALSE)
        {
            static_cast<void>(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true));
        }
    });

    const std::array<std::wstring, kTaskCount> labels{
        L"issues-baseline-0",
        L"issues-baseline-1",
    };
    for (size_t index = 0; index < kTaskCount; ++index)
    {
        const uint64_t taskId = taskBase + index;
        fileOps->RecordTaskDiagnostic(taskId,
                                      (index % 2u) == 0u ? FILESYSTEM_COPY : FILESYSTEM_MOVE,
                                      (index % 2u) == 0u ? FolderWindow::FileOperationState::DiagnosticSeverity::Warning
                                                         : FolderWindow::FileOperationState::DiagnosticSeverity::Error,
                                      HRESULT_FROM_WIN32((index % 2u) == 0u ? ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED : ERROR_DISK_FULL),
                                      L"selftest.dxui.issues.baseline",
                                      labels[index],
                                      std::format(L"C:\\selftest-issues-baseline-source-{:02}.txt", index),
                                      std::format(L"D:\\selftest-issues-baseline-dest-{:02}.txt", index));
    }

    const auto validateIssuesPaneHostSurface = [&](std::wstring_view context) noexcept
    {
        state.Require(setIssuesVisible(true, context), std::format(L"Failed to show the file-operations issues pane during {}.", context));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        const HWND pane = WaitForWindow(getVisibleIssuesPane, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms));
        state.Require(pane != nullptr && IsWindow(pane) != FALSE, std::format(L"File operations issues pane did not open during {}.", context));
        if (! pane || IsWindow(pane) == FALSE)
        {
            return false;
        }

        state.Require(WaitForAppliedBackdropKind(pane, expectedToolBackdropKind, L"File Operations issues pane", state),
                      std::format(L"File Operations issues pane did not apply the selected Acrylic tool-window backdrop during {}.", context));
        state.Require(WindowExposesUiaProvider(pane), std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should answer WM_GETOBJECT during {}.", context));
        const size_t visibleChildWindowCount = CountVisibleChildWindows(pane);
        state.Require(visibleChildWindowCount == 0u,
                      std::format(L"Issues pane should not expose visible child-control fallback during {}; got {} visible child window(s).",
                                  context,
                                  visibleChildWindowCount));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true), std::format(L"Failed to request an issues-pane refresh during {}.", context));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        const auto waitForSnapshot = [&](auto&& predicate, FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& outSnapshot) noexcept
        {
            const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
            while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
            {
                PumpPendingMessages();
                if (FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot))
                {
                    return true;
                }
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
            }

            return FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot);
        };

        FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot snapshot{};
        state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
        { return value.rowCount >= kTaskCount && value.visibleWork.visibleRowCount > 0u; },
                                      snapshot),
                      std::format(L"File-operations issues pane did not repopulate visible DX rows during {}.", context));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        state.Require(snapshot.visibleWork.visibleColumnCount > 0u,
                      std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should expose visible DX columns during {}.", context));
        state.Require(snapshot.dxResizeFailureCount == 0u,
                      std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should not hit DX resize failures during {}; saw {}.", context, snapshot.dxResizeFailureCount));

        const uint64_t expectedTaskId = taskBase;
        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSelectTask(pane, expectedTaskId),
                      std::format(L"Failed to select issues-pane task {} during {}.", expectedTaskId, context));
        state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
        { return value.selectionCount == 1u && value.primarySelectedTaskId == expectedTaskId; },
                                      snapshot),
                      std::format(L"File-operations issues pane did not expose the selected task {} during {}.", expectedTaskId, context));
        const std::wstring expectedTaskIdText = std::to_wstring(static_cast<unsigned long long>(expectedTaskId));

        const auto uiaPatternStats = CollectVisibleUiaDescendantPatternStats(pane);
        state.Require(uiaPatternStats.has_value(),
                      std::format(L"Failed to collect live UI Automation pattern statistics for the issues pane during {}.", context));
        if (uiaPatternStats.has_value())
        {
            state.Require(uiaPatternStats->visibleElementCount > 0u,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should expose visible UI Automation descendants during {}.", context));
        }

        const auto selectionState = CollectVisibleDescendantSelectionPatternState(pane, UIA_DataGridControlTypeId);
        state.Require(selectionState.has_value(), std::format(L"Failed to collect UI Automation selection state for the issues pane during {}.", context));
        if (selectionState.has_value())
        {
            state.Require(selectionState->rootControlType == UIA_DataGridControlTypeId,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should expose a visible UI Automation DataGrid during {}.", context));
            state.Require(selectionState->hasSelectionPattern, std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should expose SelectionPattern during {}.", context));
            state.Require(selectionState->selectionCount == 1u,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues pane should expose exactly one selected UIA row during {}; saw {}.",
                                      context,
                                      selectionState->selectionCount));
            state.Require(selectionState->selectedControlType == UIA_DataItemControlTypeId,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues selected UIA row should expose the DataItem control type during {}.", context));
            state.Require(selectionState->selectedHasSelectionItemPattern,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues selected UIA row should expose SelectionItemPattern during {}.", context));
            state.Require(! selectionState->selectedName.empty(),
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues selected UIA row should expose a non-empty accessible name during {}.", context));
            state.Require(selectionState->selectedName.find(expectedTaskIdText) != std::wstring::npos,
                          std::format(L"File-operations issues selected UIA row name '{}' should include the selected task id '{}' during {}.",
                                      selectionState->selectedName,
                                      expectedTaskIdText,
                                      context));
        }
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        state.Require(setIssuesVisible(false, context), std::format(L"Failed to hide the file-operations issues pane during {}.", context));
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline && getVisibleIssuesPane() != nullptr)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        state.Require(getVisibleIssuesPane() == nullptr, std::format(L"File operations issues pane did not close during {}.", context));
        return state.failure.empty();
    };

    if (! validateIssuesPaneHostSurface(L"the initial issues-pane DX host baseline probe"))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (! validateIssuesPaneHostSurface(L"the reopened issues-pane DX host baseline probe"))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return state.failure.empty();
}

// A private window and its token are destroyed before this synchronous fixture returns;
// the application-owned FolderWindow and FileOperationState remain alive throughout.
[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneTypedDecisionProjection(
    HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state, uint32_t repeatIndex, bool inspectDetail = false, unsigned qualifiedSubject = 0u, bool inspectPrimary = false) noexcept
{
    using State   = FolderWindow::FileOperationState;
    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state is required.");
    if (! fileOps)
        return false;
    RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker blocker;
    if (! RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker::IsActiveForCurrentThread())
        state.Require(blocker.Start(), L"Static Issues capture must block activation before window creation.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    auto restoreView = ResetFileOperationsIssuesPaneViewStateForTest(fileOps);
    Localization::LanguagePreference previousLanguage{};
    if (g_settings.ui.has_value() && ! g_settings.ui->language.empty() && g_settings.ui->language != L"system")
        previousLanguage = {Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, g_settings.ui->language};
    auto restoreLanguage = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        if (inspectDetail)
            static_cast<void>(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference(previousLanguage));
    });
    if (inspectDetail)
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, L"fr-FR"})),
                      L"French detail resources unavailable.");
    auto lifetime = std::make_shared<int>(0);
    wil::unique_hwnd pane(FileOperationsIssuesPane::Create(fileOps, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
    state.Require(static_cast<bool>(pane), L"Private Issues window creation failed.");
    if (! pane)
        return false;
    MONITORINFO monitor{sizeof(MONITORINFO)};
    state.Require(GetMonitorInfoW(MonitorFromWindow(mainWindow, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST), &monitor) != FALSE, L"Capture monitor unavailable.");
    SetWindowPos(pane.get(),
                 HWND_BOTTOM,
                 monitor.rcWork.left + 24,
                 monitor.rcWork.top + 24,
                 std::min(1200L, monitor.rcWork.right - monitor.rcWork.left - 48),
                 std::min(700L, monitor.rcWork.bottom - monitor.rcWork.top - 48),
                 SWP_NOACTIVATE);
    const uint64_t taskId = 0xF400000000000000ull + GetTickCount64();
    auto cleanup          = wil::scope_exit([&] { fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(taskId); });
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot initial{};
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), initial, inspectDetail);
    State::TaskDiagnosticEntry problem{};
    problem.taskId          = taskId;
    problem.operation       = FILESYSTEM_COPY;
    problem.localTime       = SYSTEMTIME{2026, 9, 1, 21, 12, 0, 0, 0};
    problem.severity        = State::DiagnosticSeverity::Warning;
    problem.status          = HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_FILE_EXISTS);
    problem.category        = L"item.conflict.prompt";
    problem.message         = L"Une photographie portant ce nom existe déjà dans le dossier de destination.";
    problem.sourcePath      = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales de plusieurs générations\\Photographie originale de la réunion familiale.raw";
    problem.destinationPath = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Collection de photographies déjà présentes\\Photographie originale de la réunion familiale.raw";
    problem.conflictPrompt  = FileOperations::ConflictPromptObservation{10u, FileOperations::ConflictClass::RegularFileExists, 0u};
    if (qualifiedSubject != 0u)
    {
        auto& display                 = problem.conflictPrompt.value();
        display.sourceName            = L"Photographie originale de la réunion familiale.raw";
        display.sourceParentPath      = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales de plusieurs générations";
        display.destinationName       = qualifiedSubject == 2u ? L"Version retouchée de la réunion familiale.raw" : display.sourceName;
        display.destinationParentPath = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Collection de photographies déjà présentes";
        problem.destinationPath       = display.destinationParentPath + L"\\" + display.destinationName;
    }
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(problem);
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane.get(), false);
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSelectTask(pane.get(), taskId), L"Original conflict must be selectable.");
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot selected{};
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), selected, inspectDetail);
    auto second                       = problem;
    second.conflictPrompt->generation = 11u;
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(second);
    auto legacy = problem;
    legacy.conflictPrompt.reset();
    legacy.category = L"legacy.conflict";
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(legacy);
    auto decision                        = problem;
    decision.severity                    = State::DiagnosticSeverity::Info;
    decision.category                    = L"item.conflict.decision";
    decision.acceptedDecision            = FileOperations::AcceptedConflictDecision{};
    auto& accepted                       = decision.acceptedDecision.value();
    accepted.taskId                      = taskId;
    accepted.promptGeneration            = 10u;
    accepted.sourceItemIndex             = 0u;
    accepted.action                      = FileOperations::ConflictAction::Overwrite;
    accepted.scope.bucket                = FileOperations::ConflictClass::RegularFileExists;
    accepted.applyToAll                  = true;
    accepted.originatingPromptGeneration = 10u;
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(decision);
    auto cached                               = decision;
    cached.conflictPrompt->generation         = 12u;
    cached.acceptedDecision->promptGeneration = 12u;
    cached.acceptedDecision->origin           = FileOperations::ConflictDecisionOrigin::CachedScope;
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(cached);
    const auto refreshStarted = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane.get(), false);
    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Issues.TypedProjection.RefreshUs",
                      L"",
                      static_cast<uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - refreshStarted).count()),
                      5u,
                      0u,
                      S_OK);
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot projected{};
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), projected, inspectDetail);
    const auto root     = SelfTest::GetSuiteArtifactPath(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands,
                                                         inspectPrimary           ? L"issues-primary-grid"
                                                         : qualifiedSubject == 2u ? L"issues-different-subject"
                                                         : qualifiedSubject == 1u ? L"issues-qualified-subject"
                                                         : inspectDetail          ? L"issues-complete-detail"
                                                                                  : L"issues-typed-projection");
    const auto captures = root.empty() ? std::filesystem::path{} : root / std::format(L"repeat-{}", repeatIndex);
    state.Require(! captures.empty() && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(captures), L"Issues capture directory unavailable.");
    if (! captures.empty())
    {
        RedrawWindow(pane.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(pane.get(), captures / L"typed-conflicts.png")),
                      L"Issues harness capture failed.");
    }
    if (inspectPrimary)
    {
        state.Require(projected.columnIds == std::vector<std::wstring>{L"subject", L"problem", L"summary-operation", L"decision", L"result"},
                      L"Default Issues columns must be file, problem, operation, decision and result; technical columns are opt-in.");
        if (projected.selectedCells.size() == 5u)
            state.Require(projected.selectedCells[0] == problem.conflictPrompt->sourceName && projected.selectedCells[1] == problem.message &&
                              projected.selectedCells[3].find(L"Remplacer") != std::wstring::npos && projected.selectedCells[4] == L"Résultat inconnu",
                          L"The summary must identify the file and keep accepted choice separate from unknown execution result.");
    }
    if (inspectDetail)
    {
        state.Require(projected.detailPresent && ! projected.technicalExpanded, L"Selected detail must exist with technical details collapsed.");
        state.Require(
            projected.detailTexts[0].find(problem.message) != std::wstring::npos &&
                projected.detailTexts[4].find(qualifiedSubject ? problem.conflictPrompt->sourceParentPath : problem.sourcePath) != std::wstring::npos &&
                projected.detailTexts[5].find(qualifiedSubject ? problem.conflictPrompt->destinationParentPath : problem.destinationPath) != std::wstring::npos,
            L"Detail must retain the complete French problem and exact stacked paths.");
        state.Require(projected.detailTexts[2].find(L"Remplacer") != std::wstring::npos &&
                          projected.detailTexts[3].find(L"Résultat inconnu") != std::wstring::npos,
                      L"An accepted replacement must be separate from the unknown execution result.");
        const auto icon = [&](wchar_t fluent, wchar_t fallback) noexcept { return projected.detailIconsUseFluentFont ? fluent : fallback; };
        state.Require(projected.detailIconGlyphs[0] == icon(FluentIcons::kWarning, FluentIcons::kFallbackWarning) &&
                          projected.detailIconGlyphs[2] == icon(FluentIcons::kForward, FluentIcons::kFallbackForward) && projected.detailIconGlyphs[3] == L'?',
                      L"Problem, accepted decision, and unknown result require distinct nonverbal symbols; acceptance is not success.");
        for (const size_t index : {0u, 2u, 3u})
            state.Require(projected.detailIconBounds[index].right + 4.0f <= projected.detailTextBounds[index].left &&
                              projected.detailIconBounds[index].left >= projected.detailViewport.left &&
                              projected.detailTextBounds[index].right <= projected.detailViewport.right,
                          L"Each semantic icon needs a separate measured gutter inside the selected detail viewport.");
        if (! projected.themeHighContrast)
            state.Require(ColorToCOLORREF(D2D1::ColorF(projected.detailIconColors[2].r, projected.detailIconColors[2].g, projected.detailIconColors[2].b)) ==
                              ColorToCOLORREF(g_folderWindow.GetTheme().accent),
                          L"Accepted decision may use accent on the icon while its complete label retains normal text contrast.");
        state.Require(projected.detailTextBounds[0].top >= projected.detailViewport.top && projected.detailTextBounds[0].left >= projected.detailViewport.left,
                      L"The first complete problem must begin inside the unscrolled detail viewport in host coordinates.");
        state.Require(projected.detailTextBounds[4].bottom <= projected.detailTextBounds[5].top, L"Source must remain above destination.");
        if (qualifiedSubject != 0u)
        {
            const auto& display      = problem.conflictPrompt.value();
            std::wstring visibleText = projected.detailTexts[8];
            for (size_t i = 0u; i < 6u; ++i)
                visibleText += L"\n" + projected.detailTexts[i];
            const auto exactlyOnce = [&](std::wstring_view name) noexcept
            { return visibleText.find(name) != std::wstring::npos && visibleText.find(name) == visibleText.rfind(name); };
            state.Require(projected.detailTexts[8].find(display.sourceName) != std::wstring::npos && exactlyOnce(display.sourceName) &&
                              (qualifiedSubject != 2u ||
                               (projected.detailTexts[8].find(display.destinationName) != std::wstring::npos && exactlyOnce(display.destinationName))) &&
                              projected.detailTextBounds[8].bottom <= projected.detailTextBounds[0].top,
                          L"Qualified filenames must appear once in a prominent subject above the problem, with folders rather than repeated filenames below.");
            state.Require(projected.detailCopyText.find(problem.sourcePath) != std::wstring::npos &&
                              projected.detailCopyText.find(problem.destinationPath) != std::wstring::npos,

        }
        const UINT dpi = GetDpiForWindow(pane.get());
        RECT shortWindow{0, 0, MulDiv(520, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96), MulDiv(320, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)};

                                 static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(pane.get(), GWL_STYLE)),

            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot frame{};

            {.timeout = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(2000)), .operationName = L"resized Issues detail frame"});

        FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), shortDetail, inspectDetail);
        if (inspectPrimary)
        {
            state.Require(shortDetail.columnIds == std::vector<std::wstring>{L"subject", L"problem", L"decision", L"result"},

                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(problemRect.right * pixelsPerDip)),
                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(std::min(problemRect.bottom, shortDetail.detailViewport.bottom) * pixelsPerDip))};

            ColorToCOLORREF(qualifiedSubject ? g_folderWindow.GetTheme().folderView.textNormal : g_folderWindow.GetTheme().folderView.warningText);

        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestDetailKey(pane.get(), VK_END), L"Detail routed End must scroll to the end.");
        FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), shortDetail, inspectDetail);
        state.Require(shortDetail.detailScrollOffset > 0.0f, L"Short detail body must expose scrollable complete content.");
        const auto captionRect = shortDetail.technicalBounds;
        const auto px          = [&](float dip) { return static_cast<LONG>(std::lround(dip * static_cast<float>(GetDpiForWindow(pane.get())) / 96.0f)); };
        std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 1> captionProbe{};

                                                 px(captionRect.top + 6.0f),
                                                 px(captionRect.left + (captionRect.right - captionRect.left) * 0.40f),

        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane.get(), true), L"Technical disclosure must expand.");
        FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestDetailKey(pane.get(), VK_END);
        FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), shortDetail, inspectDetail);

        FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestDetailKey(pane.get(), VK_HOME);

    state.Require(projected.rowCount == initial.rowCount + 4u,
                  std::format(L"Two explicit prompts, one legacy issue and one cached application must remain four rows; observed delta {}.",
                              projected.rowCount - initial.rowCount));
    state.Require(projected.primarySelectedRowId == selected.primarySelectedRowId && projected.primarySelectedTaskId == taskId,

    result.conflictResult = FileOperations::ConflictResultObservation{{taskId, 10u},

         {FileOperations::ItemCompletion::Canceled, IDS_FILEOPS_STATUS_CANCELED},

                                                                                  {L"operation", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_OPERATION},
                                                                                  {L"severity", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_SEVERITY},
                                                                                  {L"hresult", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_HRESULT},
                                                                                  {L"status", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_STATUS_TEXT},
                                                                                  {L"category", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_CATEGORY},
                                                                                  {L"message", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_MESSAGE},
                                                                                  {L"source", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_SOURCE},
                                                                                  {L"destination", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_DESTINATION},
                                                                                  {L"subject", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_FILE},
                                                                                  {L"decision", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_DECISION},
                                                                                  {L"result", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_RESULT},
                                                                                  {L"problem", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_PROBLEM},
                                                                                  {L"summary-operation", IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_OPERATION}}};
        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestFocusGrid(pane.get()), L"Live locale fixture requires logical grid focus.");
        for (const bool technical : {false, true})
        {
            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane.get(), technical);
            for (const auto* culture : {L"en-US", L"fr-FR"})
            {
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot beforeLanguage{};
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), beforeLanguage, true);
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, culture})),
                              L"Live Issues coverage requires the English and French satellites.");
                const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
                SendMessageW(pane.get(), WM_THEMECHANGED, 0, 0);
                const auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot localized{};
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), localized, true);
                const auto scenario = std::format(L"{}-{}", technical ? L"technical" : L"primary", culture);
                Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Issues.LiveLanguage.NotifyUs",
                                  scenario,
                                  static_cast<uint64_t>(duration),
                                  localized.rowCount,
                                  localized.refreshGeneration - beforeLanguage.refreshGeneration,
                                  S_OK);
                state.Require(Win32Text::GetWindowTextString(pane.get()) == LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_PANE_TITLE) &&
                                  localized.copyCaption == LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COPY_DETAIL) &&
                                  localized.technicalCaption ==
                                      LoadStringResource(nullptr, technical ? IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_HIDE_TECHNICAL : IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_TECHNICAL) &&
                                  localized.emptyStateText == LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_DXUI_NO_DATA) &&
                                  localized.detailAccessibleName == LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_SELECT_DETAIL),
                              L"A live Issues language change must refresh title, actions, empty-state and accessible detail names together.");
                state.Require(Win32Text::GetWindowTextString(pane.get()) ==
                                  (std::wstring_view(culture) == L"fr-FR" ? L"Problèmes lors des opérations sur les fichiers" : L"File operation issues"),
                              L"The native caption must include pending and resolved issues, rather than describing every row as failed.");
                for (size_t column = 0u; column < localized.columnIds.size(); ++column)
                {
                    const auto expected = std::find_if(
                        columnResources.begin(), columnResources.end(), [&](const auto& entry) { return entry.first == localized.columnIds[column]; });
                    state.Require(expected != columnResources.end() && localized.columnTitles[column] == LoadStringResource(nullptr, expected->second),
                                  L"Both primary and technical grid headings must follow the live language.");
                }
                state.Require(localized.primarySelectedRowId == beforeLanguage.primarySelectedRowId && localized.gridFocused &&
                                  localized.sortColumnIndex == beforeLanguage.sortColumnIndex && localized.sortDescending == beforeLanguage.sortDescending &&
                                  localized.columnIds == beforeLanguage.columnIds && localized.technicalExpanded == technical,
                              L"Relocalization must retain row identity, logical focus, sort, disclosure and column identities.");
                for (size_t column = 0u; column < localized.columnBounds.size() && column < beforeLanguage.columnBounds.size(); ++column)
                    state.Require(std::abs(localized.columnBounds[column].left - beforeLanguage.columnBounds[column].left) < 0.5f &&
                                      std::abs(localized.columnBounds[column].right - beforeLanguage.columnBounds[column].right) < 0.5f,
                                  L"Live language changes must preserve column order, widths and horizontal position.");
                state.Require(localized.detailTexts[0].find(problem.message) != std::wstring::npos &&
                                  localized.detailTexts[1].find(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_CMD_COPY)) != std::wstring::npos &&
                                  localized.detailTexts[2].find(FileOperations::FormatConflictActionLabel(FileOperations::ConflictAction::Overwrite,
                                                                                                          FileOperations::ConflictClass::RegularFileExists,
                                                                                                          FILESYSTEM_COPY)) != std::wstring::npos,
                              L"Localized row/detail labels must update while the recorded original problem remains unchanged.");
                if (! technical)
                    state.Require(localized.hiddenSortText.find(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_ISSUES_COL_TASK)) != std::wstring::npos,
                                  L"A hidden sort must name its column in the active language.");
                const auto unchangedThemeStarted = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
                for (unsigned notification = 0u; notification < 5u; ++notification)
                    SendMessageW(pane.get(), WM_THEMECHANGED, 0, 0);
                const auto unchangedThemeDuration =
                    std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - unchangedThemeStarted).count();
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot stable{};
                FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane.get(), stable, true);
                Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Issues.ThemeUnchanged.NotifyBatchUs",
                                  scenario,
                                  static_cast<uint64_t>(unchangedThemeDuration),
                                  5u,
                                  stable.refreshGeneration - localized.refreshGeneration,
                                  S_OK);
                state.Require(stable.refreshGeneration == localized.refreshGeneration, L"Unchanged theme notifications must not publish a new row projection.");
                RedrawWindow(pane.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(pane.get(), captures / (scenario + L"-live-language.png"))),
                              L"Live Issues language capture failed.");
            }
        }
        FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane.get(), false);
        pane.reset();
        std::wstring savedSort;
        bool descending = false;
        std::vector<Common::Settings::GridColumnLayoutEntry> saved;
        state.Require(fileOps->TryGetIssuesPaneViewState(savedSort, descending, saved), L"Closing the primary view must retain both layouts.");
        const auto taskColumn = std::find_if(saved.begin(), saved.end(), [](const auto& value) { return value.columnId == L"task"; });
        state.Require(savedSort == L"task" && descending && saved.size() == 15u && taskColumn != saved.end() && taskColumn->displayIndex == 0u &&
                          std::abs(taskColumn->widthDip - 149.0f) < 0.5f,
                      L"Closing with technical columns hidden must not discard or overwrite their saved preferences.");
    }
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesDirectTaskProjection(CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using State   = FolderWindow::FileOperationState;
    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state is required for direct Issues projection.");
    if (! fileOps)
        return false;
    RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker blocker;
    if (! RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker::IsActiveForCurrentThread())
        state.Require(blocker.Start(), L"Direct Issues probe must block activation before pane creation.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    // Commands isolation closes visible windows, but an inherited hidden Issues pane
    // can survive. Start this case with no retained pane or selection authority.
    auto restoreView = ResetFileOperationsIssuesPaneViewStateForTest(fileOps);
    state.Require(fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest() == nullptr, L"Direct Issues fixture must start without an inherited pane.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    const uint64_t firstTaskId            = 0xF510000000000000ull + GetTickCount64();
    const uint64_t completedTaskId        = firstTaskId + 1u;
    const uint64_t invalidCompletedTaskId = firstTaskId + 2u;
    auto cleanup                          = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(firstTaskId);
        fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(completedTaskId);
        fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(invalidCompletedTaskId);
        // Destroy even a hidden test-owned pane; restoreView then restores exact
        // saved sort/column preferences after the pane's close callback runs.
        fileOps->DebugResetIssuesPaneForSelfTest();
    });

    const auto contains = [fileOps](uint64_t taskId) noexcept
    {
        std::vector<uint64_t> ids;
        fileOps->CollectProjectableIssueTaskIds(ids);
        return std::binary_search(ids.begin(), ids.end(), taskId);
    };
    state.Require(! contains(firstTaskId) && ! contains(completedTaskId) && ! contains(invalidCompletedTaskId),
                  L"Fresh task IDs must not inherit unrelated Issues rows.");

    State::TaskDiagnosticEntry invalid{};
    invalid.taskId                           = firstTaskId;
    invalid.severity                         = State::DiagnosticSeverity::Error;
    invalid.conflictResult                   = FileOperations::ConflictResultObservation{};
    invalid.conflictResult.value().reference = {firstTaskId + 99u, 1u};
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(invalid);
    state.Require(! contains(firstTaskId) && ! fileOps->OpenIssuesForTask(firstTaskId),
                  L"An Error-severity result with a foreign task reference must not create a direct Issues action.");
    invalid.conflictResult.reset();
    invalid.acceptedDecision                          = FileOperations::AcceptedConflictDecision{};
    invalid.acceptedDecision.value().taskId           = firstTaskId;
    invalid.acceptedDecision.value().promptGeneration = 1u;
    invalid.acceptedDecision.value().action           = FileOperations::ConflictAction::None;
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(invalid);
    state.Require(! contains(firstTaskId), L"An invalid accepted decision must not fall through as a legacy Error row.");

    State::TaskDiagnosticEntry ordinary{};
    ordinary.taskId   = firstTaskId;
    ordinary.severity = State::DiagnosticSeverity::Warning;
    ordinary.message  = L"Promptless retained warning";
    fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(ordinary);
    state.Require(contains(firstTaskId), L"A retained promptless warning must enable direct Issues.");
    // Separate legacy row identities put the completed-only task beyond the initial
    // viewport. Selecting it must actually scroll, not merely change selection IDs.
    for (unsigned index = 0u; index < 32u; ++index)
    {
        ordinary.message = std::format(L"Retained warning {}", index);
        fileOps->EnqueueTaskDiagnostic(ordinary);
    }
    State::CompletedTaskSummary emptyCompleted{};
    emptyCompleted.taskId = firstTaskId;
    fileOps->DebugAppendCompletedTaskForSelfTest(std::move(emptyCompleted));
    state.Require(contains(firstTaskId), L"A retained live warning remains projectable when its completed summary has no issue copies.");

    State::TaskDiagnosticEntry completedDecision{};
    completedDecision.taskId                          = completedTaskId;
    completedDecision.severity                        = State::DiagnosticSeverity::Info;
    completedDecision.conflictPrompt                  = FileOperations::ConflictPromptObservation{7u, FileOperations::ConflictClass::RegularFileExists, 0u};
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision                = FileOperations::AcceptedConflictDecision{};
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision.value().taskId = completedTaskId;
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision.value().promptGeneration = 7u;
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision.value().sourceItemIndex  = 0u;
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision.value().scope.bucket     = FileOperations::ConflictClass::RegularFileExists;
    completedDecision.acceptedDecision.value().action           = FileOperations::ConflictAction::Skip;
    State::CompletedTaskSummary completed{};
    completed.taskId = completedTaskId;
    completed.issueDiagnostics.push_back(completedDecision);
    fileOps->DebugAppendCompletedTaskForSelfTest(std::move(completed));

    State::CompletedTaskSummary invalidCompleted{};
    invalidCompleted.taskId       = invalidCompletedTaskId;
    invalidCompleted.warningCount = 1u;
    auto staleIssue               = completedDecision;
    staleIssue.taskId             = invalidCompletedTaskId;
    invalidCompleted.issueDiagnostics.push_back(staleIssue);
    fileOps->DebugAppendCompletedTaskForSelfTest(std::move(invalidCompleted));
    state.Require(contains(completedTaskId) && ! contains(invalidCompletedTaskId),
                  L"Only the valid completed accepted decision must project; nonempty stale issue retention is insufficient.");

    state.Require(fileOps->OpenIssuesForTask(firstTaskId), L"Direct Issues must open and select the retained live warning.");
    const HWND pane = fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest();
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot selected{};
    state.Require(pane && fileOps->IsIssuesPaneVisible(), L"Direct Issues must leave its task pane visible.");
    const auto selectedRowIsVisible = [&selected]() noexcept
    {
        const auto bounds = selected.primarySelectedRowBounds;
        return bounds.right > bounds.left && bounds.bottom > bounds.top;
    };
    state.Require(pane && FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, selected, true) && selected.primarySelectedTaskId == firstTaskId &&
                      selectedRowIsVisible(),
                  L"Direct Issues must select its originating task row.");
    state.Require(fileOps->OpenIssuesForTask(completedTaskId) && pane == fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest(),
                  L"Direct Issues must reuse an already-visible pane for completed retention.");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, selected, true) && selected.primarySelectedTaskId == completedTaskId &&
                      selectedRowIsVisible(),
                  L"Reopening Issues must select and scroll the completed task into the viewport.");
    state.Require(fileOps->OpenIssuesForTask(firstTaskId) && FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, selected, true) &&
                      selected.primarySelectedTaskId == firstTaskId && selectedRowIsVisible(),
                  L"Returning to the earlier task must scroll its selected row back into view.");
    state.Require(! fileOps->OpenIssuesForTask(invalidCompletedTaskId), L"A stale completed typed observation must not open or select an Issues row.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneTeardownReleasesState(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable.");
    if (! fileOps)
        return false;
    RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker blocker;
    if (! RedSalamander::TestSupport::ScopedWindowActivationBlocker::IsActiveForCurrentThread())
        state.Require(blocker.Start(), L"Teardown fixtures must block activation.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    auto lifetime                = std::make_shared<int>(0);
    const uint64_t stateBaseline = FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestLiveStateCount();
    const auto hostsBaseline     = DxUi::DebugGetAttachedWindowHostCount();
    auto resetFailure            = wil::scope_exit([] { FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestFailNextCreation(false); });
    for (size_t index = 0u; index < 12u; ++index)
    {
        const bool failCreation = (index % 3u) == 0u;
        FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestFailNextCreation(failCreation);
        wil::unique_hwnd pane(FileOperationsIssuesPane::Create(fileOps, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
        state.Require(static_cast<bool>(pane) != failCreation, L"The forced WM_CREATE failure must reject only its own fixture.");
        if (pane)
            state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestLiveStateCount() == stateBaseline + 1u,
                          L"A live Issues window must own exactly one adapter state.");
        pane.reset();
        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestLiveStateCount() == stateBaseline && DxUi::DebugGetAttachedWindowHostCount() == hostsBaseline,
                      L"Closing or failing Issues creation must release both adapter state and the shared host.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
    }
    return true;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneDiffRefreshPreservesSelection(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    if (! mainWindow || IsWindow(mainWindow) == FALSE)
    {
        state.Require(false, L"Main window handle invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable for issues-pane diff-refresh validation.");
    if (! fileOps)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto getVisibleIssuesPane = [fileOps]() noexcept -> HWND
    {
        const HWND pane = fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest();
        // This legacy fixture exercises technical-column interactions explicitly.
        if (pane)
            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane, true);
        return (pane && IsActuallyVisibleChildWindow(pane)) ? pane : nullptr;
    };

    const bool wasVisible        = g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible();
    const auto restoreVisibility = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept
    {
        const bool visibleNow = g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible();
        if (visibleNow != wasVisible)
        {
            SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILEOPS_FAILED_ITEMS, 0), 0);
            const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
            while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline && g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() != wasVisible)
            {
                PumpPendingMessages();
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
            }
        }
    });

    if (! wasVisible)
    {
        SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILEOPS_FAILED_ITEMS, 0), 0);
    }

    const HWND pane = WaitForWindow(getVisibleIssuesPane, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms));
    state.Require(pane != nullptr && IsWindow(pane) != FALSE, L"File operations issues pane did not open for diff-refresh validation.");
    if (! pane || IsWindow(pane) == FALSE)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const uint64_t baseTaskId = 0xF000000000000000ull + static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64() & 0x0000FFFFFFFFFFFFull);
    const uint64_t taskA      = baseTaskId + 1u;
    const uint64_t taskB      = baseTaskId + 2u;
    fileOps->DebugClearDiagnosticsForSelfTest();
    const auto cleanupDiagnostics = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept
    {
        fileOps->DebugClearDiagnosticsForSelfTest();
        static_cast<void>(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true));
    });

    const auto waitForSnapshot = [&](auto&& predicate, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout, FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& outSnapshot) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot))
            {
                return true;
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        return FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot);
    };

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot initialSnapshot{};
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, initialSnapshot), L"Failed to capture initial issues-pane snapshot.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const uint64_t initialGeneration = initialSnapshot.refreshGeneration;

    fileOps->RecordTaskDiagnostic(taskA,
                                  FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                  FolderWindow::FileOperationState::DiagnosticSeverity::Warning,
                                  HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED),
                                  L"selftest.dxui.issues",
                                  std::format(L"selection-preserve-a-{}", taskA),
                                  L"C:\\selftest-a.txt",
                                  L"D:\\selftest-a.txt");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, false), L"Failed to request first issues-pane refresh.");

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot afterA{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& snapshot) noexcept
    { return snapshot.refreshGeneration > initialGeneration; },
                                  SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                                  afterA),
                  L"Issues pane did not observe the first diagnostic-driven refresh.");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSelectTask(pane, taskA), L"Failed to select the first injected issues-pane task.");

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot selectedA{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& snapshot) noexcept
    { return snapshot.selectionCount == 1u && snapshot.primarySelectedTaskId == taskA; },
                                  SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                                  selectedA),
                  L"Issues pane did not report the expected selection after selecting the first injected task.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot afterNoOp{};
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot noOpBaseline = selectedA;
    bool noOpRefreshSettledWithoutApplyingRows              = false;
    for (uint32_t attempt = 0; attempt < 5u && state.failure.empty(); ++attempt)
    {
        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, false), L"Failed to request no-op issues-pane refresh.");
        state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, afterNoOp), L"Failed to capture issues-pane snapshot after no-op refresh.");
        state.Require(afterNoOp.selectionCount == 1u && afterNoOp.primarySelectedTaskId == taskA,
                      std::format(L"No-op issues-pane refresh should preserve the selected task on attempt {}.", attempt + 1u));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (afterNoOp.refreshGeneration == noOpBaseline.refreshGeneration)
        {
            noOpRefreshSettledWithoutApplyingRows = true;
            break;
        }

        noOpBaseline = afterNoOp;
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }

    state.Require(noOpRefreshSettledWithoutApplyingRows,
                  std::format(L"No-op issues-pane refresh should not advance the applied refresh generation once rows are stable; "
                              L"selectedGeneration={} finalGeneration={}.",
                              selectedA.refreshGeneration,
                              afterNoOp.refreshGeneration));

    fileOps->RecordTaskDiagnostic(taskB,
                                  FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                  FolderWindow::FileOperationState::DiagnosticSeverity::Error,
                                  HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION),
                                  L"selftest.dxui.issues",
                                  std::format(L"selection-preserve-b-{}", taskB),
                                  L"C:\\selftest-b.txt",
                                  L"D:\\selftest-b.txt");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, false), L"Failed to request second issues-pane refresh.");

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot afterB{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& snapshot) noexcept
    { return snapshot.refreshGeneration > afterNoOp.refreshGeneration && snapshot.selectionCount == 1u && snapshot.primarySelectedTaskId == taskA; },
                                  SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                                  afterB),
                  L"Changed issues-pane refresh did not preserve the first selected task.");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSelectTask(pane, taskB), L"Failed to select the second injected issues-pane task.");

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot selectedB{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& snapshot) noexcept
    { return snapshot.selectionCount == 1u && snapshot.primarySelectedTaskId == taskB; },
                                  SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                                  selectedB),
                  L"Issues pane did not expose the second injected task after diff refresh.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneHeaderClickSortsResults(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    if (! mainWindow || IsWindow(mainWindow) == FALSE)
    {
        state.Require(false, L"Main window handle invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable for issues-pane header-sort validation.");
    if (! fileOps)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto getVisibleIssuesPane = [fileOps]() noexcept -> HWND
    {
        const HWND pane = fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest();
        // This legacy fixture exercises technical-column interactions explicitly.
        if (pane)
            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane, true);
        return (pane && IsActuallyVisibleChildWindow(pane)) ? pane : nullptr;
    };

    const bool wasVisible       = g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible();
    const auto setIssuesVisible = [&](bool visible, std::wstring_view label) noexcept -> bool
    {
        if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
        {
            return true;
        }

        SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILEOPS_FAILED_ITEMS, 0), 0);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
            {
                return true;
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        state.Require(false, std::format(L"File operations issues pane did not switch to {} during {}.", visible ? L"visible" : L"hidden", label));
        return false;
    };
    const auto restoreVisibility = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept { static_cast<void>(setIssuesVisible(wasVisible, L"restore")); });
    state.Require(setIssuesVisible(false, L"issues-pane header-sort baseline reset"),
                  L"Failed to hide the file-operations issues pane before header-sort validation.");
    const auto restoreViewState = ResetFileOperationsIssuesPaneViewStateForTest(fileOps);

    state.Require(setIssuesVisible(true, L"issues-pane header-sort validation"), L"Failed to show the file-operations issues pane.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const HWND pane = WaitForWindow(getVisibleIssuesPane, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms));
    state.Require(pane != nullptr && IsWindow(pane) != FALSE, L"File operations issues pane did not open for header-sort validation.");
    if (! pane || IsWindow(pane) == FALSE)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const uint64_t baseTaskId     = 0xF100000000000000ull + static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64() & 0x0000FFFFFFFFFFFFull);
    const uint64_t taskHigh       = baseTaskId + 9u;
    const auto cleanupDiagnostics = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept
    {
        fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(taskHigh);
        static_cast<void>(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true));
    });

    const auto waitForSnapshot = [&](auto&& predicate, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout, FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& outSnapshot) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot))
            {
                return true;
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        return FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot);
    };

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot baseline{};
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, baseline), L"Failed to capture baseline issues-pane snapshot.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const uint64_t baselineGeneration = baseline.refreshGeneration;
    fileOps->RecordTaskDiagnostic(taskHigh,
                                  FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                  FolderWindow::FileOperationState::DiagnosticSeverity::Warning,
                                  HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_DISK_FULL),
                                  L"selftest.dxui.issues.sort",
                                  L"zzzz-selftest-issues-sort-high",
                                  L"C:\\selftest-issues-sort-high.txt",
                                  L"D:\\selftest-issues-sort-high.txt");
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true), L"Failed to request issues-pane refresh for header-sort validation.");

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot snapshot{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot(
                      [&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
    {
        return value.refreshGeneration > baselineGeneration && value.rowCount > 0u && value.visibleWork.visibleRowCount > 0u &&
               value.visibleWork.visibleColumnCount > 0u && value.taskHeaderRect.right > value.taskHeaderRect.left;
    },
                      SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                      snapshot),
                  L"Issues pane did not expose visible rows and the task header for header-sort validation.");
    state.Require(snapshot.visibleWork.visibleColumnCount > 0u, L"Issues pane should expose visible DX columns for header-sort validation.");
    state.Require(snapshot.dxResizeFailureCount == 0u,
                  std::format(L"Issues pane should not hit DX resize failures during header-sort validation; saw {}.", snapshot.dxResizeFailureCount));

    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSelectTask(pane, taskHigh),
                  std::format(L"Failed to select task {} before issues-pane header-sort validation.", taskHigh));
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot([&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
    { return value.selectionCount == 1u && value.primarySelectedTaskId == taskHigh; },
                                  SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                                  snapshot),
                  std::format(L"Issues pane did not expose task {} as selected before header-sort validation.", taskHigh));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto taskHeaderClickPoint = [&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept -> LPARAM
    {
        return DipPointToClientLParam(
            pane, (value.taskHeaderRect.left + value.taskHeaderRect.right) * 0.5f, (value.taskHeaderRect.top + value.taskHeaderRect.bottom) * 0.5f);
    };

    const auto hitTestTaskHeaderCenter = [&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value,
                                             FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestGridHit& hit) noexcept -> bool
    {
        return FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestHitTestGridPoint(pane,
                                                                  (value.taskHeaderRect.left + value.taskHeaderRect.right) * 0.5f,
                                                                  (value.taskHeaderRect.top + value.taskHeaderRect.bottom) * 0.5f,
                                                                  hit) &&
               static_cast<unsigned>(hit.zone) == 1u && hit.columnIndex == 1u && ! hit.isHeaderResize;
    };

    const LPARAM clickPoint = taskHeaderClickPoint(snapshot);
    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestGridHit preClickHit{};
    state.Require(hitTestTaskHeaderCenter(snapshot, preClickHit), L"Failed to hit-test the issues-pane Task header before header-sort validation.");
    state.Require(static_cast<unsigned>(preClickHit.zone) == 1u && preClickHit.columnIndex == 1u && ! preClickHit.isHeaderResize,
                  std::format(L"Issues-pane Task header center did not resolve to a sortable header hit before header-sort validation. "
                              L"zone={}, column={}, resize={}, rect=({}, {}, {}, {})",
                              static_cast<unsigned>(preClickHit.zone),
                              preClickHit.columnIndex,
                              preClickHit.isHeaderResize,
                              preClickHit.rectDip.left,
                              preClickHit.rectDip.top,
                              preClickHit.rectDip.right,
                              preClickHit.rectDip.bottom));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }
    SendMouseClickToResolvedPointWindow(pane, clickPoint);

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot immediateAfterClick{};
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, immediateAfterClick),
                  L"Failed to capture issues-pane snapshot immediately after the first header click.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot ascending{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot(
                      [&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
    {
        return value.hasActiveSort && value.sortColumnIndex == 1u && ! value.sortDescending && value.selectionCount == 1u &&
               value.primarySelectedTaskId == taskHigh && value.visibleWork.visibleRowCount > 0u && value.visibleWork.visibleColumnCount > 0u &&
               value.taskHeaderRect.right > value.taskHeaderRect.left && value.dxResizeFailureCount == 0u;
    },
                      SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                      ascending),
                  std::format(L"Issues pane did not switch to ascending task sort after the first header click. "
                              L"immediateSortActive={}, immediateColumn={}, immediateDescending={}, immediateRowCount={}, immediateFirstTask={}, "
                              L"immediateSelectedTask={}, sortActive={}, column={}, descending={}, firstTask={}, selectedTask={}",
                              immediateAfterClick.hasActiveSort,
                              immediateAfterClick.sortColumnIndex,
                              immediateAfterClick.sortDescending,
                              immediateAfterClick.rowCount,
                              immediateAfterClick.firstVisibleTaskId,
                              immediateAfterClick.primarySelectedTaskId,
                              ascending.hasActiveSort,
                              ascending.sortColumnIndex,
                              ascending.sortDescending,
                              ascending.firstVisibleTaskId,
                              ascending.primarySelectedTaskId));

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestGridHit secondPreClickHit{};
    state.Require(hitTestTaskHeaderCenter(ascending, secondPreClickHit), L"Failed to hit-test the issues-pane Task header before the second sort click.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }
    SendMouseClickToResolvedPointWindow(pane, taskHeaderClickPoint(ascending));

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot immediateAfterSecondClick{};
    state.Require(FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, immediateAfterSecondClick),
                  L"Failed to capture issues-pane snapshot immediately after the second header click.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot descending{};
    state.Require(waitForSnapshot(
                      [&](const FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& value) noexcept
    {
        return value.hasActiveSort && value.sortColumnIndex == 1u && value.sortDescending && value.firstVisibleTaskId == taskHigh &&
               value.selectionCount == 1u && value.primarySelectedTaskId == taskHigh && value.visibleWork.visibleRowCount > 0u &&
               value.visibleWork.visibleColumnCount > 0u && value.dxResizeFailureCount == 0u;
    },
                      SelfTest::Scale(3000ms),
                      descending),
                  std::format(L"Issues pane did not switch to descending task sort after the second header click. "
                              L"immediateSortActive={}, immediateColumn={}, immediateDescending={}, immediateFirstTask={}, immediateSelectedTask={}, "
                              L"sortActive={}, column={}, descending={}, firstTask={}, selectedTask={}",
                              immediateAfterSecondClick.hasActiveSort,
                              immediateAfterSecondClick.sortColumnIndex,
                              immediateAfterSecondClick.sortDescending,
                              immediateAfterSecondClick.firstVisibleTaskId,
                              immediateAfterSecondClick.primarySelectedTaskId,
                              descending.hasActiveSort,
                              descending.sortColumnIndex,
                              descending.sortDescending,
                              descending.firstVisibleTaskId,
                              descending.primarySelectedTaskId));
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsIssuesPaneHeaderResizeChangesVisibleWidth(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    if (! mainWindow || IsWindow(mainWindow) == FALSE)
    {
        state.Require(false, L"Main window handle invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable for issues-pane header-resize validation.");
    if (! fileOps)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto getVisibleIssuesPane = [fileOps]() noexcept -> HWND
    {
        const HWND pane = fileOps->GetIssuesPaneHwndForSelfTest();
        // This legacy fixture exercises technical-column interactions explicitly.
        if (pane)
            FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSetTechnicalExpanded(pane, true);
        return (pane && IsActuallyVisibleChildWindow(pane)) ? pane : nullptr;
    };

    const bool wasVisible       = g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible();
    const auto setIssuesVisible = [&](bool visible, std::wstring_view label) noexcept -> bool
    {
        if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
        {
            return true;
        }

        SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILEOPS_FAILED_ITEMS, 0), 0);
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible() == visible)
            {
                return true;
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        state.Require(false, std::format(L"File operations issues pane did not switch to {} during {}.", visible ? L"visible" : L"hidden", label));
        return false;
    };
    const auto restoreVisibility = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept { static_cast<void>(setIssuesVisible(wasVisible, L"restore")); });
    state.Require(setIssuesVisible(false, L"issues-pane header-resize baseline reset"),
                  L"Failed to hide the file-operations issues pane before header-resize validation.");
    const auto restoreViewState = ResetFileOperationsIssuesPaneViewStateForTest(fileOps);

    state.Require(setIssuesVisible(true, L"issues-pane header-resize validation"), L"Failed to show the file-operations issues pane.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const HWND pane = WaitForWindow(getVisibleIssuesPane, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms));
    state.Require(pane != nullptr && IsWindow(pane) != FALSE, L"File operations issues pane did not open for header-resize validation.");
    if (! pane || IsWindow(pane) == FALSE)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const uint64_t taskId         = 0xF300000000000000ull + static_cast<uint64_t>(GetTickCount64() & 0x0000FFFFFFFFFFFFull);
    const auto cleanupDiagnostics = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept
    {
        fileOps->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(taskId);
        static_cast<void>(FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestRefresh(pane, true));
    });

    const auto waitForSnapshot = [&](auto&& predicate, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout, FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot& outSnapshot) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot))
            {
                return true;
            }

            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }

        return FileOperationsIssuesPane::TryGetSelfTestSnapshot(pane, outSnapshot) && predicate(outSnapshot);
    };

    FileOperationsIssuesPane::SelfTestSnapshot baseline{};
