                      saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateCompletedItems == static_cast<uint64_t>((std::numeric_limits<unsigned long>::max)()) + 1u &&
                      saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateTotalItems == static_cast<uint64_t>((std::numeric_limits<unsigned long>::max)()) + 1u,
                  L"Global byte aggregates should saturate at UINT64_MAX while item aggregates retain their widened sum.");
    state.Require(saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateCompletedBytes <= saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateTotalBytes &&
                      saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateCompletedItems <= saturatedAggregate.footerAggregateTotalItems,
                  L"Saturated global aggregate progress should preserve completed <= total.");

    const D2D1_RECT_F indeterminateBar = D2D1::RectF(0.0f, 0.0f, 100.0f, 6.0f);
    const D2D1_RECT_F reducedAtStart   = DebugComputeFileOperationsIndeterminateBarFill(indeterminateBar, 0ull, true);
    const D2D1_RECT_F reducedLater     = DebugComputeFileOperationsIndeterminateBarFill(indeterminateBar, 600ull, true);
    const D2D1_RECT_F animatedAtStart  = DebugComputeFileOperationsIndeterminateBarFill(indeterminateBar, 0ull, false);
    const D2D1_RECT_F animatedLater    = DebugComputeFileOperationsIndeterminateBarFill(indeterminateBar, 600ull, false);
    state.Require(reducedAtStart.left == reducedLater.left && reducedAtStart.right == reducedLater.right,
                  L"Reduced-motion indeterminate progress must remain stationary across timer ticks.");
    state.Require(animatedAtStart.left != animatedLater.left || animatedAtStart.right != animatedLater.right,
                  L"Normal-motion indeterminate progress should advance across timer ticks.");

    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsRemovalFocusUsesExactOutcomesAndOwnershipEpochs(CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    const std::wstring failure = FolderView::DebugValidateRemovalFocusContractsForSelfTest();
    state.Require(failure.empty(), failure.empty() ? L"" : std::format(L"Removal-focus contract validation failed: {}.", failure));
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsMoveRemovalFocusSelectsNextSurvivor(CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    const std::filesystem::path suiteRoot = SelfTest::GetTempRoot(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands);
    state.Require(! suiteRoot.empty(), L"SelfTest temp root unavailable for move-focus test.");
    if (suiteRoot.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path root      = suiteRoot / L"work" / (L"move_focus_" + NewGuidText());
    const std::filesystem::path sourceDir = root / L"src";
    const std::filesystem::path destDir   = root / L"dst";
    std::error_code ec;
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root, ec);
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(sourceDir / L"a_folder"), L"Failed to create a_folder for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(sourceDir / L"b_folder"), L"Failed to create b_folder for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(sourceDir / L"c_folder"), L"Failed to create c_folder for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(sourceDir / L"d.txt", "d"), L"Failed to create d.txt for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(sourceDir / L"e.txt", "e"), L"Failed to create e.txt for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(destDir), L"Failed to create destination folder for move-focus test.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    auto* fileOps                                          = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    const std::wstring leftPluginBefore                    = std::wstring(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Left));
    const std::wstring rightPluginBefore                   = std::wstring(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Right));
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> leftBefore  = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> rightBefore = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    const FolderView::SortBy leftSortBefore                = g_folderWindow.GetSortBy(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const FolderView::SortDirection leftDirectionBefore    = g_folderWindow.GetSortDirection(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const auto restore                                     = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        if (fileOps)
        {
            static_cast<void>(CloseFileOperationsPopupForSelfTest(fileOps));
        }
        static_cast<void>(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftPluginBefore));
        static_cast<void>(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightPluginBefore));
        g_folderWindow.SetSort(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftSortBefore, leftDirectionBefore);
        if (leftBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftBefore.value());
        }
        if (rightBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightBefore.value());
        }
        std::error_code cleanupError;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(root, cleanupError);
    });

    g_folderWindow.DebugResetPaneVisibilityState(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    g_folderWindow.SetActivePane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, L"builtin/file-system")),
                  L"Failed to set left filesystem for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, L"builtin/file-system")),
                  L"Failed to set right filesystem for move-focus test.");
    g_folderWindow.SetSort(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, FolderView::SortBy::Name, FolderView::SortDirection::Ascending);

    std::atomic<uint32_t> enumerationCount{0u};
    g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationCompletedCallback(FolderWindow::Pane::Left,
                                                       [&](const std::filesystem::path& folder) noexcept
    {
        if (OrdinalString::EqualsNoCasePath(folder, sourceDir))
        {
            enumerationCount.fetch_add(1u, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    });
    const auto clearEnumerationCallback = wil::scope_exit([&] { g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationCompletedCallback(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, {}); });

    std::atomic<uint32_t> completedMoves{0u};
    std::atomic<HRESULT> lastMoveResult{E_PENDING};
    const uint64_t completionToken      = g_folderWindow.AddFileOperationCompletedCallback([&](const FolderWindow::FileOperationCompletedEvent& event) noexcept
    {
        if (event.operation != FILESYSTEM_MOVE || event.sourcePaths.empty() ||
            ! OrdinalString::EqualsNoCasePath(event.sourcePaths.front().parent_path(), sourceDir))
        {
            return;
        }
        lastMoveResult.store(event.hr, std::memory_order_release);
        completedMoves.fetch_add(1u, std::memory_order_release);
    });
    const auto removeCompletionCallback = wil::scope_exit([&] noexcept { g_folderWindow.RemoveFileOperationCompletedCallback(completionToken); });

    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir);
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Failed to set source pane path for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Failed to set destination pane path for move-focus test.");
    state.Require(WaitForAtomicAtLeast(enumerationCount, 1u, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Initial move-focus enumeration did not complete.");
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, {L"a_folder", L"b_folder", L"c_folder", L"d.txt", L"e.txt"}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Move-focus fixture items did not load.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    state.Require(g_folderWindow.DebugFocusItemByDisplayName(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, L"b_folder"),
                  L"Failed to focus the middle folder before move-focus test.");
    FocusFolderViewPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    g_folderWindow.CommandMoveToOtherPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);

    const auto deadline      = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(8000ms);
    bool focusedNextSurvivor = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::error_code existsError;
        const bool removed = ! std::filesystem::exists(sourceDir / L"b_folder", existsError) && ! existsError;
        if (completedMoves.load(std::memory_order_acquire) >= 1u && SUCCEEDED(lastMoveResult.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) && removed &&
            g_folderWindow.DebugGetItemCount(FolderWindow::Pane::Left) == 4u &&
            g_folderWindow.DebugGetFocusedItemDisplayName(FolderWindow::Pane::Left) == L"c_folder" &&
            g_folderWindow.DebugGetSelectedCount(FolderWindow::Pane::Left) == 0u)
        {
            focusedNextSurvivor = true;
            break;
        }
        Sleep(10u);
    }
    state.Require(
        focusedNextSurvivor,
        std::format(L"Moving the middle folder should focus the next source survivor; completions={}, hr=0x{:08X}, items={}, focus='{}', selected={}.",
                    completedMoves.load(std::memory_order_acquire),
                    static_cast<unsigned long>(lastMoveResult.load(std::memory_order_acquire)),
                    g_folderWindow.DebugGetItemCount(FolderWindow::Pane::Left),
                    g_folderWindow.DebugGetFocusedItemDisplayName(FolderWindow::Pane::Left),
                    g_folderWindow.DebugGetSelectedCount(FolderWindow::Pane::Left)));
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsRoutineAndWithOptionsPaneCommands(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;

    state.Require(mainWindow != nullptr && IsWindow(mainWindow) != FALSE, L"Main window handle invalid for routine/options pane-transfer validation.");
    const std::filesystem::path suiteRoot = SelfTest::GetTempRoot(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands);
    state.Require(! suiteRoot.empty(), L"SelfTest temp root unavailable for routine/options pane-transfer validation.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path root              = suiteRoot / L"work" / (L"pane_transfer_options_" + NewGuidText());
    const std::filesystem::path sourceDir         = root / L"src";
    const std::filesystem::path destDir           = root / L"dst";
    constexpr std::wstring_view kArtifactCopyName = L"copy.rs_ren_0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
    constexpr std::wstring_view kArtifactMoveName = L"move.rs_ren_fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210";
    const std::array<std::wstring_view, 6> sourceNames{
        L"options-copy.txt",
        L"options-move.txt",
        L"routine-copy.txt",
        L"routine-move.txt",
        kArtifactCopyName,
        kArtifactMoveName,
    };

    std::error_code cleanupError;
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root, cleanupError);
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(sourceDir) && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(destDir), L"Failed to create routine/options pane-transfer directories.");
    for (const std::wstring_view name : sourceNames)
    {
        state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(sourceDir / name, "pane-transfer-options"),
                      std::format(L"Failed to create routine/options pane-transfer fixture '{}'.", name));
    }
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto cleanupRoot                                     = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept
    {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(root, ec);
    });
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> leftPathBefore  = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> rightPathBefore = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    const auto restorePanes                                    = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept
    {
        if (leftPathBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftPathBefore.value());
        }
        if (rightPathBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightPathBefore.value());
        }
        PumpPendingMessages();
    });
    const auto clearPromptOverride                             = wil::scope_exit([]() noexcept
    {
        HostClearTestPromptResultOverride();
        HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
    });
    std::atomic<uint32_t> routineCompletionMask{0u};
    const uint64_t completionToken      = g_folderWindow.AddFileOperationCompletedCallback([&](const FolderWindow::FileOperationCompletedEvent& event) noexcept
    {
        if (FAILED(event.hr) || event.sourcePaths.size() != 1u || ! OrdinalString::EqualsNoCasePath(event.sourcePaths.front().parent_path(), sourceDir))
        {
            return;
        }

        if (event.operation == FILESYSTEM_COPY && event.sourcePaths.front().filename() == L"routine-copy.txt")
        {
            routineCompletionMask.fetch_or(1u, std::memory_order_release);
        }
        else if (event.operation == FILESYSTEM_MOVE && event.sourcePaths.front().filename() == L"routine-move.txt")
        {
            routineCompletionMask.fetch_or(2u, std::memory_order_release);
        }
        else if (event.operation == FILESYSTEM_COPY && event.sourcePaths.front().filename() == kArtifactCopyName)
        {
            routineCompletionMask.fetch_or(4u, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    });
    const auto removeCompletionCallback = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { g_folderWindow.RemoveFileOperationCompletedCallback(completionToken); });

    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir);
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Failed to set the source pane for routine/options validation.");
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Failed to set the destination pane for routine/options validation.");
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(FolderWindow::Pane::Left,
                                   {L"options-copy.txt", L"options-move.txt", L"routine-copy.txt", L"routine-move.txt", kArtifactCopyName, kArtifactMoveName},
                                   SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Routine/options pane-transfer fixtures did not load.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto waitForPromptCount = [](uint64_t expected, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() >= expected)
            {
                return true;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        PumpPendingMessages();
        return HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() >= expected;
    };
    const auto waitForPathState = [](const std::filesystem::path& path, bool expected, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            std::error_code ec;
            if (std::filesystem::exists(path, ec) == expected && ! ec)
            {
                return true;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        std::error_code ec;
        return std::filesystem::exists(path, ec) == expected && ! ec;
    };
    const auto waitForCompletionMask = [&](uint32_t expected, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout) noexcept
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if ((routineCompletionMask.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & expected) == expected)
            {
                return true;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        PumpPendingMessages();
        return (routineCompletionMask.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & expected) == expected;
    };
    const auto invokeForFocusedItem = [&](std::wstring_view name, UINT commandId) noexcept
    {
        state.Require(g_folderWindow.DebugFocusItemByDisplayName(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, name),
                      std::format(L"Failed to focus '{}' before command {}.", name, commandId));
        FocusFolderViewPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
        SendMessageW(mainWindow, WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(commandId, 0), 0);
    };

    HostSetTestPromptResultOverride(HOST_PROMPT_RESULT_CANCEL);
    for (const auto& [name, commandId] : std::array<std::pair<std::wstring_view, UINT>, 2>{
             std::pair{std::wstring_view{L"options-copy.txt"}, static_cast<UINT>(IDM_PANE_COPY_TO_OTHER_WITH_OPTIONS)},
             std::pair{std::wstring_view{L"options-move.txt"}, static_cast<UINT>(IDM_PANE_MOVE_TO_OTHER_WITH_OPTIONS)},
         })
    {
        HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
        invokeForFocusedItem(name, commandId);
        state.Require(waitForPromptCount(1u, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)),
                      std::format(L"With-options command {} did not publish its pre-consumption prompt.", commandId));
        HostPromptDebugSnapshot prompt{};
        state.Require(HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() == 1u && HostGetTestLastPromptDebugSnapshot(prompt),
                      std::format(L"With-options command {} should publish exactly one captured prompt.", commandId));
        state.Require(prompt.hasFileOperationOptions,
                      std::format(L"With-options command {} did not use the shared File Operations options surface.", commandId));
        state.Require(waitForPathState(sourceDir / name, true, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)) && waitForPathState(destDir / name, false, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)),
                      std::format(L"Cancel from with-options command {} changed source or destination state.", commandId));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
    HostClearTestPromptResultOverride();

    HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
    invokeForFocusedItem(L"routine-copy.txt", IDM_PANE_COPY_TO_OTHER);
    state.Require(waitForPathState(destDir / L"routine-copy.txt", true, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)), L"Routine F5-path copy did not reach the destination.");
    state.Require(waitForCompletionMask(1u, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)), L"Routine F5-path copy did not publish a successful terminal completion.");
    state.Require(waitForPathState(sourceDir / L"routine-copy.txt", true, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)), L"Routine F5-path copy removed its source.");
    state.Require(HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() == 0u, std::format(L"Routine copy showed {} generic prompt(s).", HostGetTestPromptRequestCount()));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
    invokeForFocusedItem(L"routine-move.txt", IDM_PANE_MOVE_TO_OTHER);
    state.Require(waitForPathState(sourceDir / L"routine-move.txt", false, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)) &&
                      waitForPathState(destDir / L"routine-move.txt", true, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)),
                  L"Routine F6-path move did not preserve exact source/destination semantics.");
    state.Require(waitForCompletionMask(3u, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)), L"Routine F6-path move did not publish a successful terminal completion.");
    state.Require(HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() == 0u, std::format(L"Routine move showed {} generic prompt(s).", HostGetTestPromptRequestCount()));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
    invokeForFocusedItem(kArtifactCopyName, IDM_PANE_COPY_TO_OTHER);
    state.Require(waitForPathState(destDir / kArtifactCopyName, true, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)) &&
                      waitForPathState(sourceDir / kArtifactCopyName, true, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)),
                  L"Routine Copy of a name-only Possible artifact did not preserve source and publish destination.");
    state.Require(waitForCompletionMask(7u, SelfTest::Scale(5000ms)),
                  L"Routine Copy of a name-only Possible artifact did not publish a successful terminal completion.");
    state.Require(HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() == 0u,
                  std::format(L"Routine Copy of a name-only Possible artifact showed {} prompt(s).", HostGetTestPromptRequestCount()));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    HostResetTestPromptRequestCount();
    HostSetTestPromptResultOverride(HOST_PROMPT_RESULT_CANCEL);
    invokeForFocusedItem(kArtifactMoveName, IDM_PANE_MOVE_TO_OTHER);
    HostClearTestPromptResultOverride();
    HostPromptDebugSnapshot artifactPrompt{};
    state.Require(HostGetTestPromptRequestCount() == 1u && HostGetTestLastPromptDebugSnapshot(artifactPrompt) &&
                      artifactPrompt.presentation == HOST_PROMPT_PRESENTATION_ARTIFACT_TOUCH && artifactPrompt.defaultResult == HOST_PROMPT_RESULT_CANCEL,
                  L"Routine Move of a Possible artifact should show exactly one Cancel-default artifact warning.");
    state.Require(waitForPathState(sourceDir / kArtifactMoveName, true, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)) &&
                      waitForPathState(destDir / kArtifactMoveName, false, SelfTest::Scale(500ms)),
                  L"Canceling the Possible-artifact Move warning changed source or destination state.");

    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsCompletedPostFailureStillAppliesUiContract(HWND /*mainWindow*/, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    auto* fileOps = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(fileOps != nullptr, L"File-operations state unavailable for PostCompleted fallback validation.");
    if (! fileOps)
    {
        return false;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path suiteRoot = SelfTest::GetTempRoot(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands);
    const std::filesystem::path root      = suiteRoot / L"work" / (L"fo_post_fail_" + NewGuidText());
    const std::filesystem::path sourceDir = root / L"src";
    const std::filesystem::path destDir   = root / L"dst";
    const std::array<std::filesystem::path, 2> sources{sourceDir / L"payload-reaper.txt", sourceDir / L"payload-submit-fail.txt"};
    std::error_code ec;
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root, ec);
    state.Require(SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(sourceDir) && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(destDir) && SelfTest::WriteTextFile(sources[0], "post-fail-reaper") &&
                      SelfTest::WriteTextFile(sources[1], "post-fail-submit"),
                  L"Failed to seed PostCompleted fallback folders.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const std::wstring leftPluginBefore                    = std::wstring(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Left));
    const std::wstring rightPluginBefore                   = std::wstring(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Right));
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> leftBefore  = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const std::optional<std::filesystem::path> rightBefore = g_folderWindow.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    const auto restore                                     = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        static_cast<void>(CloseFileOperationsPopupForSelfTest(fileOps));
        static_cast<void>(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftPluginBefore));
        static_cast<void>(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightPluginBefore));
        if (leftBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftBefore.value());
        }
        if (rightBefore.has_value())
        {
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightBefore.value());
        }
        std::error_code cleanupEc;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(root, cleanupEc);
    });

    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, L"builtin/file-system")),
                  L"Failed to set left filesystem for PostCompleted fallback.");
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, L"builtin/file-system")),
                  L"Failed to set right filesystem for PostCompleted fallback.");
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir);
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, sourceDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Failed to navigate left pane for PostCompleted fallback.");
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Failed to navigate right pane for PostCompleted fallback.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto leftFs  = g_folderWindow.GetFileSystem(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const auto rightFs = g_folderWindow.GetFileSystem(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    state.Require(leftFs && rightFs, L"Failed to resolve filesystems for PostCompleted fallback.");
    if (! leftFs || ! rightFs)
    {
        return false;
    }

    std::array<std::atomic<uint64_t>, 2> completedTaskIds{};
    const uint64_t token      = g_folderWindow.AddFileOperationCompletedCallback([&](const FolderWindow::FileOperationCompletedEvent& event) noexcept
    {
        if (event.operation != FILESYSTEM_COPY || event.sourcePaths.size() != 1u)
        {
            return;
        }
        for (size_t sourceIndex = 0u; sourceIndex < sources.size(); ++sourceIndex)
        {
            if (OrdinalString::EqualsNoCasePath(event.sourcePaths.front(), sources[sourceIndex]))
            {
                completedTaskIds[sourceIndex].store(event.taskId, std::memory_order_release);
                return;
            }
        }
    });
    const auto removeCallback = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { g_folderWindow.RemoveFileOperationCompletedCallback(token); });

    state.Require(CloseFileOperationsPopupForSelfTest(fileOps), L"Could not clear file-operations tasks before PostCompleted fallback.");
    const DWORD uiTid = GetCurrentThreadId();
    for (size_t sourceIndex = 0u; sourceIndex < sources.size(); ++sourceIndex)
    {
        fileOps->DebugForceNextFileOperationCompletedPostFailure();
        if (sourceIndex == 1u)
        {
            fileOps->DebugForceNextOrphanedCompletionDrainSubmissionFailure();
        }
        uint64_t startedTaskId = 0;
        const HRESULT startHr  = fileOps->AdmitOperation(FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                                         FolderWindow::Pane::Left,
                                                         FolderWindow::Pane::Right,
                                                         leftFs,
                                                         {sources[sourceIndex]},
                                                         destDir,
                                                         FILESYSTEM_FLAG_NONE,
                                                         false,
                                                         0,
                                                         FolderWindow::FileOperationState::ExecutionMode::PerItem,
                                                         false,
                                                         nullptr,
                                                         &startedTaskId);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(startHr) && startedTaskId != 0u,
                      std::format(L"Failed to start PostCompleted fallback copy {} (hr=0x{:08X}).", sourceIndex, static_cast<unsigned long>(startHr)));
        if (! state.failure.empty())
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (sourceIndex == 1u)
        {
            // Do not pump the UI completion yet. Admission failure must first put
            // ownership of the still-running jthread back on Task, so RemoveTask's
            // destruction path necessarily joins before releasing Task storage.
            const auto restorationDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(8000ms);
            while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < restorationDeadline && fileOps->DebugLastRestoredCompletionWorkerTaskId() != startedTaskId)
            {
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(1ms);
            }
            state.Require(fileOps->DebugLastRestoredCompletionWorkerTaskId() == startedTaskId,
                          L"Forced drain-submission failure did not restore the completion worker to Task.");
            std::vector<FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task*> liveBeforeCompletion;
            fileOps->CollectTasks(liveBeforeCompletion);
            const bool taskOwnsWorker = std::ranges::any_of(liveBeforeCompletion, [startedTaskId](const FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task* task) noexcept {
                return task && task->GetId() == startedTaskId && task->_thread.joinable();
            });
            state.Require(taskOwnsWorker, L"Drain-submission failure must restore the joinable worker handle before posting the UI wakeup.");
        }

        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(8000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline && completedTaskIds[sourceIndex].load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0u)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }
        state.Require(completedTaskIds[sourceIndex].load(std::memory_order_acquire) == startedTaskId,
                      sourceIndex == 0u ? L"Forced dual-post failure must still invoke UI completion callbacks."
                                        : L"A forced drain-submission failure must retry the queued UI wakeup.");

        const DWORD workerTid = fileOps->DebugLastFileOperationCompletionWorkerTid();
        const DWORD applyTid  = fileOps->DebugLastFileOperationCompletionApplyTid();
        state.Require(
            workerTid != 0u && applyTid == uiTid && workerTid != applyTid,
            std::format(L"Completion fallback must apply on the owning UI thread (workerTid={}, applyTid={}, uiTid={}).", workerTid, applyTid, uiTid));

        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::vector<FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task*> liveTasks;
        fileOps->CollectTasks(liveTasks);
        const bool taskStillLive = std::ranges::any_of(
            liveTasks, [startedTaskId](const FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task* task) noexcept { return task && task->GetId() == startedTaskId; });
        state.Require(! taskStillLive, L"Completion fallback must still RemoveTask on the UI thread.");
    }
    return state.failure.empty();
}

// Fixture-only serialization: native clipboard persistence needs owned bytes,
// unlike the product's Unicode-only publication helper. Never log these contents.
[[nodiscard]] HRESULT ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(const STGMEDIUM& medium, std::vector<std::byte>& bytes) noexcept
{
    constexpr uint64_t maximumBytes = 64u * 1024u * 1024u;
    if (medium.tymed == TYMED_GDI && medium.hBitmap)
    {
        BITMAP bitmap{};
        if (GetObjectW(medium.hBitmap, static_cast<int>(sizeof(bitmap)), &bitmap) != static_cast<int>(sizeof(bitmap)) || bitmap.bmWidth <= 0 ||
            bitmap.bmHeight == 0)
            return E_INVALIDARG;
        const uint64_t width       = static_cast<uint64_t>(bitmap.bmWidth);
        const int64_t signedHeight = bitmap.bmHeight;
        const uint64_t height      = static_cast<uint64_t>(signedHeight < 0 ? -signedHeight : signedHeight);
        const uint64_t pixelBytes  = width * height * sizeof(uint32_t);
        if (pixelBytes > maximumBytes - sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER))
            return E_INVALIDARG;
        BITMAPINFO info{};
        info.bmiHeader.biSize        = sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER);
        info.bmiHeader.biWidth       = bitmap.bmWidth;
        info.bmiHeader.biHeight      = -static_cast<LONG>(height);
        info.bmiHeader.biPlanes      = 1u;
        info.bmiHeader.biBitCount    = 32u;
        info.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB;
        info.bmiHeader.biSizeImage   = static_cast<DWORD>(pixelBytes);
        bytes.resize(sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER) + static_cast<size_t>(pixelBytes));
        wil::unique_hdc_window dc{GetDC(nullptr)};
        if (! dc || GetDIBits(dc.get(), medium.hBitmap, 0u, static_cast<UINT>(height), bytes.data() + sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER), &info, DIB_RGB_COLORS) !=
                        static_cast<int>(height))
            return E_FAIL;
        // GetDIBits may rewrite optional header fields. CF_BITMAP has RGB pixels,
        // not alpha semantics; its reserved high bytes are not stable across GDI
        // conversions. Compare a canonical header and the three color channels.
        BITMAPINFOHEADER canonical{};
        canonical.biSize        = sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER);
        canonical.biWidth       = bitmap.bmWidth;
        canonical.biHeight      = -static_cast<LONG>(height);
        canonical.biPlanes      = 1u;
        canonical.biBitCount    = 32u;
        canonical.biCompression = BI_RGB;
        canonical.biSizeImage   = static_cast<DWORD>(pixelBytes);
        std::memcpy(bytes.data(), &canonical, sizeof(canonical));
        for (size_t offset = sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER) + 3u; offset < bytes.size(); offset += 4u)
            bytes[offset] = std::byte{};
        return S_OK;
    }
    if (medium.tymed == TYMED_HGLOBAL)
    {
        const SIZE_T size = GlobalSize(medium.hGlobal);
        if (! medium.hGlobal || size > maximumBytes)
            return E_INVALIDARG;
        bytes.resize(size);
        if (size == 0u)
            return S_OK;
        const void* data = GlobalLock(medium.hGlobal);
        if (! data)
        {
            const DWORD error = GetLastError();
            return error ? HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(error) : E_FAIL;
        }
        const auto unlock = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(GlobalUnlock(medium.hGlobal)); });
        std::memcpy(bytes.data(), data, size);
        return S_OK;
    }
    if (medium.tymed != TYMED_ISTREAM || ! medium.pstm)
        return DV_E_TYMED;
    STATSTG stat{};
    RETURN_IF_FAILED(medium.pstm->Stat(&stat, STATFLAG_NONAME));
    if (stat.cbSize.QuadPart > maximumBytes)
        return E_INVALIDARG;
    // Clone only for an independent cursor; the vector materializes the backing.
    wil::com_ptr<IStream> reader;
    RETURN_IF_FAILED(medium.pstm->Clone(reader.put()));
    RETURN_IF_FAILED(reader->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_SET, nullptr));
    bytes.resize(static_cast<size_t>(stat.cbSize.QuadPart));
    ULONG read = 0u;
    RETURN_IF_FAILED(reader->Read(bytes.data(), static_cast<ULONG>(bytes.size()), &read));
    return read == bytes.size() ? S_OK : STG_E_READFAULT;
}

struct FileOperationsClipboardFormatBytes
{
    FORMATETC format{}; // No target-device pointer: unsupported descriptors fail before mutation.
    std::vector<std::byte> bytes;
};

// OLE readback opens the desktop clipboard even after a successful flush. Other
// readers can briefly hold it. Retry only this read-only lock failure, at most
// three attempts with the canonical bounded quiet wait; never retry a copy or
// publication, and never adopt contents from a different clipboard generation.
template <typename Read> [[nodiscard]] HRESULT RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest(Read&& read) noexcept
{
    const DWORD sequence = GetClipboardSequenceNumber();
    const HWND owner     = GetClipboardOwner();
    for (unsigned int attempt = 0u; attempt < 3u; ++attempt)
    {
        const HRESULT result = read();
        if (result != CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN || attempt == 2u)
            return result;
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.ReadContention", L"read-only; contents omitted", 0u, attempt + 1u, 0u, result);
        if (! WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(nullptr))
            return result;
        if (GetClipboardSequenceNumber() != sequence || GetClipboardOwner() != owner || GetClipboardSequenceNumber() != sequence)
            return HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED);
    }
    return E_UNEXPECTED;
}

[[nodiscard]] HRESULT ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(IDataObject& source, FORMATETC& request, wil::unique_stg_medium& medium) noexcept
{
    return RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept
    {
        wil::unique_stg_medium attempt;
        const HRESULT result = source.GetData(&request, &attempt);
        if (SUCCEEDED(result))
            medium = std::move(attempt);
        return result;
    });
}

[[nodiscard]] bool VerifyFileOperationsClipboardBytes(IDataObject& restored,
                                                      const std::vector<FileOperationsClipboardFormatBytes>& expected,
                                                      CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    for (const auto& value : expected)
    {
        FORMATETC request = value.format;
        wil::unique_stg_medium medium;
        std::vector<std::byte> actual;
        HRESULT read = ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(restored, request, medium);
        if (SUCCEEDED(read))
            read = ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(medium, actual);
        if (! state.Require(
                SUCCEEDED(read) && actual == value.bytes,
                std::format(L"Clipboard readback must preserve format {} exactly: hr=0x{:08X}.", request.cfFormat, static_cast<unsigned long>(read))))
            return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// The product helper publishes Unicode only. This test-only snapshot must retain
// every advertised clipboard format before its directed Ctrl+C phase can mutate it.
[[nodiscard]] bool RetainFileOperationsClipboardFormats(IDataObject& source,
                                                        wil::com_ptr<IDataObject>& retainedClipboard,
                                                        size_t& retainedFormats,
                                                        CaseState& state,
                                                        std::vector<FileOperationsClipboardFormatBytes>* expected = nullptr) noexcept
{
    retainedFormats = 0u;
    if (expected)
        expected->clear();
    uint64_t retainedBytes = 0u;
    wil::com_ptr<IEnumFORMATETC> clipboardFormats;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(SHCreateDataObject(nullptr, 0u, nullptr, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(retainedClipboard.put()))) &&
                      SUCCEEDED(source.EnumFormatEtc(DATADIR_GET, clipboardFormats.put())),
                  L"Create an owned clipboard snapshot before keyboard copy.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    bool clipboardSnapshotComplete = false;
    for (size_t index = 0u; index <= 256u; ++index)
    {
        FORMATETC format{};
        const HRESULT next = clipboardFormats->Next(1u, &format, nullptr);
        wil::unique_cotaskmem_ptr<DVTARGETDEVICE> targetDevice(format.ptd);
        if (next == S_FALSE)
        {
            clipboardSnapshotComplete = true;
            break;
        }
        if (FAILED(next) || index == 256u)
            break;
        if (! state.Require(! format.ptd && format.dwAspect == DVASPECT_CONTENT && format.lindex == -1,
                            L"Directed clipboard tests require persistable unindexed content formats before mutation."))
            return false;
        wil::unique_stg_medium medium;
        const HRESULT read = ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(source, format, medium);
        if (SUCCEEDED(read) && medium.tymed == TYMED_ISTREAM && medium.pstm)
        {
            STATSTG streamStat{};
            ULARGE_INTEGER position{};
            const HRESULT stat = medium.pstm->Stat(&streamStat, STATFLAG_NONAME);
            const HRESULT seek = medium.pstm->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_CUR, &position);
            Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.StreamSnapshot",
                              std::to_wstring(format.cfFormat),
                              0u,
                              streamStat.cbSize.QuadPart,
                              position.QuadPart,
                              FAILED(stat) ? stat : seek);
        }
        std::vector<std::byte> bytes;
        const HRESULT serialized = SUCCEEDED(read) ? ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(medium, bytes) : read;
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.SnapshotFormat", std::to_wstring(format.cfFormat), 0u, medium.tymed, bytes.size(), serialized);
        retainedBytes += bytes.size();
        if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(serialized) && retainedBytes <= 64u * 1024u * 1024u,
                            std::format(L"Materialize clipboard format {} within the 64 MiB fixture bound before mutation: hr=0x{:08X}.",
                                        format.cfFormat,
                                        static_cast<unsigned long>(serialized))))
            return false;
        if (medium.tymed == TYMED_ISTREAM || medium.tymed == TYMED_HGLOBAL)
        {
            // A stream-only Shell data object passes direct GetData yet loses its
            // bytes at OleFlushClipboard/readback. Publish native byte storage.
            // HGLOBAL also needs an independent copy: pUnkForRelease may retain
            // a producer that can still modify the original allocation.
            wil::unique_stg_medium serializedMedium;
            serializedMedium.tymed   = TYMED_HGLOBAL;
            serializedMedium.hGlobal = GlobalAlloc(GMEM_MOVEABLE, bytes.size());
            if (! state.Require(serializedMedium.hGlobal != nullptr, L"Allocate independent clipboard bytes before mutation."))
                return false;
            if (! bytes.empty())
            {
                void* data = GlobalLock(serializedMedium.hGlobal);
                if (! state.Require(data != nullptr, L"Lock independent clipboard bytes before mutation."))
                    return false;
                const auto unlock = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(GlobalUnlock(serializedMedium.hGlobal)); });
                std::memcpy(data, bytes.data(), bytes.size());
            }
            medium = std::move(serializedMedium);
        }
        else if (medium.tymed == TYMED_GDI)
        {
            // Retaining a release owner alone cannot protect against the producer
            // mutating its pixels. Keep an independent bitmap in its native carrier.
            wil::unique_stg_medium copied;
            copied.tymed   = TYMED_GDI;
            copied.hBitmap = static_cast<HBITMAP>(CopyImage(medium.hBitmap, IMAGE_BITMAP, 0, 0, LR_CREATEDIBSECTION));
            if (! state.Require(copied.hBitmap != nullptr, L"Clone the bitmap clipboard backing before mutation."))
                return false;
            medium = std::move(copied);
        }
        // EnumFormatEtc can advertise several acceptable carriers. The retained
        // object owns only this returned medium; advertising the original mask
        // makes Shell return HGLOBAL for stream-only requests during OLE flush.
        if (SUCCEEDED(read))
            format.tymed = medium.tymed;
        const HRESULT formatStored = SUCCEEDED(read) ? retainedClipboard->SetData(&format, &medium, TRUE) : read;
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(formatStored),
                      std::format(L"Retain clipboard format {} before mutation: hr=0x{:08X}.", format.cfFormat, static_cast<unsigned long>(formatStored)));
        if (FAILED(formatStored))
            return false;
        static_cast<void>(medium.release());
        if (expected)
            expected->push_back({format, std::move(bytes)});
        ++retainedFormats;
    }
    state.Require(clipboardSnapshotComplete, L"Clipboard snapshot must finish within the bounded format limit before testing copy.");
    if (! clipboardSnapshotComplete)
        return false;
    // Shell can coalesce entries with the same format key. Prove the retained
    // object can still serve every admitted value before replacing the clipboard.
    return ! expected || VerifyFileOperationsClipboardBytes(*retainedClipboard, *expected, state);
}

// Tracks only this fixture's admitted generation. Process ownership alone cannot
// distinguish a later copy from another window in the same process. These checks
// detect interference; Win32/OLE provides no atomic compare-and-publish operation.
struct FileOperationsClipboardGeneration
{
    DWORD sequence = 0u;
    HWND owner     = nullptr;
    bool valid     = false;
    bool published = false;

    [[nodiscard]] bool MatchesCurrent() const noexcept
    {
        return valid && GetClipboardSequenceNumber() == sequence && GetClipboardOwner() == owner && GetClipboardSequenceNumber() == sequence;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool Record(HWND expectedOwner, bool wasPublished) noexcept
    {
        sequence  = GetClipboardSequenceNumber();
        owner     = GetClipboardOwner();
        valid     = owner == expectedOwner && GetClipboardSequenceNumber() == sequence;
        published = published || wasPublished;
        return valid;
    }
};

// OLE publication wakes desktop clipboard readers. Reuse the canonical bounded
// quiet-point policy, and never flush an object that another publisher replaced.
[[nodiscard]] HRESULT FlushOwnedFileOperationsClipboardForSelfTest(IDataObject* expected,
                                                                   FileOperationsClipboardGeneration& generation,
                                                                   unsigned simulatedOpenFailures = 0u) noexcept
{
    for (unsigned attempt = 0u; attempt < 3u; ++attempt)
    {
        const bool quiet = WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(nullptr);
        // Delayed rendering may advance the sequence during the wait. Admit it
        // only with exact OLE object identity and the same publishing window.
        if (! generation.valid || GetClipboardOwner() != generation.owner || OleIsCurrentClipboard(expected) != S_OK ||
            ! generation.Record(generation.owner, true))
            return HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED);
        if (! quiet)
            return HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_BUSY);
        const bool injected  = attempt < simulatedOpenFailures;
        const HRESULT result = injected ? CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN : OleFlushClipboard();
        if (! generation.Record(generation.owner, true))
            return HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED);
        if (result != CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN)
            return result;
        // CANT_OPEN means the flush did not open/mutate the clipboard. Retry
        // materialization, never OleSetClipboard; recheck identity on each attempt.
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.FlushContention", injected ? L"injected" : L"native", 0u, attempt + 1u, 0u, result);
    }
    return CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsClipboardSnapshotMediumContract(CaseState& state,
                                                                     FileOperationsClipboardGeneration* clipboardGeneration = nullptr) noexcept
{
    const bool clipboardRoundTrip = clipboardGeneration != nullptr;
    if (! clipboardRoundTrip)
    {
        unsigned int attempts   = 0u;
        const HRESULT transient = RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept { return ++attempts == 1u ? CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN : S_OK; });
        state.Require(transient == S_OK && attempts == 2u, L"A transient read-only clipboard lock must recover within the bounded policy.");
        attempts                = 0u;
        const HRESULT permanent = RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept
        {
            ++attempts;
            return DV_E_TYMED;
        });
        state.Require(permanent == DV_E_TYMED && attempts == 1u, L"Unsupported clipboard media must fail immediately without a lock retry.");
        attempts           = 0u;
        const HRESULT busy = RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept
        {
            ++attempts;
            return CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN;
        });
        state.Require(busy == CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN && attempts == 3u, L"Persistent clipboard contention must stop at three read attempts and remain failed.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
    }
    // By default this regression uses owned data objects only. The directed popup
    // fixture also runs OLE flush/readback inside its existing clipboard restore scope.
    wil::com_ptr<IDataObject> source;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(SHCreateDataObject(nullptr, 0u, nullptr, nullptr, IID_PPV_ARGS(source.put()))), L"Create owned clipboard source.");
    if (! source)
        return false;
    const UINT htmlFormat = RegisterClipboardFormatW(L"HTML Format");
    state.Require(htmlFormat != 0u, L"Register the HTML fixture format.");
    // Native clipboard readers may parse advertised formats. Publish valid CF_HTML
    // with UTF-8 byte offsets and its terminating NUL, never opaque invalid HTML.
    static constexpr char htmlPayload[] = "Version:1.0\r\nStartHTML:0000000105\r\nEndHTML:0000000181\r\n"
                                          "StartFragment:0000000137\r\nEndFragment:0000000149\r\n"
                                          "<html><body><!--StartFragment--><b>caf\xc3\xa9</b><!--EndFragment--></body></html>";
    constexpr std::string_view payload{htmlPayload, sizeof(htmlPayload)};
    FORMATETC format{static_cast<CLIPFORMAT>(htmlFormat), nullptr, DVASPECT_CONTENT, -1, TYMED_HGLOBAL | TYMED_ISTREAM};
    // A producer can retain and modify an HGLOBAL through its release owner.
    // Use an actual owning stream to exercise that lifetime without touching
    // storage after SetData(TRUE) has transferred the medium.
    wil::com_ptr<IStream> htmlBacking;
    ULONG htmlWritten = 0u;
    if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(CreateStreamOnHGlobal(nullptr, TRUE, htmlBacking.put())) &&
                            SUCCEEDED(htmlBacking->Write(payload.data(), static_cast<ULONG>(payload.size()), &htmlWritten)) && htmlWritten == payload.size(),
                        L"Create the shared HTML global-memory producer."))
        return false;
    wil::unique_stg_medium seed;
    seed.tymed         = TYMED_HGLOBAL;
    HGLOBAL htmlGlobal = nullptr;
    if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(GetHGlobalFromStream(htmlBacking.get(), &htmlGlobal)), L"Obtain the producer-owned HTML backing."))
        return false;
    auto htmlOwner       = htmlBacking;
    seed.hGlobal         = htmlGlobal;
    seed.pUnkForRelease  = htmlOwner.detach();
    const HRESULT seeded = source->SetData(&format, &seed, TRUE);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(seeded), L"Seed the owned multi-medium format.");
    if (FAILED(seeded))
        return false;
    static_cast<void>(seed.release());
    // PNG can arrive as a stream. Persist exact bytes through native HGLOBAL,
    // including when the original owner invalidates its backing after snapshot.
    const UINT pngFormat = RegisterClipboardFormatW(L"PNG");
    // One opaque red RGBA pixel; valid PNG chunks, stored-deflate data and checksums.
    constexpr std::array<unsigned char, 73> pngPayload{{0x89u, 0x50u, 0x4eu, 0x47u, 0x0du, 0x0au, 0x1au, 0x0au, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x0du, 0x49u, 0x48u, 0x44u,
                                                        0x52u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x01u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x01u, 0x08u, 0x06u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x1fu,
                                                        0x15u, 0xc4u, 0x89u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x10u, 0x49u, 0x44u, 0x41u, 0x54u, 0x78u, 0x01u, 0x01u, 0x05u,
                                                        0x00u, 0xfau, 0xffu, 0x00u, 0xffu, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0xffu, 0x05u, 0x00u, 0x01u, 0xffu, 0xfau, 0x5cu, 0x88u,
                                                        0xd1u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x49u, 0x45u, 0x4eu, 0x44u, 0xaeu, 0x42u, 0x60u, 0x82u}};
    wil::com_ptr<IStream> pngBacking;
    state.Require(pngFormat != 0u && SUCCEEDED(CreateStreamOnHGlobal(nullptr, TRUE, pngBacking.put())), L"Create owned PNG stream backing.");
    if (! pngBacking)
        return false;
    ULONG written = 0u;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(pngBacking->Write(pngPayload.data(), static_cast<ULONG>(pngPayload.size()), &written)) && written == pngPayload.size() &&
                      SUCCEEDED(pngBacking->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_SET, nullptr)),
                  L"Seed exact owned PNG bytes.");
    {
        wil::com_ptr<IWICImagingFactory> factory;
        wil::com_ptr<IStream> decodeStream;
        wil::com_ptr<IWICBitmapDecoder> decoder;
        wil::com_ptr<IWICBitmapFrameDecode> frame;
        wil::com_ptr<IWICFormatConverter> converter;
        UINT width  = 0u;
        UINT height = 0u;
        std::array<BYTE, 4> pixel{};
        if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WICImagingFactory, nullptr, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, IID_PPV_ARGS(factory.put()))) &&
                                SUCCEEDED(pngBacking->Clone(decodeStream.put())) &&
                                SUCCEEDED(factory->CreateDecoderFromStream(decodeStream.get(), nullptr, WICDecodeMetadataCacheOnLoad, decoder.put())) &&
                                SUCCEEDED(decoder->GetFrame(0u, frame.put())) && SUCCEEDED(frame->GetSize(&width, &height)) && width == 1u && height == 1u &&
                                SUCCEEDED(factory->CreateFormatConverter(converter.put())) &&
                                SUCCEEDED(converter->Initialize(
                                    frame.get(), GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppRGBA, WICBitmapDitherTypeNone, nullptr, 0.0, WICBitmapPaletteTypeCustom)) &&
                                SUCCEEDED(converter->CopyPixels(nullptr, 4u, static_cast<UINT>(pixel.size()), pixel.data())) &&
                                pixel == std::array<BYTE, 4>{{255u, 0u, 0u, 255u}},
                            L"The native PNG fixture must decode to its intended pixel before any clipboard publication."))
            return false;
    }
    FORMATETC pngRequest{static_cast<CLIPFORMAT>(pngFormat), nullptr, DVASPECT_CONTENT, -1, TYMED_ISTREAM};
    wil::unique_stg_medium pngSeed;
    pngSeed.tymed    = TYMED_ISTREAM;
    auto streamOwner = pngBacking;
    pngSeed.pstm     = streamOwner.detach();
    std::vector<std::byte> cursorProbe;
    LARGE_INTEGER offset{};
    offset.QuadPart = 5;
    ULARGE_INTEGER position{};
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(pngSeed.pstm->Seek(offset, STREAM_SEEK_SET, nullptr)) && SUCCEEDED(ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(pngSeed, cursorProbe)) &&
                      SUCCEEDED(pngSeed.pstm->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_CUR, &position)) && position.QuadPart == 5u && cursorProbe.size() == pngPayload.size() &&
                      std::memcmp(cursorProbe.data(), pngPayload.data(), pngPayload.size()) == 0 && SUCCEEDED(pngSeed.pstm->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_SET, nullptr)),
                  L"Materializing a stream must preserve its source cursor and include bytes before that cursor.");
    const HRESULT pngSeeded = source->SetData(&pngRequest, &pngSeed, TRUE);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(pngSeeded), L"Publish the owned PNG stream format.");
    if (FAILED(pngSeeded))
        return false;
    static_cast<void>(pngSeed.release());
    BITMAPINFO bitmapInfo{};
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biSize        = sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER);
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biWidth       = 2;
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biHeight      = -2;
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biPlanes      = 1u;
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biBitCount    = 32u;
    bitmapInfo.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB;
    void* bitmapPixels                 = nullptr;
    wil::unique_stg_medium bitmapSeed;
    bitmapSeed.tymed   = TYMED_GDI;
    bitmapSeed.hBitmap = CreateDIBSection(nullptr, &bitmapInfo, DIB_RGB_COLORS, &bitmapPixels, nullptr, 0u);
    if (! state.Require(bitmapSeed.hBitmap && bitmapPixels, L"Create an owned bitmap clipboard fixture."))
        return false;
    constexpr std::array<uint32_t, 4> bitmapPayload{{0x00123456u, 0xff654321u, 0x80abcdefu, 0x00fedcbau}};
    std::memcpy(bitmapPixels, bitmapPayload.data(), sizeof(bitmapPayload));
    FORMATETC bitmapRequest{CF_BITMAP, nullptr, DVASPECT_CONTENT, -1, TYMED_GDI};
    if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(source->SetData(&bitmapRequest, &bitmapSeed, TRUE)), L"Publish owned bitmap fixture pixels."))
        return false;
    static_cast<void>(bitmapSeed.release());
    for (size_t round = 0u; round < 3u; ++round)
    {
        wil::com_ptr<IDataObject> retained;
        size_t count = 0u;
        std::vector<FileOperationsClipboardFormatBytes> expected;
        if (! RetainFileOperationsClipboardFormats(*source, retained, count, state, &expected))
            return false;
        if (round == 0u)
        {
            constexpr std::array<unsigned char, payload.size()> replacementHtml{};
            if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(htmlBacking->Seek({}, STREAM_SEEK_SET, nullptr)) &&
                                    SUCCEEDED(htmlBacking->Write(replacementHtml.data(), static_cast<ULONG>(replacementHtml.size()), &htmlWritten)) &&
                                    htmlWritten == replacementHtml.size(),
                                L"Invalidate the producer-owned HTML bytes after snapshot."))
                return false;
            wil::unique_stg_medium htmlProbe;
            htmlProbe.tymed = TYMED_ISTREAM;
            auto probeOwner = htmlBacking;
            htmlProbe.pstm  = probeOwner.detach();
            std::vector<std::byte> changedHtml;
            if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(htmlProbe, changedHtml)) && changedHtml.size() == payload.size() &&
                                    std::memcmp(changedHtml.data(), payload.data(), payload.size()) != 0,
                                L"The shared HGLOBAL producer must prove its original bytes changed after snapshot."))
                return false;
            htmlBacking.reset();
            // SetData(TRUE) transferred the original pixels: replace the source
            // through IDataObject rather than touching a handed-off raw pointer.
            void* replacementPixels = nullptr;
            wil::unique_stg_medium replacement;
            replacement.tymed   = TYMED_GDI;
            replacement.hBitmap = CreateDIBSection(nullptr, &bitmapInfo, DIB_RGB_COLORS, &replacementPixels, nullptr, 0u);
            if (! state.Require(replacement.hBitmap && replacementPixels, L"Create the replacement bitmap source."))
                return false;
            std::memset(replacementPixels, 0, sizeof(bitmapPayload));
            if (! state.Require(SUCCEEDED(source->SetData(&bitmapRequest, &replacement, TRUE)), L"Replace original bitmap pixels after snapshot."))
                return false;
            static_cast<void>(replacement.release());
            wil::unique_stg_medium changedBitmap;
            std::vector<std::byte> changedPixels;
            if (! state.Require(
                    SUCCEEDED(source->GetData(&bitmapRequest, &changedBitmap)) && SUCCEEDED(ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(changedBitmap, changedPixels)) &&
                        std::ranges::any_of(expected, [&](const auto& value) { return value.format.cfFormat == CF_BITMAP && value.bytes != changedPixels; }),
                    L"The bitmap fixture must prove its source changed after the independent snapshot."))
                return false;
        }
        source.reset();
        if (round == 0u)
        {
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(pngBacking->SetSize({})), L"Invalidate only the fixture-owned PNG backing after snapshot.");
            pngBacking.reset();
        }
        state.Require(count > 0u, L"Retain the rich format after releasing its source.");
        if (clipboardRoundTrip)
        {
            const bool quiet        = WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(nullptr);
            const bool unchanged    = clipboardGeneration->MatchesCurrent();
            const HRESULT published = ! quiet       ? HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_BUSY)
                                      : ! unchanged ? HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED)
                                                    : OleSetClipboard(retained.get());
            HRESULT flushed         = published;
            if (SUCCEEDED(published))
            {
                const HWND publishedOwner = GetClipboardOwner();
                const bool current        = publishedOwner && OleIsCurrentClipboard(retained.get()) == S_OK;
                if (! current || ! clipboardGeneration->Record(publishedOwner, true))
                {
                    clipboardGeneration->valid = false;
                    flushed                    = HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED);
                }
                else
                {
                    if (round == 0u)
                    {
                        const HRESULT busy = FlushOwnedFileOperationsClipboardForSelfTest(retained.get(), *clipboardGeneration, 3u);
                        if (! state.Require(busy == CLIPBRD_E_CANT_OPEN && OleIsCurrentClipboard(retained.get()) == S_OK &&
                                                clipboardGeneration->MatchesCurrent(),
                                            L"Persistent flush-open contention must stop without replacing or releasing the current fixture object."))
                            return false;
                    }
                    flushed = FlushOwnedFileOperationsClipboardForSelfTest(retained.get(), *clipboardGeneration, round == 0u ? 2u : 0u);
                }
            }
            DWORD clipboardProcess     = 0u;
            const HWND clipboardWindow = GetOpenClipboardWindow();
            if (clipboardWindow)
                static_cast<void>(GetWindowThreadProcessId(clipboardWindow, &clipboardProcess));
            Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.Publish", L"owned rich-format fixture; contents omitted", 0u, round, count, published);
            Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.Flush", L"owned rich-format fixture; contents omitted", 0u, round, clipboardProcess, flushed);
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(flushed),
                          std::format(L"Flush the owned rich-format snapshot before readback: round={}, set=0x{:08X}, flush=0x{:08X}, openClipboardPid={}.",
                                      round,
                                      static_cast<unsigned long>(published),
                                      static_cast<unsigned long>(flushed),
                                      clipboardProcess));
            if (FAILED(flushed))
                return false;
            retained.reset();
            const HRESULT restored = RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept { return OleGetClipboard(retained.put()); });
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(restored), L"Reacquire the flushed rich-format clipboard data object.");
            if (FAILED(restored))
                return false;
        }
        if (! VerifyFileOperationsClipboardBytes(*retained, expected, state))
            return false;
        // Prove the readback check rejects corruption, not only missing formats.
        const auto nonempty = std::ranges::find_if(expected, [](const auto& value) noexcept { return ! value.bytes.empty(); });
        if (! state.Require(nonempty != expected.end(), L"The rich-format fixture must contain bytes for its corruption witness."))
            return false;
        CaseState mismatch;
        nonempty->bytes.front() ^= std::byte{1u};
        state.Require(! VerifyFileOperationsClipboardBytes(*retained, expected, mismatch) && ! mismatch.failure.empty(),
                      L"Clipboard readback verification must detect a changed payload byte.");
        FORMATETC globalRequest = format;
        globalRequest.tymed     = TYMED_HGLOBAL;
        wil::unique_stg_medium global;
        const HRESULT read = ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(*retained, globalRequest, global);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.GlobalReadback", std::to_wstring(globalRequest.cfFormat), 0u, round, global.tymed, read);
        state.Require(
            SUCCEEDED(read) && global.tymed == TYMED_HGLOBAL && GlobalSize(global.hGlobal) >= payload.size(),
            std::format(L"Retained rich format must expose its complete global-memory payload: round={}, format={}, hr=0x{:08X}, carrier={}, size={}.",
                        round,
                        globalRequest.cfFormat,
                        static_cast<unsigned long>(read),
                        global.tymed,
                        global.tymed == TYMED_HGLOBAL ? GlobalSize(global.hGlobal) : 0u));
        if (FAILED(read) || global.tymed != TYMED_HGLOBAL || GlobalSize(global.hGlobal) < payload.size())
            return false;
        {
            const void* data = GlobalLock(global.hGlobal);
            state.Require(data != nullptr, L"Lock retained rich-format bytes.");
            if (! data)
                return false;
            const auto unlock = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(GlobalUnlock(global.hGlobal)); });
            state.Require(std::memcmp(data, payload.data(), payload.size()) == 0, L"Rich-format bytes must survive each independent snapshot.");
        }
        FORMATETC streamRequest = format;
        streamRequest.tymed     = TYMED_ISTREAM;
        wil::unique_stg_medium stream;
        const HRESULT streamRead = ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(*retained, streamRequest, stream);
        state.Require(FAILED(streamRead) || stream.tymed == TYMED_ISTREAM, L"A retained format must not return a global handle for a stream-only request.");
        wil::unique_stg_medium png;
        FORMATETC serializedPngRequest = pngRequest;
        serializedPngRequest.tymed     = TYMED_HGLOBAL;
        const HRESULT pngRead          = ReadFileOperationsClipboardMediumForSelfTest(*retained, serializedPngRequest, png);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(pngRead) && png.tymed == TYMED_HGLOBAL,
                      std::format(L"Retained PNG bytes after backing invalidation: round={}, OLE={}, hr=0x{:08X}, carrier={}.",
                                  round,
                                  clipboardRoundTrip,
                                  static_cast<unsigned long>(pngRead),
                                  png.tymed));
        if (FAILED(pngRead) || png.tymed != TYMED_HGLOBAL)
            return false;
        std::vector<std::byte> actualPng;
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(ReadFileOperationsClipboardBytes(png, actualPng)) && actualPng.size() == pngPayload.size() &&
                          std::memcmp(actualPng.data(), pngPayload.data(), pngPayload.size()) == 0,
                      L"PNG bytes must survive backing invalidation and each independent snapshot/flush/readback.");
        if (clipboardRoundTrip && ! state.Require(clipboardGeneration->MatchesCurrent(),
                                                  L"Clipboard changed during fixture readback; preserve the newer contents without another publication."))
            return false;
        source = std::move(retained);
    }
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot MakeThroughputPersistenceTask()
{
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot task{};
    task.taskId                   = 91001u;
    task.operation                = FILESYSTEM_COPY;
    task.lifecyclePhase           = static_cast<uint8_t>(FileOperations::TaskLifecyclePhase::Running);
    task.started                  = true;
    task.discoveryClosed          = true;
    task.hasProgressCallbacks     = true;
    task.lastProgressCallbackTick = GetTickCount64();
    task.operationStartTick       = task.lastProgressCallbackTick - 25000u;
    task.currentSourcePath        = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.currentDestinationPath   = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Archives familiales\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.totalBytes               = 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
    task.completedBytes           = task.totalBytes / 2u;
    task.totalItems               = 10u;
    task.completedItems           = 5u;
    return task;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupThroughputExternalReload(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperationsPopupInternal;
    auto* operations = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    if (! state.Require(operations && ! operations->HasActiveOperations() && ! operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest(),
                        L"The reload fixture requires exclusive ownership of an idle operation popup."))
        return false;
    const auto originalSettings = g_settings;
    SettingsHotReload::Stop();
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Pending settings writes must settle before external reload.");
    const auto restore = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        SettingsHotReload::Stop();
        static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
        // Restore through the same application route, including theme/plugin participants.
        auto restored = originalSettings;
        Common::Settings::Settings latest{};
        const HRESULT loaded = Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", latest);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(loaded), L"Could not obtain the latest isolated settings stamp for restoration.");
        if (FAILED(loaded))
            return;
        restored.persistence.expectedFileStamp = latest.persistence.expectedFileStamp;
        RestoreMainSettingsHotReload(mainWindow, state);
        const HRESULT saved = Common::Settings::SaveSettings(L"RedSalamander", restored);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(saved), L"Could not restore the original isolated settings after reload.");
        if (SUCCEEDED(saved))
        {
            const auto settled = RedSalamander::TestSupport::PumpMessagesUntil([&]() noexcept {
                return g_settings.persistence.expectedFileStamp == restored.persistence.expectedFileStamp;
            }, {.timeout = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(5000)), .operationName = L"throughput reload restoration"});
            state.Require(settled.conditionMet, settled.timeoutDiagnostic);
        }
    });
    operations->SetPopupFooterOnly(false);
    operations->SetPopupCompactDensity(false);
    operations->SetPopupThroughputExpanded(true);
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"The baseline settings save did not settle.");
    RestoreMainSettingsHotReload(mainWindow, state);
    operations->DebugEnsurePopupVisibleForSelfTest();
    const HWND popup      = operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest();
    const auto closePopup = wil::scope_exit([&] { operations->DebugResetPopupForSelfTest(); });
    if (! state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"The production-owned popup was not created."))
        return false;
    auto* popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup, GWLP_USERDATA));
    if (! state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"The production-owned popup has no retained state."))
        return false;
    auto task = MakeThroughputPersistenceTask();
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    // A reload can arrive while the user sizes a short popup. Keep that real
    // sizing state active so a new card cannot rely on automatic window growth
    // to become visible. All interaction remains inside the owned test window.
    SendMessageW(popup, WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    const auto finishSizing = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        if (operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest() == popup)
            SendMessageW(popup, WM_EXITSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    });
    const UINT dpi          = GetDpiForWindow(popup);
    RECT desired{0, 0, MulDiv(720, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96), MulDiv(260, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)};
    state.Require(
        AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(
            &desired, static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup, GWL_STYLE)), FALSE, static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup, GWL_EXSTYLE)), dpi) !=
                FALSE &&
            SetWindowPos(popup, nullptr, 0, 0, desired.right - desired.left, desired.bottom - desired.top, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE) != FALSE,
        L"Could not establish the short reload viewport.");
    const auto read = [&](uint64_t id)
    {
        for (size_t pass = 0u; pass < 3u; ++pass)
            RedrawWindow(popup, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
        PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot layout{};
        layout.taskId = id;
        state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, layout), L"The live reload layout snapshot is unavailable.");
        return layout;
    };
    state.Require(read(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded, L"The baseline card must start expanded.");
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Popup creation saves must settle before measuring reload.");
    const auto generation = operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration;
    for (const bool expanded : {false, true})
    {
        Common::Settings::Settings external{};
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", external)), L"Could not load the external writer's snapshot.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
        if (! external.fileOperations)
            external.fileOperations.emplace();
        external.fileOperations->popupThroughputExpanded = expanded;
        const auto started                               = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::SaveSettings(L"RedSalamander", external)), L"Could not publish the external throughput preference.");
        const auto applied = RedSalamander::TestSupport::PumpMessagesUntil([&]() noexcept {
            return operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded() == expanded && g_settings.persistence.expectedFileStamp == external.persistence.expectedFileStamp;
        }, {.timeout = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(5000)), .operationName = L"production throughput settings reload"});
        state.Require(applied.conditionMet, applied.timeoutDiagnostic);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Throughput.ExternalReloadUs",
                          expanded ? L"expanded" : L"collapsed",
                          static_cast<uint64_t>(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count()),
                          generation,
                          operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration,
                          S_OK);
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
        state.Require(operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest() == popup && read(91001u).taskThroughputExpanded,
                      L"Reload must retain the same owned popup and the first live card's choice.");
        if (expanded)
            state.Require(! read(91002u).taskThroughputExpanded, L"Reload must retain the second live card's collapsed choice.");
        ++task.taskId;
        popupState->debugVisualScenario->push_back(task);
        auto added = read(task.taskId);
        state.Require(added.taskThroughputExpanded == expanded,
                      std::format(L"The new card must adopt the external default: task={} expected={} actual={} compact={} scroll={}.",
                                  task.taskId,
                                  expanded,
                                  added.taskThroughputExpanded,
                                  added.taskCompactRow,
                                  added.scrollPosition));
        // Preference state exists for offscreen cards; the visible summary does
        // not. Reveal the appended card before asserting the rendered state.
        SendMessageW(popup, WM_VSCROLL, SB_BOTTOM, 0);
        const auto visible = RedSalamander::TestSupport::PumpMessagesUntil(
            [&]() noexcept
        {
            added = read(task.taskId);
            return added.taskThroughputSummaryVisible != expanded;
        },
            {.timeout = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(5000)), .operationName = L"reloaded card throughput presentation"});
        state.Require(visible.conditionMet, visible.timeoutDiagnostic);
        state.Require(operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration == generation,
                      L"Applying and displaying an external preference must not enqueue a settings write.");
    }
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupThroughputRestart(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperationsPopupInternal;
    using namespace RedSalamander::TestSupport;
    constexpr std::wstring_view modeEnvironment = L"REDSALAMANDER_THROUGHPUT_RESTART_CHILD";
    const std::wstring mode                     = RedSalamander::TestSupport::GetEnvironmentString(modeEnvironment);
    auto* operations                            = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    if (! state.Require(operations && ! operations->HasActiveOperations() && ! operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest(),
                        L"The restart fixture requires an idle operation popup."))
        return false;
    if (! mode.empty())
    {
        const bool initialExpanded = mode != L"collapsed-expand";
