        state.Require(mode == L"default-collapse" || mode == L"collapsed-expand" || mode == L"expanded", L"Unknown throughput restart child mode.");
        state.Require(operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded() == initialExpanded,
                      L"Startup must load the saved throughput preference before the fixture changes any settings.");
        if (mode != L"default-collapse")
            state.Require(g_settings.windows.contains(L"FileOperationsPopup") && g_settings.windows.contains(L"FileOperationsPopupExpanded"),
                          L"Both popup placement records must survive an application process restart.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
        operations->DebugEnsurePopupVisibleForSelfTest();
        const HWND popup      = operations->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest();
        const auto closePopup = wil::scope_exit([&] { operations->DebugResetPopupForSelfTest(); });
        if (! state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"The restarted application could not create its owned popup."))
            return false;
        auto* popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup, GWLP_USERDATA));
        if (! state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"The restarted popup state is unavailable."))
            return false;
        const auto task = MakeThroughputPersistenceTask();
        popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
        for (size_t pass = 0u; pass < 3u; ++pass)
            RedrawWindow(popup, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
        PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot layout{};
        layout.taskId = task.taskId;
        state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, layout) && layout.taskThroughputExpanded == initialExpanded &&
                          layout.hostedGraphControlCount == (initialExpanded ? 1u : 0u),
                      L"The restarted card and graph must reflect the preference loaded at application startup.");
        if (mode != L"expanded")
            state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, {PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskToggleThroughput, task.taskId, 0u}),
                          L"The child must save its next preference through the real disclosure action.");
        operations->SavePopupPlacement(popup);
        operations->SavePopupExpandedPlacement(popup);
        operations->DebugResetPopupForSelfTest();
        state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"The child disclosure and placement saves did not settle before exit.");
        Debug::Perf::Emit(
            L"FileOps.Popup.Throughput.RestartChild", mode, GetCurrentProcessId(), initialExpanded, operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded(), S_OK);
        return state.failure.empty();
    }
    SettingsHotReload::Stop();
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Parent settings writes must settle before child isolation.");
    const auto restoreWatcher = wil::scope_exit([&] { RestoreMainSettingsHotReload(mainWindow, state); });
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    const auto artifactRoot = SelfTest::GetSuiteArtifactPath(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands, L"throughput-restart");
    state.Require(! artifactRoot.empty() && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(artifactRoot), L"The restart receipt directory is unavailable.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    try
    {
        std::error_code rootError;
        const auto root = ResolveTestSandboxBase(rootError);
        if (! state.Require(! rootError && ! root.empty(), L"The validated child test sandbox root is unavailable."))
            return false;
        const std::wstring runId = std::format(L"throughput-restart-{}-{}", GetCurrentProcessId(), GetTickCount64());
        ScopedEnvironmentVariable isolatedRoot(kTestRootEnvironmentVariable, std::wstring_view(root.native()));
        ScopedEnvironmentVariable isolatedRun(kTestRunIdEnvironmentVariable, std::wstring_view(runId));
        const auto childRun = root / L"runs" / runId;
        const auto cleanup  = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(SelfTest::RemoveAll(childRun)); });
        Common::Settings::Settings seed{};
        seed.theme.currentThemeId = L"builtin/dark";
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::SaveSettings(L"RedSalamander", seed)), L"Could not seed default settings in the isolated child run.");
        const auto executable = wil::GetModuleFileNameW();
        if (! state.Require(static_cast<bool>(executable), L"The restart executable path is unavailable.") || ! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
        std::array<std::optional<Common::Settings::WindowPlacement>, 2> savedPlacements{};
        for (const auto* childMode : {L"default-collapse", L"collapsed-expand", L"expanded"})
        {
            ScopedEnvironmentVariable childModeEnvironment(modeEnvironment, std::wstring_view(childMode));
            const auto result = RunChildProcess(
                {.executablePath = executable.get(),
                 .arguments      = {L"--commands-selftest", L"--selftest-timeout-multiplier=3", L"--selftest-case=cmd_pane_fileops_popup_throughput_restart"},
                 .timeout        = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(30000))});
            const auto receipt = std::format("mode={}\nprocessId={}\nexitCode={}\nelapsedMs={}\ncompleted={}\n",
                                             Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(childMode),
                                             result.processId,
                                             result.exitCode,
                                             result.elapsed.count(),
                                             result.Completed());
            state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(artifactRoot / (std::wstring(childMode) + L".txt"), receipt),
                          L"Could not preserve the restart child receipt.");
            std::error_code copyError;
            const auto childResults = childRun / L"artifacts" / L"selftest" / L"last_run" / L"commands" / L"results.json";
            const bool copied       = std::filesystem::copy_file(
                childResults, artifactRoot / (std::wstring(childMode) + L"-results.json"), std::filesystem::copy_options::overwrite_existing, copyError);
            state.Require(copied && ! copyError, L"Could not preserve the child assertions before restarting it.");
            state.Require(result.Completed() && result.exitCode == 0u,
                          std::format(L"Throughput restart child '{}' failed: {} (exit {}).", childMode, result.diagnostic, result.exitCode));
            if (! state.failure.empty())
                return false;
            Common::Settings::Settings saved{};
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", saved)),
                          L"The parent must read the child settings after process shutdown.");
            const bool expectedExpanded = std::wstring_view(childMode) != L"default-collapse";
            state.Require((! saved.fileOperations || saved.fileOperations->popupThroughputExpanded) == expectedExpanded,
                          L"Process shutdown must preserve the disclosure saved by the child UI.");
            size_t index = 0u;
            for (const auto* id : {L"FileOperationsPopup", L"FileOperationsPopupExpanded"})
            {
                const auto found = saved.windows.find(id);
                if (! state.Require(found != saved.windows.end(), L"The restarted process must retain both placement records on disk."))
                    return false;
                const auto& actual = found->second;
                if (savedPlacements[index])
                {
                    const auto& expected = *savedPlacements[index];
                    state.Require(actual.state == expected.state && actual.bounds.x == expected.bounds.x && actual.bounds.y == expected.bounds.y &&
                                      actual.bounds.width == expected.bounds.width && actual.bounds.height == expected.bounds.height &&
                                      actual.dpi == expected.dpi,
                                  L"Restart and disclosure changes must preserve each saved position, dimensions, state and DPI.");
                }
                savedPlacements[index++] = actual;
            }
            if (! state.failure.empty())
                return false;
        }
    }
    catch (const std::system_error& error)
    {
        // The test must not fall back to the user's settings if environment isolation fails.
        state.Require(false, std::format(L"Throughput restart isolation failed: {}.", error.code().value()));
    }
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupThroughputDisclosure(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state, uint32_t repeatIndex) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperationsPopupInternal;
    auto* operations = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(operations != nullptr, L"File operations state is required for the throughput fixture.");
    if (! operations)
        return false;
    const auto previousSettings = g_settings.fileOperations;
    const auto previousWindows  = g_settings.windows;
    const auto previousTheme    = g_folderWindow.GetTheme();
    auto restore                = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
        g_settings.fileOperations = previousSettings;
        g_settings.windows        = previousWindows;
        g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(previousTheme);
        static_cast<void>(SettingsHotReload::QueueSettingsSave(L"RedSalamander", g_settings, L"FileOps.Settings.SaveUs", L"throughput fixture restore"));
        static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
    });
    operations->SetPopupFooterOnly(false);
    operations->SetPopupCompactDensity(false);
    operations->SetPopupThroughputExpanded(true);
    auto theme                  = previousTheme;
    theme.reducedMotionOverride = true;
    g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(theme);
    auto lifetime = std::make_shared<int>(0);
    wil::unique_hwnd popup(FileOperationsPopup::Create(operations, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
    state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"Could not create the throughput fixture popup.");
    if (! popup)
        return false;
    auto* popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWLP_USERDATA));
    state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"Throughput fixture popup state is unavailable.");
    if (! popupState)
        return false;
    TaskSnapshot task{};
    task.taskId                    = 90001u;
    task.operation                 = FILESYSTEM_COPY;
    task.lifecyclePhase            = static_cast<uint8_t>(FileOperations::TaskLifecyclePhase::Running);
    task.started                   = true;
    task.discoveryClosed           = true;
    task.hasProgressCallbacks      = true;
    task.lastProgressCallbackTick  = GetTickCount64();
    task.operationStartTick        = task.lastProgressCallbackTick - 25000u;
    task.currentSourcePath         = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives de la famille\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.currentDestinationPath    = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Archives de la famille\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.totalBytes                = 8ull * 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
    task.completedBytes            = task.totalBytes / 2u;
    task.totalItems                = 128u;
    task.completedItems            = 64u;
    const TaskSnapshot runningTask = task;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
    for (size_t redraw = 0u; redraw < 3u; ++redraw)
        RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
    for (size_t batch = 0u; batch < 5u; ++batch)
    {
        Debug::Perf::Scope measurement(L"FileOps.Popup.Throughput.ExpandedRedrawBatchUs");
        for (size_t redraw = 0u; redraw < 8u; ++redraw)
            RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
    }
    PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot layout{};
    layout.taskId = task.taskId;
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup.get(), layout), L"Throughput layout snapshot is unavailable.");
    state.Require(layout.hostedGraphControlCount == 1u, L"The running fixture must initially host its throughput graph.");
    state.Require(layout.taskThroughputDisclosureVisible, L"An eligible running task must expose a throughput disclosure without collapsing the task card.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    const auto redraw = [&]
    {
        for (size_t pass = 0u; pass < 3u; ++pass)
            RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
    };
    const auto snapshot = [&](uint64_t taskId)
    {
        PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot result{};
        result.taskId = taskId;
        state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup.get(), result), L"Disclosure transition snapshot is unavailable.");
        return result;
    };
    const auto toggle = [&](uint64_t taskId)
    {
        state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup.get(), {PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskToggleThroughput, taskId, 0u}),
                      L"Could not invoke the published throughput disclosure.");
        redraw();
    };
    RECT expandedWindow{};
    GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &expandedWindow);
    operations->SavePopupPlacement(popup.get());
    const auto placementsBeforeDisclosure = g_settings.windows;
    const auto requirePlacements          = [&](const Common::Settings::Settings& saved)
    {
        for (const auto* id : {L"FileOperationsPopup", L"FileOperationsPopupExpanded"})
        {
            const auto before = placementsBeforeDisclosure.find(id);
            const auto after  = saved.windows.find(id);
            state.Require(before != placementsBeforeDisclosure.end() && after != saved.windows.end(),
                          L"Both ordinary and expanded popup placement keys must survive disclosure persistence.");
            if (before == placementsBeforeDisclosure.end() || after == saved.windows.end())
                continue;
            const auto& expected = before->second;
            const auto& actual   = after->second;
            state.Require(actual.state == expected.state && actual.bounds.x == expected.bounds.x && actual.bounds.y == expected.bounds.y &&
                              actual.bounds.width == expected.bounds.width && actual.bounds.height == expected.bounds.height && actual.dpi == expected.dpi,
                          L"Saving the throughput disclosure must preserve each popup's saved position, dimensions, state and DPI.");
        }
    };
    const auto generationBefore = operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration;
    toggle(task.taskId);
    const auto collapsed = snapshot(task.taskId);
    RECT collapsedWindow{};
    GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &collapsedWindow);
    state.Require(! collapsed.taskThroughputExpanded && collapsed.taskThroughputSummaryVisible && collapsed.hostedGraphControlCount == 0u,
                  L"Collapse must retain rate/ETA while removing the hosted graph and its accessible child.");
    state.Require(collapsed.taskPauseVisible && collapsed.taskCancelVisible && ! collapsed.taskCompactRow && ! collapsed.hasVisibleButtonOverlap,
                  L"Throughput collapse must retain usable task actions and the expanded task card.");
    state.Require(EqualRect(&expandedWindow, &collapsedWindow) != FALSE, L"Throughput disclosure must not resize the outer popup.");
    state.Require(! operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded(), L"The last explicit collapse must update the remembered default.");
    const auto generationAfter = operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration;
    state.Require(generationAfter > generationBefore, L"Disclosure must queue persistence through the existing save coordinator.");
    for (size_t batch = 0u; batch < 5u; ++batch)
    {
        Debug::Perf::Scope measurement(L"FileOps.Popup.Throughput.CollapsedRedrawBatchUs");
        for (size_t pass = 0u; pass < 8u; ++pass)
            RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
    }
    state.Require(operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration == generationAfter,
                  L"Painting a collapsed card must not queue settings writes.");
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Throughput persistence did not settle.");
    Common::Settings::Settings stored{};
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", stored)) && stored.fileOperations.has_value() &&
                      ! stored.fileOperations->popupThroughputExpanded,
                  L"Collapsed throughput must survive the canonical settings-file disk round trip.");
    requirePlacements(stored);

    // Deny atomic replacement of this runner-isolated settings file. A failed
    // asynchronous save must not roll back the live disclosure or pretend that
    // its new default reached disk. Reuse the settings hot-reload failure fixture.
    const auto settingsPath = Common::Settings::GetSettingsPath(L"RedSalamander");
    {
        wil::unique_hfile blockedTarget(
            CreateFileW(settingsPath.c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr));
        state.Require(static_cast<bool>(blockedTarget), L"Could not deny replacement of the isolated throughput settings file.");
        if (! blockedTarget)
            return false;
        toggle(task.taskId);
        const auto queued = operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest().queuedGeneration;
        state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"The denied throughput save did not settle.");
        const auto settled = operations->DebugGetSettingsSaveSnapshotForSelfTest();
        state.Require(settled.completedGeneration >= queued && ! settled.pending && ! settled.saveInProgress,
                      L"The denied save must finish processing rather than leave a stuck persistence worker.");
        state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded && operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded(),
                      L"A failed save must preserve the live expanded card and its in-memory default.");
        Common::Settings::Settings afterDeniedSave{};
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", afterDeniedSave)) && afterDeniedSave.fileOperations.has_value() &&
                          ! afterDeniedSave.fileOperations->popupThroughputExpanded,
                      L"The denied save must leave the previously collapsed setting intact on disk.");
        requirePlacements(afterDeniedSave);
    }
    for (const bool expectedExpanded : {false, true, false})
    {
        toggle(task.taskId);
        state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Throughput saving did not recover after replacement was allowed.");
        Common::Settings::Settings recovered{};
        const HRESULT reloadHr       = Common::Settings::TryLoadSettingsNoRecovery(L"RedSalamander", recovered);
        const bool persistedExpanded = ! recovered.fileOperations.has_value() || recovered.fileOperations->popupThroughputExpanded;
        const bool liveExpanded      = snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded;
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(reloadHr) && persistedExpanded == expectedExpanded && liveExpanded == expectedExpanded,
                      std::format(L"Disclosure saving must recover: reload=0x{:08X}, expected={}, persisted={}, live={}, explicit settings={}.",
                                  static_cast<unsigned long>(reloadHr),
                                  expectedExpanded,
                                  persistedExpanded,
                                  liveExpanded,
                                  recovered.fileOperations.has_value()));
        requirePlacements(recovered);
    }

    // A second card keeps its own choice when the first card updates the global default.
    auto second = task;
    second.taskId += 1u;
    popupState->debugVisualScenario->push_back(second);
    redraw();
    state.Require(! snapshot(second.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded, L"A new card must start from the remembered collapsed default.");
    toggle(task.taskId);
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded && ! snapshot(second.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded,
                  L"Expanding one card must not move the other live card.");
    operations->SetPopupThroughputExpanded(false);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded, L"A changed remembered default must not reset a live card.");

    operations->SetPopupCompactDensity(true);
    // Remove explicit density overrides installed by the synthetic initial scenario.
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task, second}, true, true);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedGraphControlCount == 0u && ! snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputDisclosureVisible,
                  L"Compact cards must temporarily suppress throughput visuals.");
    operations->SetPopupCompactDensity(false);
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task, second}, false, true);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded && ! snapshot(second.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded,
                  L"Density changes must retain each live card's throughput choice.");
    operations->SetPopupFooterOnly(true);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedGraphControlCount == 0u, L"Footer-only mode must remove all graph controls.");
    operations->SetPopupFooterOnly(false);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded && ! operations->GetPopupThroughputExpanded(),
                  L"Footer-only mode must not rewrite the disclosure choice or remembered default.");

    // Specification captures use the production popup and the shared harness screenshot path.
    Localization::LanguagePreference previousLanguage{};
    if (g_settings.ui.has_value() && ! g_settings.ui->language.empty() && g_settings.ui->language != L"system")
        previousLanguage = {Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, g_settings.ui->language};
    const auto restoreLanguage = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference(previousLanguage)); });
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, L"fr-FR"})),
                  L"French localization is required for throughput documentation captures.");
    state.Require(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_THROUGHPUT) == L"Débit", L"The throughput disclosure must use the French satellite.");
    auto captureTheme                  = ResolveAppTheme(ThemeMode::Dark, L"throughput-disclosure-capture");
    captureTheme.reducedMotionOverride = true;
    g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(captureTheme);

    task.destinationFolder = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Archives de la famille";
    popup.reset();
    popup.reset(FileOperationsPopup::Create(operations, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
    state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"Could not recreate the throughput popup.");
    if (! popup)
        return false;
    popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWLP_USERDATA));
    if (! popupState)
        return false;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
    redraw();
    state.Require(! snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded && snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputSummaryVisible,
                  L"A recreated popup must start from the saved preference instead of discarded transient task IDs.");

    const auto captureRoot = SelfTest::GetSuiteArtifactPath(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands, L"throughput-disclosure");
    const auto captures    = captureRoot.empty() ? std::filesystem::path{} : captureRoot / std::format(L"repeat-{}", repeatIndex);
    state.Require(! captures.empty() && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(captures), L"Could not create the governed throughput capture directory.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    std::ofstream manifest(captures / L"scenarios.tsv", std::ios::binary);
    manifest << "file\tscenario\tclientWidthDip\tdpi\tlanguage\tfixture\n";
    for (const int widthDip : {480, 760})
    {
        const UINT dpi = GetDpiForWindow(popup.get());
        RECT desired{0, 0, MulDiv(widthDip, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96), MulDiv(480, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)};
        AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(&desired,
                                 static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_STYLE)),
                                 FALSE,
                                 static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_EXSTYLE)),
                                 dpi);
        SetWindowPos(popup.get(), nullptr, 0, 0, desired.right - desired.left, desired.bottom - desired.top, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
        redraw();
        for (const bool expanded : {false, true})
        {
            if (snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded != expanded)
                toggle(task.taskId);
            redraw();
            const std::wstring name = std::format(L"throughput-{}__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", expanded ? L"expanded" : L"collapsed", widthDip, dpi);
            const auto disclosure   = snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputDisclosureRect;
            const RECT labelBounds{static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(disclosure.right)) + MulDiv(4, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96),
                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(disclosure.top)),
                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(disclosure.right)) + MulDiv(100, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96),
                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(disclosure.bottom))};
            std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 2> probes{};
            probes[0].clientBounds          = labelBounds;
            probes[0].expectedColor         = captureTheme.menu.text;
            probes[0].minimumMatchingPixels = 8u;
            probes[1].clientBounds          = labelBounds;
            probes[1].expectedColor         = captureTheme.windowBackground;
            probes[1].minimumMatchingPixels = 100u;
            const HRESULT captured          = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / name, probes);
            Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.LegacyContentPixels",
                              L"throughput label foreground/background",
                              0u,
                              probes[0].matchedPixels,
                              probes[1].matchedPixels,
                              captured);
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(captured),
                          std::format(L"Capture {} must contain legacy text and background: hr=0x{:08X}, foreground={}, background={}.",
                                      name,
                                      static_cast<unsigned long>(captured),
                                      probes[0].matchedPixels,
                                      probes[1].matchedPixels));
            manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(name) << '\t' << (expanded ? "expanded" : "collapsed") << '\t' << widthDip << '\t' << dpi
                     << "\tfr-FR\tproduction-renderer-synthetic-copy\n";
            if (widthDip == 760 && expanded)
            {
                // A readable image must still be rejected when the required semantic witness
                // is absent. This exercises the failure result and retained diagnostic PNG.
                probes[0].expectedColor = RGB(255, 0, 255);
                probes[0].tolerance     = 0u;
                const auto rejectedPath = captures / L"negative-control-missing-color.png";
                const HRESULT rejected  = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), rejectedPath, probes);
                std::error_code captureError;
                state.Require(rejected == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_INVALID_DATA) && probes[0].matchedPixels == 0u &&
                                  std::filesystem::is_regular_file(rejectedPath, captureError) && ! captureError,
                              L"A capture missing its required color witness must fail and retain the diagnostic image.");

                // Exercise the legacy/DComp boundary behind a sibling owned test window.
                // This cover stays inside the popup footprint, never activates or takes input,
                // and is destroyed before the remaining scenarios or any early return.
                RECT popupBounds{};
                state.Require(GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &popupBounds) != FALSE, L"Could not locate the popup for the covered capture.");
                wil::unique_hwnd cover(CreateWindowExW(WS_EX_NOACTIVATE | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW,
                                                       L"STATIC",
                                                       L"",
                                                       WS_POPUP | WS_DISABLED,
                                                       popupBounds.left,
                                                       popupBounds.top,
                                                       popupBounds.right - popupBounds.left,
                                                       popupBounds.bottom - popupBounds.top,
                                                       mainWindow,
                                                       nullptr,
                                                       GetModuleHandleW(nullptr),
                                                       nullptr));
                state.Require(static_cast<bool>(cover), L"Could not create the non-activating capture cover.");
                if (! cover)
                    return false;
                state.Require(SetWindowPos(cover.get(), HWND_TOP, 0, 0, 0, 0, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOACTIVATE | SWP_SHOWWINDOW) != FALSE,
                              L"Could not show the owned capture cover.");
                RedrawWindow(cover.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW);
                probes[0].expectedColor = captureTheme.menu.text;
                probes[0].tolerance     = 12u;
                for (const bool covered : {true, false})
                {
                    if (! covered)
                        cover.reset();
                    redraw();
                    const std::wstring boundaryName =
                        std::format(L"throughput-{}__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", covered ? L"covered" : L"uncovered", widthDip, dpi);
                    const HRESULT boundaryCapture = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / boundaryName, probes);
                    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.CoveredContentPixels",
                                      covered ? L"covered" : L"uncovered",
                                      0u,
                                      probes[0].matchedPixels,
                                      probes[1].matchedPixels,
                                      boundaryCapture);
                    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(boundaryCapture),
                                  std::format(L"Capture {} must retain both legacy witnesses: hr=0x{:08X}, foreground={}, background={}.",
                                              boundaryName,
                                              static_cast<unsigned long>(boundaryCapture),
                                              probes[0].matchedPixels,
                                              probes[1].matchedPixels));
                    manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(boundaryName) << '\t' << (covered ? "covered" : "uncovered") << '\t' << widthDip
                             << '\t' << dpi << "\tfr-FR\tproduction-renderer-synthetic-copy\n";
                }
                for (unsigned int generation = 1u; generation <= 3u; ++generation)
                {
                    const uintptr_t oldContext = popupState->DebugPreparedContextIdentity();
                    state.Require(oldContext != 0u && popupState->DebugHasCurrentPreparedFrame(),
                                  L"Device recovery must start with a prepared frame on the current host context.");
                    state.Require(popupState->DebugSimulateDeviceLoss() && ! popupState->DebugHasCurrentPreparedFrame(),
                                  L"Device loss must detach the host context and invalidate the old prepared frame.");
                    {
                        Debug::Perf::Scope recovery(L"FileOps.Popup.DeviceRecovery.RedrawUs");
                        redraw();
                    }
                    const auto recovered  = snapshot(task.taskId);
                    const bool newContext = popupState->DebugPreparedContextIdentity() != oldContext;
                    state.Require(newContext && popupState->DebugHasCurrentPreparedFrame() && ! recovered.failureSurfaceActive &&
                                      recovered.hostedGraphControlCount == 1u && recovered.taskThroughputDisclosureVisible,
                                  L"Normal painting must replace the old device generation and restore the frame and retained controls.");
                    const std::wstring recoveryName = std::format(L"throughput-device-recovery-{}__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", generation, widthDip, dpi);
                    const HRESULT recoveryCapture   = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / recoveryName, probes);
                    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.DeviceRecovery.ContentPixels",
                                      recoveryName.c_str(),
                                      0u,
                                      probes[0].matchedPixels,
                                      probes[1].matchedPixels,
                                      recoveryCapture);
                    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(recoveryCapture),
                                  L"A recovered device must present readable French text and background through the capture harness.");
                    manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(recoveryName) << "\tdevice-recovery\t" << widthDip << '\t' << dpi
                             << "\tfr-FR\tproduction-renderer-synthetic-copy\n";
                    toggle(task.taskId);
                    state.Require(! snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded, L"The disclosure must still collapse after device recovery.");
                    toggle(task.taskId);
                    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded, L"The disclosure must still expand after device recovery.");
                }
                // Let real timer messages run while hidden. The product still updates
                // taskbar progress, but the hidden client must not submit hosted frames.
                for (unsigned int cycle = 0u; cycle < 3u; ++cycle)
                {
                    ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_HIDE);
                    PumpPendingMessages();
                    const uint64_t hiddenFrames   = popupState->DebugHostedRenderCount();
                    const uintptr_t hiddenContext = popupState->DebugPreparedContextIdentity();
                    const ULONGLONG hiddenStart   = GetTickCount64();
                    while (GetTickCount64() - hiddenStart < 350u)
                    {
                        MsgWaitForMultipleObjectsEx(0, nullptr, 25u, QS_ALLINPUT, MWMO_INPUTAVAILABLE);
                        PumpPendingMessages();
                    }
                    const uint64_t afterHiddenFrames = popupState->DebugHostedRenderCount();
                    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.DeviceRecovery.HiddenFrames",
                                      L"350ms hidden message-pump interval; taskbar timer remains enabled",
                                      (GetTickCount64() - hiddenStart) * 1000u,
                                      hiddenFrames,
                                      afterHiddenFrames,
                                      S_OK);
                    state.Require(afterHiddenFrames == hiddenFrames && popupState->DebugPreparedContextIdentity() == hiddenContext,
                                  L"A hidden popup must not render hosted frames or recreate its prepared context during timer delivery.");
                    ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
                    redraw();
                    state.Require(popupState->DebugHasCurrentPreparedFrame() && popupState->DebugHostedRenderCount() > afterHiddenFrames,
                                  L"Showing the popup again must resume its prepared frame and hosted rendering.");
                }
                const std::wstring shownName = std::format(L"throughput-shown-after-idle__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", widthDip, dpi);
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / shownName, probes)),
                              L"The client must remain readable after repeated hidden intervals.");
                manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(shownName) << "\tshown-after-idle\t" << widthDip << '\t' << dpi
                         << "\tfr-FR\tproduction-renderer-synthetic-copy\n";
            }
        }
    }

    // Deliver a synthetic zero-client resize without minimizing or activating a desktop
    // window. Suspension must not be mistaken for a permanent renderer failure.
    RECT resumedClient{};
    GetClientRect(popup.get(), &resumedClient);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_SIZE, SIZE_MINIMIZED, 0);
    redraw();
    state.Require(! snapshot(task.taskId).failureSurfaceActive, L"A zero-sized host must suspend without entering the failure surface.");
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_SIZE, SIZE_RESTORED, MAKELPARAM(resumedClient.right, resumedClient.bottom));
    redraw();
    state.Require(! snapshot(task.taskId).failureSurfaceActive && snapshot(task.taskId).hostedGraphControlCount == 1u,
                  L"Restoring a nonzero host must resume the prepared background and graph controls.");

    task.discoveryAheadActive     = true;
    task.discoveryClosed          = false;
    task.discoveredTotalBytes     = 8ull * 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
    task.completedBytes           = 2ull * 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
    task.discoveredFileCount      = 117u;
    task.discoveredDirectoryCount = 11u;
    task.completedItems           = 48u;
    task.currentSourcePath =
        L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales très anciennes\\Sélection définitive des photographies originales\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.currentDestinationPath =
        L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Archives familiales très anciennes\\Sélection définitive des photographies originales\\Photographie originale.raw";
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).taskDiscoveryIndicatorVisible, L"The open-discovery capture must render its discovery activity row.");
    const std::wstring renderedDiscoveryProgress = DebugFormatFileOperationsDiscoveryProgress(task);
    const std::wstring renderedDiscoveredSoFar   = DebugFormatFileOperationsDiscoveredSoFar(task);
    state.Require(renderedDiscoveryProgress.find(L"Transférés") != std::wstring::npos && renderedDiscoveryProgress.find(L"éléments") != std::wstring::npos &&
                      renderedDiscoveredSoFar.find(L"Découverts") != std::wstring::npos && renderedDiscoveredSoFar.find(L"éléments") != std::wstring::npos,
                  std::format(L"The open-discovery capture must expose separate French discovery and transfer rows; actual='{}' and '{}'.",
                              renderedDiscoveredSoFar,
                              renderedDiscoveryProgress));
    bool exercisePathKeyboard = false;
    FileOperationsClipboardGeneration clipboardGeneration;
    RECT focusedPathClientBounds{};
    const auto requirePath = [&](std::wstring_view label, std::wstring_view path, std::wstring_view suffix)
    {
        const std::wstring fullText = std::format(L"{} {}", label, path);
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        const bool found = FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(popup.get(), 0, fullText, provider.put()) && provider;
        state.Require(found, std::format(L"A visible path must expose its complete French label and Unicode value: {}.", fullText));
        if (! found)
            return;
        std::wstring help;
        std::wstring id;
        state.Require(TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_HelpTextPropertyId, help) && help == fullText &&
                          TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_AutomationIdPropertyId, id) &&
                          id == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.{}", task.taskId, suffix),
                      L"Path UIA help must retain the exact value and identify the displayed row.");
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
        UiaRect bounds{};
        RECT windowBounds{};
        GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &windowBounds);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment && SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&bounds)) && bounds.width > 0.0 &&
                          bounds.height > 0.0 && bounds.left >= windowBounds.left && bounds.top >= windowBounds.top &&
                          bounds.left + bounds.width <= windowBounds.right && bounds.top + bounds.height <= windowBounds.bottom,
                      L"The full path must belong to a positive visible screen rectangle inside the popup.");
        if (exercisePathKeyboard && fragment && state.failure.empty())
        {
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(fragment->SetFocus()), L"The full path must accept UIA/keyboard focus.");
            wil::unique_variant focusedProperty;
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(provider->GetPropertyValue(UIA_HasKeyboardFocusPropertyId, &focusedProperty)) && focusedProperty.vt == VT_BOOL &&
                              focusedProperty.boolVal == VARIANT_TRUE,
                          L"The focused path must publish acknowledged keyboard focus, not only a host pointer.");
            POINT clientOrigin{};
            ClientToScreen(popup.get(), &clientOrigin);
            focusedPathClientBounds = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.left)) - clientOrigin.x,
                                       static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.top)) - clientOrigin.y,
                                       static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.left + bounds.width)) - clientOrigin.x,
                                       static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.top + bounds.height)) - clientOrigin.y};
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_RETURN, 0);
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_RETURN, 0);
            state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId == id && popupState->DebugFocusedPathTooltip() == fullText,
                          L"Focused path must reveal its exact labelled value without activating a task decision.");
            if (! state.failure.empty())
                return;
            if (! state.Require(WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(popup.get()) && clipboardGeneration.MatchesCurrent(),
                                L"Clipboard changed before path copy; preserve the newer contents without another publication."))
                return;
            const DWORD sequenceBeforeCopy = clipboardGeneration.sequence;
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_CONTROL, 0);
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, 'C', 0);
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, 'C', 0);
            SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_CONTROL, 0);
            if (! state.Require(clipboardGeneration.Record(popup.get(), true) && clipboardGeneration.sequence != sequenceBeforeCopy,
                                L"Path copy must publish a new clipboard generation owned by the popup."))
                return;
            state.Require(ReadClipboardUnicodeText(popup.get()) == path && clipboardGeneration.MatchesCurrent(),
                          L"Ctrl+C must copy the exact Unicode path without its label or ellipsis, and retain its clipboard generation.");
        }
    };
    const auto requireMissingPath = [&](std::wstring_view label, std::wstring_view path)
    {
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        state.Require(! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(popup.get(), 0, std::format(L"{} {}", label, path), provider.put()),
                      L"A replaced or hidden path must leave the current UIA tree.");
    };
    for (const int widthDip : {480, 640, 760, 480})
    {
        const UINT dpi = GetDpiForWindow(popup.get());
        RECT desired{0, 0, MulDiv(widthDip, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96), MulDiv(480, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)};
        AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(&desired,
                                 static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_STYLE)),
                                 FALSE,
                                 static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_EXSTYLE)),
                                 dpi);
        SetWindowPos(popup.get(), nullptr, 0, 0, desired.right - desired.left, desired.bottom - desired.top, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
        redraw();
        const std::wstring name = std::format(L"discovery-open__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", widthDip, dpi);
        for (size_t batch = 0u; batch < 5u; ++batch)
        {
            Debug::Perf::Scope measurement(L"FileOps.Popup.DiscoveryActions.RedrawBatchUs");
            Debug::Perf::Scope footerMeasurement(L"FileOps.Popup.FooterActions.RedrawBatchUs");
            Debug::Perf::Scope pathMeasurement(L"FileOps.Popup.PathAccess.RedrawBatchUs");
            for (size_t pass = 0u; pass < 8u; ++pass)
                RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
        }
        const auto discoveryLayout = snapshot(task.taskId);
        state.Require(discoveryLayout.graphLabelsOutsidePlot && discoveryLayout.graphLabelsFit &&
                          discoveryLayout.graphRateLabelRect.left >= discoveryLayout.graphPlotRect.left &&
                          discoveryLayout.graphEtaLabelRect.right <= discoveryLayout.graphPlotRect.right &&
                          (discoveryLayout.graphRateLabelRect.right <= discoveryLayout.graphEtaLabelRect.left ||
                           discoveryLayout.graphRateLabelRect.bottom <= discoveryLayout.graphEtaLabelRect.top),
                      std::format(L"French speed/ETA labels must fit, remain distinct, and sit entirely above the plot at {} DIP.", widthDip));
        requirePath(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_FROM), task.currentSourcePath, L"Source.0");
        requirePath(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_TO), task.destinationFolder.native(), L"Destination");
        state.Require(discoveryLayout.footerMeasuredActionCount == 2u &&
                          discoveryLayout.footerActionMinimumPaddingPx >= static_cast<float>(MulDiv(24, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)) &&
                          discoveryLayout.footerActionHitTargetsActive && discoveryLayout.footerQueueModeSelectorHitTargetActive &&
                          discoveryLayout.footerOptionsHitTargetActive && ! discoveryLayout.hasVisibleButtonOverlap,
                      std::format(L"French footer actions must fit with 24 DIP padding and keep every target at {} DIP: padding={}px, count={}.",
                                  widthDip,
                                  discoveryLayout.footerActionMinimumPaddingPx,
                                  discoveryLayout.footerMeasuredActionCount));
        state.Require(
            discoveryLayout.taskSkipVisible && discoveryLayout.taskSkipHitTargetActive && discoveryLayout.taskSkipLabelWidthPx > 0.0f &&
                discoveryLayout.taskSkipControlWidthPx >= discoveryLayout.taskSkipLabelWidthPx + static_cast<float>(MulDiv(24, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)) &&
                discoveryLayout.taskCancelHitTargetActive && discoveryLayout.taskSpeedLimitHitTargetActive && ! discoveryLayout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap,
            std::format(L"French discovery actions must fit their live typography and remain reachable at {} DIP: label={}px, control={}px.",
                        widthDip,
                        discoveryLayout.taskSkipLabelWidthPx,
                        discoveryLayout.taskSkipControlWidthPx));
        RECT clientBounds{};
        GetClientRect(popup.get(), &clientBounds);
        std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 4> probes{};
        probes[0].clientBounds          = clientBounds;
        probes[0].expectedColor         = captureTheme.menu.disabledText;
        probes[0].minimumMatchingPixels = 8u;
        probes[1].clientBounds          = clientBounds;
        probes[1].expectedColor         = captureTheme.windowBackground;
        probes[1].minimumMatchingPixels = 100u;
        const std::array labelBounds{discoveryLayout.graphRateLabelRect, discoveryLayout.graphEtaLabelRect};
        for (size_t label = 0u; label < labelBounds.size(); ++label)
        {
            const auto& bounds                       = labelBounds[label];
            probes[label + 2u].clientBounds          = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.left)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.top)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.right)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.bottom))};
            probes[label + 2u].expectedColor         = captureTheme.menu.disabledText;
            probes[label + 2u].minimumMatchingPixels = 8u;
        }
        const HRESULT captured = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / name, probes);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.DiscoveryProgressPixels",
                          L"French discovery progress foreground/background",
                          0u,
                          probes[0].matchedPixels,
                          probes[1].matchedPixels,
                          captured);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.GraphLabelPixels", name.c_str(), 0u, probes[2].matchedPixels, probes[3].matchedPixels, captured);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(captured),
                      std::format(L"Capture {} must contain the French discovery presentation: hr=0x{:08X}, foreground={}, background={}.",
                                  name,
                                  static_cast<unsigned long>(captured),
                                  probes[0].matchedPixels,
                                  probes[1].matchedPixels));
        manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(name) << "\tdiscovery-open\t" << widthDip << '\t' << dpi
                 << "\tfr-FR\tproduction-renderer-synthetic-copy-long-paths\n";
    }

    // Feed long translation-style sentences through the real measurement and paint path.
    // The fixture explicitly labels these as stress text rather than shipping translations.
    popupState->debugGraphLabelText = {
        {L"Vitesse moyenne de transfert actuellement observée : 64,0 Mo par seconde",
         L"Temps restant estimé pour terminer la copie pendant la découverte des fichiers : environ une minute et vingt secondes"}};
    redraw();
    const auto stackedLabels = snapshot(task.taskId);
    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.GraphLabels.WrappedBounds",
                      std::format(L"visibleOverhangPx={} trailingWhitespaceOverhangPx={}",
                                  stackedLabels.graphLabelVisibleOverhangPx,
                                  stackedLabels.graphLabelWhitespaceOverhangPx),
                      0u,
                      0u,
                      0u,
                      stackedLabels.graphLabelsFit ? S_OK : E_FAIL);
    state.Require(stackedLabels.graphLabelsOutsidePlot && stackedLabels.graphLabelsFit &&
                      stackedLabels.graphRateLabelRect.bottom < stackedLabels.graphEtaLabelRect.top,
                  L"Long French graph legends must stack, wrap completely, and remain above a positive plot.");
    std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 2> longLabelProbes{};
    const std::array longLabelBounds{stackedLabels.graphRateLabelRect, stackedLabels.graphEtaLabelRect};
    for (size_t index = 0u; index < longLabelBounds.size(); ++index)
    {
        const auto& bounds                           = longLabelBounds[index];
        longLabelProbes[index].clientBounds          = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.left)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.top)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.right)),
                                                        static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.bottom))};
        longLabelProbes[index].expectedColor         = captureTheme.menu.disabledText;
        longLabelProbes[index].minimumMatchingPixels = 8u;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        const auto& text = popupState->debugGraphLabelText[index];
        const bool found = FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(popup.get(), 0, text, provider.put()) && provider;
        std::wstring help;
        std::wstring id;
        state.Require(found && TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_HelpTextPropertyId, help) && help == text &&
                          TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_AutomationIdPropertyId, id) &&
                          id == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Throughput.{}", task.taskId, index == 0u ? L"Rate" : L"Eta"),
                      L"The visible long legend must expose its complete text and stable identity through UIA.");
    }
    const std::wstring stackedName = std::format(L"graph-labels-stacked__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", GetDpiForWindow(popup.get()));
    const HRESULT stackedCapture   = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / stackedName, longLabelProbes);
    Debug::Perf::Emit(
        L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.GraphLabelPixels", stackedName.c_str(), 0u, longLabelProbes[0].matchedPixels, longLabelProbes[1].matchedPixels, stackedCapture);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(stackedCapture), L"Both stacked French legends must paint inside their measured rectangles.");
    manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(stackedName) << "\tstacked-graph-labels\t480\t" << GetDpiForWindow(popup.get())
             << "\tfr-FR\tsynthetic-long-french-legend\n";
    popupState->debugGraphLabelText = {};
    redraw();

    const HWND foregroundBeforePathKeys = GetForegroundWindow();
    POINT cursorBeforePathKeys{};
    const bool cursorSaved = GetCursorPos(&cursorBeforePathKeys) != FALSE;
    if (! state.Require(WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(popup.get()), L"Wait for a quiet clipboard before retaining every format."))
        return false;
    if (! state.Require(clipboardGeneration.Record(GetClipboardOwner(), false), L"Capture a stable clipboard generation before snapshot."))
        return false;
    wil::com_ptr<IDataObject> clipboardBeforePathKeys;
    const HRESULT clipboardSaved = RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept { return OleGetClipboard(clipboardBeforePathKeys.put()); });
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(clipboardSaved), L"Preserve the existing clipboard before path keyboard tests.");
    if (FAILED(clipboardSaved))
        return false;
    wil::com_ptr<IDataObject> retainedClipboard;
    size_t retainedFormats = 0u;
    std::vector<FileOperationsClipboardFormatBytes> expectedClipboard;
    if (! RetainFileOperationsClipboardFormats(*clipboardBeforePathKeys, retainedClipboard, retainedFormats, state, &expectedClipboard))
        return false;
    if (! state.Require(clipboardGeneration.MatchesCurrent(),
                        L"Clipboard changed during snapshot; preserve the newer contents without starting directed mutation."))
        return false;
    const auto restoreInput = [&]() noexcept
    {
        const auto restoreDesktop = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept
        {
            if (cursorSaved)
                SetCursorPos(cursorBeforePathKeys.x, cursorBeforePathKeys.y);
            if (foregroundBeforePathKeys && IsWindow(foregroundBeforePathKeys))
                SetForegroundWindow(foregroundBeforePathKeys);
        });
        // Do not write if this fixture never published, or a newer generation
        // appeared (including another window in this same process).
        const bool quiet              = WaitForClipboardQuietForSelfTest(popup.get());
        const bool ownershipUnchanged = clipboardGeneration.MatchesCurrent();
        const HRESULT restored        = ! quiet                           ? HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_BUSY)
                                        : ! ownershipUnchanged            ? HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED)
                                        : ! clipboardGeneration.published ? S_FALSE
                                                                          : OleSetClipboard(retainedFormats ? retainedClipboard.get() : nullptr);
        HRESULT flushed               = restored;
        if (restored == S_OK)
        {
            const HWND restoredOwner = GetClipboardOwner();
            const bool current       = ! retainedFormats || OleIsCurrentClipboard(retainedClipboard.get()) == S_OK;
            if (! current || ! clipboardGeneration.Record(restoredOwner, true))
            {
                clipboardGeneration.valid = false;
                flushed                   = HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED);
            }
            else if (retainedFormats)
                flushed = FlushOwnedFileOperationsClipboardForSelfTest(retainedClipboard.get(), clipboardGeneration);
        }
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.RestorePublish", L"contents omitted", 0u, retainedFormats, 0u, restored);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.RestoreFlush", L"contents omitted", 0u, retainedFormats, 0u, flushed);
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(restored) && SUCCEEDED(flushed),
                      std::format(L"Restore clipboard snapshot: set=0x{:08X}, flush=0x{:08X}, formats={}.",
                                  static_cast<unsigned long>(restored),
                                  static_cast<unsigned long>(flushed),
                                  retainedFormats));
        wil::com_ptr<IDataObject> restoredClipboard;
        CaseState restoredState;
        const bool contentsRestored =
            SUCCEEDED(flushed) && SUCCEEDED(RetryFileOperationsClipboardReadForSelfTest([&]() noexcept { return OleGetClipboard(restoredClipboard.put()); })) &&
            restoredClipboard && VerifyFileOperationsClipboardBytes(*restoredClipboard, expectedClipboard, restoredState) &&
            (retainedFormats || CountClipboardFormats() == 0) && clipboardGeneration.MatchesCurrent();
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Clipboard.RestoreReadback",
                          L"exact bytes; contents omitted",
                          0u,
                          retainedFormats,
                          contentsRestored ? expectedClipboard.size() : 0u,
                          contentsRestored ? S_OK : E_FAIL);
        state.Require(contentsRestored,
                      ! ownershipUnchanged            ? L"Clipboard generation changed; preserve current contents instead of overwriting them."
                      : restoredState.failure.empty() ? L"Reacquire restored clipboard for exact byte verification."
                                                      : restoredState.failure);
    };
    auto restorePathInput = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { restoreInput(); });
    state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(popup.get()), L"Path keyboard tests require the existing warning and owned foreground.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    if (! TestFileOperationsClipboardSnapshotMediumContract(state, &clipboardGeneration))
        return false;
    exercisePathKeyboard = true;

    const std::wstring fromLabel     = LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_FROM);
    const std::wstring toLabel       = LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_TO);
    task.discoveryClosed             = true;
    task.discoveryAheadActive        = false;
    task.inFlightFileCount           = 2u;
    task.inFlightFiles[0].sourcePath = task.currentSourcePath;
    task.inFlightFiles[1].sourcePath = L"C:\\Photographies\\Deuxième sélection très longue\\Souvenirs de l'été \U0001F4F7.raw";
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    requirePath(fromLabel, task.inFlightFiles[0].sourcePath, L"Source.0");
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_TAB, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_TAB, 0);
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.Source.1", task.taskId),
                  L"Tab must reach the next visible source path in reading order.");
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_SHIFT, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_TAB, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_TAB, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_SHIFT, 0);
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.Source.0", task.taskId),
                  L"Shift+Tab must return to the previous path.");
    requirePath(fromLabel, task.inFlightFiles[1].sourcePath, L"Source.1");
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
    state.Require(popupState->DebugFocusedPathTooltip().empty() &&
                      snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.Source.1", task.taskId),
                  L"Escape dismisses path detail without losing its focus.");
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYDOWN, VK_RETURN, 0);
    SendMessageW(popup.get(), WM_KEYUP, VK_RETURN, 0);
    redraw();
    // Focus/reveal and the clipboard quiet wait pump layout messages. Use the
    // current focused provider geometry, not its rectangle from before SetFocus.
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> focusedPathProvider;
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> focusedPathFragment;
    UiaRect focusedPathBounds{};
    POINT focusedClientOrigin{};
    const std::wstring focusedPathText = std::format(L"{} {}", fromLabel, task.inFlightFiles[1].sourcePath);
    if (! state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(popup.get(), 0, focusedPathText, focusedPathProvider.put()) && focusedPathProvider &&
                            SUCCEEDED(focusedPathProvider.query_to(focusedPathFragment.put())) && focusedPathFragment &&
                            SUCCEEDED(focusedPathFragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&focusedPathBounds)) && ClientToScreen(popup.get(), &focusedClientOrigin),
                        L"Capture must obtain the current focused path's screen rectangle."))
        return false;
    focusedPathClientBounds            = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(focusedPathBounds.left)) - focusedClientOrigin.x,
                                          static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(focusedPathBounds.top)) - focusedClientOrigin.y,
                                          static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(focusedPathBounds.left + focusedPathBounds.width)) - focusedClientOrigin.x,
                                          static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(focusedPathBounds.top + focusedPathBounds.height)) - focusedClientOrigin.y};
    const std::wstring focusedPathName = std::format(L"path-keyboard-focused__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", GetDpiForWindow(popup.get()));
    const auto focusColor              = MakeAppThemeDxPalette(captureTheme).focusStrokeOuter;
    std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 1> pathFocusProbe{};
    const int focusInset                    = MulDiv(10, static_cast<int>(GetDpiForWindow(popup.get())), 96);
    const int focusOutset                   = MulDiv(4, static_cast<int>(GetDpiForWindow(popup.get())), 96);
    pathFocusProbe[0].clientBounds          = {focusedPathClientBounds.left + focusInset,
                                               focusedPathClientBounds.top - focusOutset,
                                               focusedPathClientBounds.right - focusInset,
                                               focusedPathClientBounds.top};
    pathFocusProbe[0].expectedColor         = ColorToCOLORREF(D2D1::ColorF(focusColor.r, focusColor.g, focusColor.b));
    pathFocusProbe[0].minimumMatchingPixels = 60u;
    const HRESULT focusCapture              = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(popup.get(), captures / focusedPathName, pathFocusProbe);
    Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.PathFocusPixels", focusedPathName.c_str(), 0u, pathFocusProbe[0].matchedPixels, 0u, focusCapture);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(focusCapture),
                  std::format(L"The focused Unicode path must paint its contrasting focus outline: hr=0x{:08X}, probe=({},{},{},{}).",
                              static_cast<unsigned long>(focusCapture),
                              pathFocusProbe[0].clientBounds.left,
                              pathFocusProbe[0].clientBounds.top,
                              pathFocusProbe[0].clientBounds.right,
                              pathFocusProbe[0].clientBounds.bottom));
    manifest << Common::Strings::Utf8FromUtf16ReplacingInvalid(focusedPathName) << "\tpath-keyboard-focused\t480\t" << GetDpiForWindow(popup.get())
             << "\tfr-FR\towned-foreground-keyboard-full-path\n";
    const std::wstring oldPath       = task.inFlightFiles[0].sourcePath;
    task.inFlightFiles[0].sourcePath = L"C:\\Photographies\\Nouvelle sélection définitive\\Fichier suivant.raw";
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    state.Require(snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.Source.1", task.taskId),
                  L"Updating another source row must retain focused path identity.");
    requirePath(fromLabel, task.inFlightFiles[0].sourcePath, L"Source.0");
    requireMissingPath(fromLabel, oldPath);
    requirePath(fromLabel, task.inFlightFiles[1].sourcePath, L"Source.1");
    const std::wstring retiredPath = task.inFlightFiles[1].sourcePath;
    task.inFlightFileCount         = 1u;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    requireMissingPath(fromLabel, retiredPath);
    state.Require(! snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId.empty() &&
                      snapshot(task.taskId).hostedFocusedAutomationId != std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Path.Source.1", task.taskId),
                  L"Removing the focused path must leave a live, safe focus target.");
    task.inFlightFileCount = 0u;
    task.operation         = FILESYSTEM_DELETE;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    requirePath(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_DELETING), task.currentSourcePath, L"Source.0");
    requireMissingPath(toLabel, task.destinationFolder.native());
    task.operation      = FILESYSTEM_COPY;
    task.finished       = true;
    task.lifecyclePhase = static_cast<uint8_t>(FileOperations::TaskLifecyclePhase::Terminal);
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    requirePath(fromLabel, task.currentSourcePath, L"Source.0");
    requirePath(toLabel, task.currentDestinationPath, L"Destination");
    requireMissingPath(toLabel, task.destinationFolder.native());
    task.currentSourcePath.clear();
    task.currentDestinationPath.clear();
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    redraw();
    requireMissingPath(fromLabel, L"");
    requireMissingPath(toLabel, L"");
    restoreInput();
    restorePathInput.release();
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({runningTask}, false, true);
    redraw();
    if (snapshot(runningTask.taskId).taskThroughputExpanded)
        toggle(runningTask.taskId);
    operations->SetPopupThroughputExpanded(false);
    state.Require(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u), L"Remembered disclosure must settle before reopening the popup.");
    RECT beforeReopen{};
    GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &beforeReopen);
    popup.reset();
    popup.reset(FileOperationsPopup::Create(operations, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
    state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"The throughput popup must reopen with its remembered disclosure and placement.");
    if (! popup)
        return false;
    popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWLP_USERDATA));
    state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"The reopened throughput popup must own a live adapter.");
    if (! popupState)
        return false;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({runningTask}, false, true);
    ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
    redraw();
    RECT afterReopen{};
    GetWindowRect(popup.get(), &afterReopen);
    const auto reopened = snapshot(runningTask.taskId);
    state.Require(! reopened.taskThroughputExpanded && reopened.taskThroughputSummaryVisible && reopened.hostedGraphControlCount == 0u &&
                      EqualRect(&beforeReopen, &afterReopen) != FALSE,
                  std::format(L"Reopening the same running task must retain placement and collapsed summary: expanded={}, summary={}, graphs={}, "
                              L"before=({},{},{},{}), after=({},{},{},{}).",
                              reopened.taskThroughputExpanded,
                              reopened.taskThroughputSummaryVisible,
                              reopened.hostedGraphControlCount,
                              beforeReopen.left,
                              beforeReopen.top,
                              beforeReopen.right,
                              beforeReopen.bottom,
                              afterReopen.left,
                              afterReopen.top,
                              afterReopen.right,
                              afterReopen.bottom));
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupResourceLifetime(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperationsPopupInternal;
    auto* operations = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(operations != nullptr, L"File operations state is required for the resource fixture.");
    if (! operations)
        return false;
    const auto previousSettings = g_settings.fileOperations;
    const auto previousTheme    = g_folderWindow.GetTheme();
    auto restore                = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
        g_settings.fileOperations = previousSettings;
        g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(previousTheme);
        static_cast<void>(SettingsHotReload::QueueSettingsSave(L"RedSalamander", g_settings, L"FileOps.Settings.SaveUs", L"resource fixture restore"));
        static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
    });
    operations->SetPopupFooterOnly(false);
    operations->SetPopupCompactDensity(false);
    operations->SetPopupThroughputExpanded(true);
    auto theme                  = previousTheme;
    theme.reducedMotionOverride = true;
    g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(theme);
    static_cast<void>(operations->DebugFlushPendingSettingsSaveForSelfTest(10000u));
    PumpPendingMessages();

    const auto pumpFor = [](ULONGLONG durationMs)
    {
        const ULONGLONG start = GetTickCount64();
        while (GetTickCount64() - start < durationMs)
        {
            MsgWaitForMultipleObjectsEx(0, nullptr, 25u, QS_ALLINPUT, MWMO_INPUTAVAILABLE);
            PumpPendingMessages();
        }
    };
    const auto fileTimeUs = [](FILETIME value) noexcept { return ((static_cast<uint64_t>(value.dwHighDateTime) << 32u) | value.dwLowDateTime) / 10u; };
    const auto sample     = [&](const std::wstring& phase)
    {
        PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX memory{};
        memory.cb     = sizeof(memory);
        DWORD handles = 0u;
        FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, user{};
        state.Require(K32GetProcessMemoryInfo(GetCurrentProcess(), reinterpret_cast<PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS*>(&memory), memory.cb) != FALSE &&
                          GetProcessHandleCount(GetCurrentProcess(), &handles) != FALSE,
                      L"Resource fixture must obtain valid process memory and handle samples.");
        state.Require(GetThreadTimes(GetCurrentThread(), &created, &exited, &kernel, &user) != FALSE, L"Resource fixture must obtain UI-thread CPU samples.");
        const uint64_t threadCpuUs = fileTimeUs(kernel) + fileTimeUs(user);
        state.Require(GetProcessTimes(GetCurrentProcess(), &created, &exited, &kernel, &user) != FALSE, L"Resource fixture must obtain process CPU samples.");
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.Memory", phase.c_str(), 0u, memory.PrivateUsage, memory.WorkingSetSize, S_OK);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.GuiHandles",
                          phase.c_str(),
                          0u,
                          GetGuiResources(GetCurrentProcess(), GR_GDIOBJECTS),
                          GetGuiResources(GetCurrentProcess(), GR_USEROBJECTS),
                          S_OK);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.HandlesAndHosts", phase.c_str(), 0u, handles, DxUi::DebugGetAttachedWindowHostCount(), S_OK);
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.CpuTotals", phase.c_str(), 0u, threadCpuUs, fileTimeUs(kernel) + fileTimeUs(user), S_OK);
    };
    const size_t baselineHosts         = DxUi::DebugGetAttachedWindowHostCount();
    const DWORD ownerThread            = GetCurrentThreadId();
    const uint32_t baselineAttachments = DxUi::DebugGetSharedWindowHostAttachmentCountForThread(ownerThread);
    TaskSnapshot task{};
    task.taskId                 = 90002u;
    task.operation              = FILESYSTEM_COPY;
    task.lifecyclePhase         = static_cast<uint8_t>(FileOperations::TaskLifecyclePhase::Running);
    task.started                = true;
    task.discoveryClosed        = true;
    task.hasProgressCallbacks   = true;
    task.totalBytes             = 8ull * 1024ull * 1024ull * 1024ull;
    task.completedBytes         = task.totalBytes / 2u;
    task.totalItems             = 128u;
    task.completedItems         = 64u;
    task.currentSourcePath      = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales très anciennes\\Photographie originale.raw";
    task.currentDestinationPath = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Archives familiales très anciennes\\Photographie originale.raw";
    auto lifetime               = std::make_shared<int>(0);
    sample(L"cold-before-warmup");
    for (unsigned int cycle = 0u; cycle < 25u; ++cycle)
    {
        const std::wstring label = std::format(L"{}-{}", cycle < 5u ? L"warmup" : L"measured", cycle < 5u ? cycle : cycle - 5u);
        wil::unique_hwnd popup(FileOperationsPopup::Create(operations, &g_folderWindow, mainWindow, lifetime));
        state.Require(static_cast<bool>(popup), L"Resource fixture could not create its owned popup.");
        if (! popup)
            return false;
        auto* popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWLP_USERDATA));
        state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"Resource fixture popup state is unavailable.");
        if (! popupState)
            return false;
        task.lastProgressCallbackTick = GetTickCount64();
        task.operationStartTick       = task.lastProgressCallbackTick - 25000u;
        popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
        const UINT dpi = GetDpiForWindow(popup.get());
        RECT bounds{0, 0, MulDiv(760, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96), MulDiv(480, static_cast<int>(dpi), 96)};
        state.Require(
            AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(&bounds,
                                     static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_STYLE)),
                                     FALSE,
                                     static_cast<DWORD>(GetWindowLongPtrW(popup.get(), GWL_EXSTYLE)),
                                     dpi) != FALSE &&
                SetWindowPos(popup.get(), nullptr, 0, 0, bounds.right - bounds.left, bounds.bottom - bounds.top, SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE) !=
                    FALSE,
            L"The resource fixture must establish its fixed 760 by 480 DIP client.");
        Debug::Perf::Emit(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.FixtureDpi", label.c_str(), 0u, dpi, 0u, S_OK);
        ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
        const auto redraw = [&]
        {
            for (unsigned int frame = 0u; frame < 32u; ++frame)
                RedrawWindow(popup.get(), nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
        };
        sample(label + L"-visible-before");
        {
            Debug::Perf::Scope active(L"FileOps.Popup.Resources.Redraw32Us");
            active.SetDetail(label);
            redraw();
        }
        state.Require(popupState->DebugHasCurrentPreparedFrame(), L"The resource fixture must present a current prepared frame.");
        sample(label + L"-visible-after");
        ShowWindow(popup.get(), SW_HIDE);
        PumpPendingMessages();
