    state.Require(recreatedFooterOnlyLayout.footerOnly, L"Recreated popup should retain the persisted footer-only state.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke expandDetails{};
    expandDetails.kind = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::FooterToggleDetails;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, expandDetails), L"Failed to restore expanded file-operation details.");
    PumpPendingMessages();

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot restoredLayout{};
    restoredLayout.taskId = taskId.value();
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, restoredLayout), L"Failed to capture restored file-operation layout snapshot.");
    state.Require(! restoredLayout.footerOnly, L"Restoring details should clear the footer-only popup state.");
    state.Require(restoredLayout.conflictApplyToAllVisible, L"Restored conflict prompt should show the apply-to-all toggle.");
    RECT restoredExpandedPopupRect{};
    state.Require(GetWindowRect(popup, &restoredExpandedPopupRect) != FALSE, L"Failed to capture expanded popup placement after recreation.");
    constexpr LONG kPlacementTolerancePx = 4;
    state.Require(std::abs(restoredExpandedPopupRect.left - expandedPopupRectBeforeFooterOnly.left) <= kPlacementTolerancePx &&
                      std::abs(restoredExpandedPopupRect.top - expandedPopupRectBeforeFooterOnly.top) <= kPlacementTolerancePx &&
                      std::abs(restoredExpandedPopupRect.right - expandedPopupRectBeforeFooterOnly.right) <= kPlacementTolerancePx &&
                      std::abs(restoredExpandedPopupRect.bottom - expandedPopupRectBeforeFooterOnly.bottom) <= kPlacementTolerancePx,
                  L"Expanding a recreated footer-only popup should restore the previous expanded placement.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }
    Trace(L"FileOpsPopupPresentation: footer-only recreation validation complete");
    return true;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ResolveInitialFileOperationsPopupConflict(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    using ConflictAction = FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task::ConflictAction;
    CaseState& state     = fixture.state;
    const HWND popup     = fixture.popup;
    const auto& taskId   = fixture.taskId;
    const auto& dest     = fixture.dest;

    // The direct Skip action can apply to all similar conflicts; its former overflow placement is stale.
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke applyToAllToggle{};
    applyToAllToggle.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictToggleApplyToAll;
    applyToAllToggle.taskId = taskId.value();
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, applyToAllToggle), L"Failed to toggle the conflict prompt's All-similar checkbox.");

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke skipInvoke{};
    skipInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictMore;
    skipInvoke.taskId = taskId.value();
    skipInvoke.data   = static_cast<uint32_t>(ConflictAction::Skip);
    state.Require(! DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, skipInvoke), L"A stale overflow Skip invocation must be rejected after promotion to primary.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }
    skipInvoke.kind = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictAction;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, skipInvoke), L"Failed to invoke direct Skip with All-similar checked.");

    const auto completionDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool conflictResolved         = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < completionDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot resolvedSnapshot{};
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(popup, taskId.value(), resolvedSnapshot) &&
            (! resolvedSnapshot.conflict.active || resolvedSnapshot.finished))
        {
            conflictResolved = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(conflictResolved, L"Direct Skip with All-similar checked did not resolve the active conflict prompt.");
    state.Require(TextFileEqualsForFileOpsPrompt(dest, "existing payload"), L"Direct Skip must keep the existing destination payload untouched.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot completedSnapshot{};
    const auto finishedDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool taskFinished           = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < finishedDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(popup, taskId.value(), completedSnapshot) && completedSnapshot.finished)
        {
            taskFinished = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }

    state.Require(taskFinished, L"Compact conflict prompt task did not finish after resolving the overflow action.");
    state.Require(completedSnapshot.warningCount > 0 || completedSnapshot.errorCount > 0,
                  L"Skipping a conflict should leave completed-task diagnostics for the collapsed More menu.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ResolveInitialFileOperationsPopupConflictWithKeepBoth(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    using ConflictAction                          = FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task::ConflictAction;
    CaseState& state                              = fixture.state;
    const std::filesystem::path uniqueDestination = fixture.destDir / L"payload (2).txt";
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot beforeLayout{};
    beforeLayout.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, beforeLayout),
                  L"Failed to capture the UIA notification count before Keep Both completion.");

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke keepBothInvoke{};
    keepBothInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictAction;
    keepBothInvoke.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
    keepBothInvoke.data   = static_cast<uint32_t>(ConflictAction::KeepBoth);
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(fixture.popup, keepBothInvoke),
                  L"Failed to invoke Keep Both through the hosted File Operations conflict controls.");

    const auto finishedDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool taskFinished           = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < finishedDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot completed{};
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(fixture.popup, fixture.taskId.value(), completed) && completed.finished)
        {
            taskFinished = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }

    state.Require(taskFinished, L"Keep Both copy did not complete within the bounded deadline.");
    state.Require(TextFileEqualsForFileOpsPrompt(fixture.dest, "existing payload"), L"Keep Both must preserve the original colliding destination.");
    state.Require(TextFileEqualsForFileOpsPrompt(uniqueDestination, "new payload"), L"Keep Both must copy to the first available provider-aware sibling name.");
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot completedLayout{};
    completedLayout.taskId          = fixture.taskId.value();
    const auto notificationDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(2000ms);
    bool completionAnnounced        = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < notificationDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, completedLayout) &&
            completedLayout.hostedAccessibilityNotificationCount > beforeLayout.hostedAccessibilityNotificationCount)
        {
            completionAnnounced = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(completionAnnounced, L"Task completion should raise a hosted UIA notification after the needs-attention announcement.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ValidateFileOperationsCompletedGroupAndNavigation(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    using ConflictAction                                 = FolderWindow::FileOperationState::Task::ConflictAction;
    constexpr uint32_t kCompletedActionExportIssues      = 2u;
    constexpr uint32_t kCompletedActionFailedItems       = 3u;
    constexpr uint32_t kCompletedActionOpenDestination   = 4u;
    constexpr uint32_t kCompletedActionRevealDestination = 5u;
    CaseState& state                                     = fixture.state;
    auto* fileOps                                        = fixture.fileOps;
    const HWND popup                                     = fixture.popup;
    const auto& taskId                                   = fixture.taskId;
    const auto& sourceDir                                = fixture.sourceDir;
    const auto& destDir                                  = fixture.destDir;
    const auto& leftFileSystem                           = fixture.leftFileSystem;

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot completedLayout{};
    completedLayout.taskId          = taskId.value();
    const auto autoCollapseDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(2000ms);
    bool collapseObserved           = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < autoCollapseDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        completedLayout        = {};
        completedLayout.taskId = taskId.value();
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, completedLayout) && completedLayout.taskAutoCollapsedOnCompletion &&
            completedLayout.taskCompactRow && completedLayout.taskCompactProgressVisible && completedLayout.completedAutoCollapsedCount >= 1u)
        {
            collapseObserved = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(collapseObserved, L"Failed to observe the completed file-operation layout after its UI-thread auto-collapse transition.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskAutoCollapsedOnCompletion, L"Completed diagnostic task should auto-collapse when it first finishes.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskCompactRow, L"Auto-collapsed completed diagnostic task should render as a compact row.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskCompactProgressVisible, L"Auto-collapsed completed diagnostic task should keep compact progress visible.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedAutoCollapsedCount >= 1u,
                  std::format(L"Completed auto-collapse count should include the diagnostic task; saw {}.", completedLayout.completedAutoCollapsedCount));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    state.Require(fixture.ExpandTaskIfCollapsed(taskId.value(), L"completed diagnostic"), L"Failed to expand completed diagnostic task before action checks.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    completedLayout                   = {};
    completedLayout.taskId            = taskId.value();
    const auto expandedLayoutDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(2000ms);
    D2D1_RECT_F previousMoreRect{};
    size_t stableExpandedLayoutSamples = 0u;
    bool havePreviousExpandedLayout    = false;
    bool expandedLayoutSettled         = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < expandedLayoutDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot candidate{};
        candidate.taskId = taskId.value();
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, candidate) && ! candidate.taskCompactRow && candidate.completedDiagnosticsMoreButtonRectVisible)
        {
            const D2D1_RECT_F currentMoreRect = candidate.completedDiagnosticsMoreButtonRect;
            const bool sameButtonGeometry     = havePreviousExpandedLayout && std::abs(currentMoreRect.left - previousMoreRect.left) <= 0.01f &&
                                                std::abs(currentMoreRect.top - previousMoreRect.top) <= 0.01f &&
                                                std::abs(currentMoreRect.right - previousMoreRect.right) <= 0.01f &&
                                                std::abs(currentMoreRect.bottom - previousMoreRect.bottom) <= 0.01f;
            stableExpandedLayoutSamples       = sameButtonGeometry ? stableExpandedLayoutSamples + 1u : 0u;
            completedLayout                   = candidate;
            previousMoreRect                  = currentMoreRect;
            havePreviousExpandedLayout        = true;
            if (stableExpandedLayoutSamples >= 3u)
            {
                expandedLayoutSettled = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            stableExpandedLayoutSamples = 0u;
            havePreviousExpandedLayout  = false;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(expandedLayoutSettled, L"Expanded completed file-operation card's More hit target did not settle before activation.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedVisibleActionCount == 3u,
                  std::format(L"Completed diagnostic task must expose Dismiss, Issues and More; saw {}.", completedLayout.completedVisibleActionCount));
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedIssuesVisible && completedLayout.completedIssuesHitTargetActive && completedLayout.completedIssuesLabelFits &&
                      completedLayout.completedActionRowFits,
                  L"Completed diagnostics must expose a complete, measured direct Issues action.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedDismissVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should keep Dismiss as the primary action.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedDiagnosticsMoreVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should expose a More affordance for diagnostics.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedDiagnosticsMoreButtonRectVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should expose the More button rectangle.");
    state.Require(! completedLayout.completedShowLogVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should not expose Show log as a flat button.");
    state.Require(! completedLayout.completedExportIssuesVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should not expose Export issues as a flat button.");
    state.Require(! completedLayout.completedFailedItemsActionVisible, L"Direct Issues replaces the duplicate Failed items overflow entry.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedOpenDestinationActionVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should expose Open destination in the More action set.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.completedRevealDestinationActionVisible, L"Completed diagnostic task should expose Reveal item in the More action set.");
    state.Require(! completedLayout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap, L"Completed diagnostic task rendered overlapping button hit targets.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskStatusKind == FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot::StatusKind::Partial,
                  L"Completed diagnostic task should surface a single partial/needs-attention status.");
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskStatusActiveStateCount == 1u,
                  std::format(L"Completed diagnostic task should have exactly one active status; saw {}.", completedLayout.taskStatusActiveStateCount));
    state.Require(
        completedLayout.globalNeedAttentionCount == 0u,
        std::format(L"Completed diagnostic cards should not keep the live footer attention count active; saw {}.", completedLayout.globalNeedAttentionCount));
    state.Require(completedLayout.taskbarProgressState == static_cast<uint32_t>(TBPF_NOPROGRESS),
                  std::format(L"Completed diagnostic cards should not keep taskbar progress active; saw state {}.", completedLayout.taskbarProgressState));
    state.Require(! completedLayout.footerAggregateProgressVisible,
                  L"Completed diagnostic cards should not keep the live footer aggregate progress bar visible without active work.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    state.Require(DismissVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuForFileOps(popup, SelfTest::Scale(1000ms)),
                  L"Existing DxUI context menu did not close before completed-task More placement validation.");
    const D2D1_RECT_F moreRect = completedLayout.completedDiagnosticsMoreButtonRect;
    const POINT moreClick{
        static_cast<LONG>(std::lround((moreRect.left + moreRect.right) * 0.5f)),
        static_cast<LONG>(std::lround((moreRect.top + moreRect.bottom) * 0.5f)),
    };
    POINT moreTopLeft{static_cast<LONG>(std::lround(moreRect.left)), static_cast<LONG>(std::lround(moreRect.top))};
    POINT moreBottomRight{static_cast<LONG>(std::lround(moreRect.right)), static_cast<LONG>(std::lround(moreRect.bottom))};
    state.Require(ClientToScreen(popup, &moreTopLeft) != FALSE && ClientToScreen(popup, &moreBottomRight) != FALSE,
                  L"Failed to map completed-task More button rectangle to screen coordinates.");
    const RECT moreScreenRect{moreTopLeft.x, moreTopLeft.y, moreBottomRight.x, moreBottomRight.y};

    std::atomic<bool> menuOpened{false};
    std::atomic<bool> menuClosed{false};
    std::atomic<bool> menuDebugReadable{false};
    std::atomic<bool> menuRightAlignedToButton{false};
    std::atomic<bool> menuVerticallyAttachedToButton{false};
    std::atomic<bool> menuObserverFinished{false};
    std::jthread menuObserver([&](std::stop_token stopToken) noexcept
    {
        using namespace std::chrono_literals;
        const auto finishedScope = wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { menuObserverFinished.store(true, std::memory_order_release); });

        const auto openDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        HWND menu               = nullptr;
        while (! stopToken.stop_requested() && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < openDeadline)
        {
            menu = FindVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuWindowForFileOps(popup);
            if (menu && IsWindow(menu) != FALSE)
            {
                menuOpened.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
                DxUi::ContextMenuPopupDebugState menuState{};
                if (DxUi::DebugGetContextMenuPopupState(menu, menuState))
                {
                    menuDebugReadable.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
                    const int tolerancePx = std::max(10, MulDiv(16, static_cast<int>(menuState.dpi), USER_DEFAULT_SCREEN_DPI));
                    menuRightAlignedToButton.store(std::abs(menuState.surfaceRectPx.right - moreScreenRect.right) <= tolerancePx, std::memory_order_release);
                    menuVerticallyAttachedToButton.store(std::abs(menuState.surfaceRectPx.bottom - moreScreenRect.top) <= tolerancePx,
                                                         std::memory_order_release);
                }
                break;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }

        if (menu && IsWindow(menu) != FALSE)
        {
            PostMessageW(menu, WM_KEYDOWN, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
            PostMessageW(menu, WM_KEYUP, VK_ESCAPE, 0);
        }

        const auto closeDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
        while (! stopToken.stop_requested() && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < closeDeadline)
        {
            if (FindVisibleOwnedDxUiContextMenuWindowForFileOps(popup) == nullptr)
            {
                menuClosed.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
                return;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        }
    });

    const LPARAM moreClickLParam = MAKELPARAM(moreClick.x, moreClick.y);
    SendMessageW(popup, WM_MOUSEMOVE, 0, moreClickLParam);
    SendMessageW(popup, WM_LBUTTONDOWN, MK_LBUTTON, moreClickLParam);
    SendMessageW(popup, WM_LBUTTONUP, 0, moreClickLParam);
    // The observer SendMessages UI-thread windows (menu debug state, owner lookups); a blind
    // join here deadlocks it. Keep pumping until the observer finishes on its own deadlines.
    const auto observerDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(8000ms);
    while (! menuObserverFinished.load(std::memory_order_acquire) && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < observerDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
    }
    menuObserver.request_stop();
    menuObserver.join();

    state.Require(menuOpened.load(std::memory_order_acquire), L"Completed-task More button did not open a DxUI context menu.");
    state.Require(menuClosed.load(std::memory_order_acquire), L"Completed-task More context menu did not close after Escape.");
    state.Require(menuDebugReadable.load(std::memory_order_acquire), L"Completed-task More context menu did not expose readable debug state.");
    state.Require(menuRightAlignedToButton.load(std::memory_order_acquire),
                  L"Completed-task More context menu should align to the More button's right edge, not the popup center.");
    state.Require(menuVerticallyAttachedToButton.load(std::memory_order_acquire),
                  L"Completed-task More context menu should open directly above the More button, not over the active progress card.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke completedMoreInvoke{};
    completedMoreInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskCompletedMore;
    completedMoreInvoke.taskId = taskId.value();
    completedMoreInvoke.data   = kCompletedActionExportIssues;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, completedMoreInvoke), L"Failed to invoke completed-task Export issues through the More affordance.");
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke failedItemsInvoke{};
    failedItemsInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskCompletedMore;
    failedItemsInvoke.taskId = taskId.value();
    failedItemsInvoke.data   = kCompletedActionFailedItems;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, failedItemsInvoke), L"Failed to invoke completed-task Failed items through the More affordance.");

    const auto issuesPaneDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
    bool issuesPaneVisible        = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < issuesPaneDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        if (g_folderWindow.IsFileOperationsIssuesPaneVisible())
        {
            issuesPaneVisible = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(issuesPaneVisible, L"Completed-task Failed items action did not open the Failed Items pane.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const std::filesystem::path aggregateConflictSource = sourceDir / L"aggregate-conflict.txt";
    const std::filesystem::path aggregateConflictDest   = destDir / aggregateConflictSource.filename();
    state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(aggregateConflictSource, "new aggregate payload"), L"Failed to seed aggregate live-summary conflict source file.");
    state.Require(SelfTest::WriteTextFile(aggregateConflictDest, "existing aggregate payload"),
                  L"Failed to seed aggregate live-summary conflict destination file.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    uint64_t aggregateConflictTaskId       = 0;
    const HRESULT aggregateConflictStartHr = fileOps->AdmitOperation(FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                                                     FolderWindow::Pane::Left,
                                                                     FolderWindow::Pane::Right,
                                                                     leftFileSystem,
                                                                     {aggregateConflictSource},
                                                                     destDir,
                                                                     FILESYSTEM_FLAG_NONE,
                                                                     false,
                                                                     0,
                                                                     FolderWindow::FileOperationState::ExecutionMode::PerItem,
                                                                     false,
                                                                     fixture.rightFileSystem,
                                                                     &aggregateConflictTaskId);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(aggregateConflictStartHr),
                  std::format(L"Failed to start aggregate live-summary conflict copy (hr=0x{:08X}).", static_cast<unsigned long>(aggregateConflictStartHr)));
    state.Require(aggregateConflictTaskId != 0, L"Aggregate live-summary conflict copy did not return a task id.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto aggregateConflictDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool aggregateConflictActive         = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < aggregateConflictDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot aggregateConflictSnapshot{};
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(popup, aggregateConflictTaskId, aggregateConflictSnapshot) && aggregateConflictSnapshot.conflict.active)
        {
            aggregateConflictActive = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(aggregateConflictActive, L"Aggregate live-summary conflict copy did not reach an active conflict prompt.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot aggregateConflictLayout{};
    aggregateConflictLayout.taskId = aggregateConflictTaskId;
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, aggregateConflictLayout),
                  L"Failed to capture aggregate live-summary conflict layout snapshot.");
    state.Require(aggregateConflictLayout.globalNeedAttentionCount == 1u,
                  std::format(L"Live footer attention count should include only the active conflict, not completed diagnostic cards; saw {}.",
                              aggregateConflictLayout.globalNeedAttentionCount));
    state.Require(aggregateConflictLayout.taskbarProgressState == static_cast<uint32_t>(TBPF_ERROR),
                  std::format(L"Active conflict should keep taskbar state at error without inherited completed totals; saw state {}.",
                              aggregateConflictLayout.taskbarProgressState));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke aggregateSkipInvoke{};
    aggregateSkipInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictAction;
    aggregateSkipInvoke.taskId = aggregateConflictTaskId;
    aggregateSkipInvoke.data   = static_cast<uint32_t>(ConflictAction::Skip);
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, aggregateSkipInvoke), L"Failed to resolve aggregate live-summary conflict with Skip.");

    const auto aggregateFinishedDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool aggregateConflictFinished       = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < aggregateFinishedDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot aggregateFinishedSnapshot{};
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(popup, aggregateConflictTaskId, aggregateFinishedSnapshot) && aggregateFinishedSnapshot.finished)
        {
            aggregateConflictFinished = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(aggregateConflictFinished, L"Aggregate live-summary conflict task did not finish after Skip.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    // Reuse the fixture source that was present before the pane enumeration. Creating a new
    // source and immediately starting discovery makes this UI contract depend on watcher
    // publication timing instead of the completed-card behavior it is intended to validate.
    const std::filesystem::path successSource = fixture.source;
    const std::filesystem::path successDest   = fixture.dest;
    std::error_code successCleanupEc;
    std::filesystem::remove(successDest, successCleanupEc);
    state.Require(! successCleanupEc, L"Failed to remove the completed destination action target before the successful copy.");
    std::error_code successSourceEc;
    state.Require(std::filesystem::is_regular_file(successSource, successSourceEc) && ! successSourceEc,
                  L"Completed destination action source file is unavailable.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    std::atomic<uint64_t> completedDestinationActionTaskId{0};
    const uint64_t completedDestinationActionCallbackToken =
        g_folderWindow.AddFileOperationCompletedCallback([&](const FolderWindow::FileOperationCompletedEvent& event) noexcept
    {
        if (event.operation == FILESYSTEM_COPY && event.sourcePaths.size() == 1u && OrdinalString::EqualsNoCasePath(event.sourcePaths.front(), successSource))
        {
            completedDestinationActionTaskId.store(event.taskId, std::memory_order_release);
        }
    });
    const auto removeCompletedDestinationActionCallback =
        wil::scope_exit([&]() noexcept { g_folderWindow.RemoveFileOperationCompletedCallback(completedDestinationActionCallbackToken); });

    const HRESULT successStartHr = fileOps->AdmitOperation(FILESYSTEM_COPY,
                                                           FolderWindow::Pane::Left,
                                                           FolderWindow::Pane::Right,
                                                           leftFileSystem,
                                                           {successSource},
                                                           destDir,
                                                           FILESYSTEM_FLAG_NONE,
                                                           false,
                                                           0,
                                                           FolderWindow::FileOperationState::ExecutionMode::PerItem,
                                                           false,
                                                           fixture.rightFileSystem);
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(successStartHr),
                  std::format(L"Failed to start completed destination action copy (hr=0x{:08X}).", static_cast<unsigned long>(successStartHr)));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    const auto successTaskIdDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    uint64_t successTaskId           = 0;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < successTaskIdDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        successTaskId = completedDestinationActionTaskId.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (successTaskId != 0)
        {
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(successTaskId != 0, L"Failed to identify the completed destination action copy task.");
    if (successTaskId == 0)
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::TaskSnapshot successCompletedSnapshot{};
    const auto successFinishedDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(5000ms);
    bool successTaskFinished           = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < successFinishedDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupTaskSnapshot(popup, successTaskId, successCompletedSnapshot) && successCompletedSnapshot.finished)
        {
            successTaskFinished = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(successTaskFinished, L"Completed destination action copy did not finish.");
    state.Require(successCompletedSnapshot.warningCount == 0 && successCompletedSnapshot.errorCount == 0,
                  std::format(L"Completed destination action copy should finish without diagnostics "
                              L"(hr=0x{:08X}, warnings={}, errors={}, message='{}').",
                              static_cast<unsigned long>(successCompletedSnapshot.resultHr),
                              successCompletedSnapshot.warningCount,
                              successCompletedSnapshot.errorCount,
                              successCompletedSnapshot.lastDiagnosticMessage));
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot successLayout{};
    const auto successCollapseDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(2000ms);
    bool successCollapseObserved       = false;
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < successCollapseDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        successLayout        = {};
        successLayout.taskId = successTaskId;
        if (DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, successLayout) && successLayout.taskAutoCollapsedOnCompletion && successLayout.taskCompactRow &&
            successLayout.taskCompactProgressVisible)
        {
            successCollapseObserved = true;
            break;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(successCollapseObserved, L"Failed to observe the completed success task after its UI-thread auto-collapse transition.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupVisible, L"Multiple completed file-operation cards should show a Completed (N) group.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupExpanded, L"Completed group should start expanded so completed-card actions remain discoverable.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupAnimationEnabled, L"Completed group should use bounded expand/collapse motion when reduced motion is off.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupCount == 3u,
                  std::format(L"Completed group should contain exactly the three case-owned file-operation cards; saw {}.", successLayout.completedGroupCount));
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupCompletedCount + successLayout.completedGroupPartialCount + successLayout.completedGroupFailedCount +
                          successLayout.completedGroupCanceledCount ==
                      successLayout.completedGroupCount,
                  L"Completed-group result badges should classify every grouped operation exactly once.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupVisibleTaskCount == successLayout.completedGroupCount,
                  std::format(L"Expanded completed group should show all grouped tasks; visible={}, total={}.",
                              successLayout.completedGroupVisibleTaskCount,
                              successLayout.completedGroupCount));
    state.Require(successLayout.taskAutoCollapsedOnCompletion, L"Completed success task should auto-collapse when it first finishes.");
    state.Require(successLayout.taskCompactRow, L"Auto-collapsed completed success task should render as a compact row.");
    state.Require(successLayout.taskCompactProgressVisible, L"Auto-collapsed completed success task should keep compact progress visible.");

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke collapseCompletedGroup{};
    collapseCompletedGroup.kind = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::CompletedGroupToggle;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, collapseCompletedGroup), L"Failed to collapse the Completed (N) group.");
    // Assert the first collapsed frame, while its height may still be animating.
    // Pumping until the animation completes would miss stale child controls.
    RedrawWindow(popup, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot collapsedGroupLayout{};
    collapsedGroupLayout.taskId = successTaskId;
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, collapsedGroupLayout), L"Failed to capture collapsed completed-group layout.");
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupVisible, L"Completed group should remain visible after collapse.");
    state.Require(! collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupExpanded, L"Completed group should report collapsed after its group toggle.");
    state.Require(
        collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupVisibleTaskCount == 0u,
        std::format(L"Collapsed completed group should hide grouped task rows; saw {} visible.", collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupVisibleTaskCount));
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupCompletedCount == successLayout.completedGroupCompletedCount &&
                      collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupPartialCount == successLayout.completedGroupPartialCount &&
                      collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupFailedCount == successLayout.completedGroupFailedCount &&
                      collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupCanceledCount == successLayout.completedGroupCanceledCount,
                  L"Collapsed completed group should preserve Completed, Partial, Failed, and Canceled badge counts.");
    const size_t expectedBadgeTooltipCount = static_cast<size_t>(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupCompletedCount != 0u) +
                                             static_cast<size_t>(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupPartialCount != 0u) +
                                             static_cast<size_t>(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupFailedCount != 0u) +
                                             static_cast<size_t>(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupCanceledCount != 0u);
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.hostedTooltipRegionCount == expectedBadgeTooltipCount,
                  std::format(L"Collapsed result badges should expose one tooltip region per nonzero category; expected {}, saw {}.",
                              expectedBadgeTooltipCount,
                              collapsedGroupLayout.hostedTooltipRegionCount));
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupBadgeTooltipsInformative,
                  L"Collapsed result badge tooltips should explain the category instead of repeating the visible count.");
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.completedGroupClearTooltipEmpty, L"Clear completed should not expose a tooltip that merely repeats its visible label.");
    state.Require(collapsedGroupLayout.taskHiddenByCompletedGroup, L"Selected completed task should report hidden while the group is collapsed.");

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke expandCompletedGroup{};
    expandCompletedGroup.kind = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::CompletedGroupToggle;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, expandCompletedGroup), L"Failed to expand the Completed (N) group.");
    PumpPendingMessages();
    successLayout        = {};
    successLayout.taskId = successTaskId;
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, successLayout), L"Failed to capture re-expanded completed-group layout.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedGroupExpanded, L"Completed group should report expanded after the second group toggle.");
    state.Require(! successLayout.taskHiddenByCompletedGroup, L"Selected completed task should be visible again after expanding the group.");
    state.Require(fixture.ExpandTaskIfCollapsed(successTaskId, L"completed success"),
                  L"Failed to expand completed success task before destination action checks.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    successLayout        = {};
    successLayout.taskId = successTaskId;
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(popup, successLayout), L"Failed to capture expanded completed destination action layout snapshot.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedVisibleActionCount <= 2u,
                  std::format(L"Completed success task should expose at most 2 actions; saw {}.", successLayout.completedVisibleActionCount));
    state.Require(successLayout.completedDismissVisible, L"Completed success task should keep Dismiss as the primary action.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedDiagnosticsMoreVisible, L"Completed success task should expose a More affordance for destination actions.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedOpenDestinationActionVisible, L"Completed success task should expose Open destination in the More action set.");
    state.Require(successLayout.completedRevealDestinationActionVisible, L"Completed success task should expose Reveal item in the More action set.");
    state.Require(! successLayout.completedFailedItemsActionVisible, L"Completed success task should not expose Failed items without diagnostics.");
    state.Require(! successLayout.completedShowLogVisible, L"Completed success task should not expose Show log as a flat button.");
    state.Require(! successLayout.completedExportIssuesVisible, L"Completed success task should not expose Export issues as a flat button.");
    state.Require(successLayout.taskStatusVisualTone == static_cast<uint32_t>(FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupStatusVisualTone::Ok),
                  std::format(L"Completed success task should map to Ok tone; saw {}.", successLayout.taskStatusVisualTone));
    state.Require(successLayout.taskStatusVisualColorRef == ColorToCOLORREF(g_folderWindow.GetTheme().fileOperations.successText),
                  L"Completed success task should resolve the Ok status color through fileOperations.successText.");
    state.Require(successLayout.taskStatusVisualColorRef != ColorToCOLORREF(g_folderWindow.GetTheme().accent),
                  L"Completed success task should not reuse the active accent color for the Ok status.");
    state.Require(! successLayout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap, L"Completed success task rendered overlapping button hit targets.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
    {
        return false;
    }

    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(g_folderWindow.SetFileSystemPluginForPane(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, kBuiltinDummyFileSystemIdForFileOpsPrompt)),
                  L"Failed to switch the destination pane away from the completed task provider.");
    state.Require(NavigationLocation::EqualsNoCase(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Right), kBuiltinDummyFileSystemIdForFileOpsPrompt),
                  L"Destination pane did not switch to the dummy provider before completed-action validation.");
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderHistory({});

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke openDestinationInvoke{};
    openDestinationInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskCompletedMore;
    openDestinationInvoke.taskId = successTaskId;
    openDestinationInvoke.data   = kCompletedActionOpenDestination;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, openDestinationInvoke),
                  L"Failed to invoke completed-task Open destination through the More affordance.");
    state.Require(NavigationLocation::EqualsNoCase(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Right), L"builtin/file-system"),
                  L"Open destination should restore the provider captured by the completed task.");
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Open destination did not navigate the destination pane to the completed task folder.");
    state.Require(g_folderWindow.GetFolderHistory().size() == 1u,
                  L"Open destination should perform one qualified navigation without an intermediate provider-root visit.");

    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupSelfTestInvoke revealDestinationInvoke{};
    revealDestinationInvoke.kind   = FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskCompletedMore;
    revealDestinationInvoke.taskId = successTaskId;
    revealDestinationInvoke.data   = kCompletedActionRevealDestination;
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(popup, revealDestinationInvoke), L"Failed to invoke completed-task Reveal item through the More affordance.");
    state.Require(fixture.WaitForFocusedPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, successDest, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Reveal item did not focus the copied destination item in the destination pane.");

    const HRESULT rejectedLocationHr = g_folderWindow.ExecuteInPaneLocation(
        FolderWindow::Pane::Right, kBuiltinDummyFileSystemIdForFileOpsPrompt, L"invalid-short-id", {}, std::filesystem::path(L"/"), {}, 0u, false);
    state.Require(FAILED(rejectedLocationHr), L"Completed-action navigation should reject a mismatched provider identity.");
    state.Require(NavigationLocation::EqualsNoCase(g_folderWindow.GetFileSystemPluginId(FolderWindow::Pane::Right), L"builtin/file-system"),
                  L"Rejected completed-action navigation should restore the pane provider.");
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, destDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Rejected completed-action navigation should restore the pane path.");

    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupConflictAndMetadata(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    FileOperationsPopupTestFixture fixture(state);
    if (InitializeFileOperationsPopupTestFixture(mainWindow, fixture, true) && ValidateFileOperationsConflictAndMetadata(fixture))
    {
        static_cast<void>(ResolveInitialFileOperationsPopupConflictWithKeepBoth(fixture));
    }
    state.Require(fixture.Cleanup(), L"Conflict-metadata popup test did not clean every case-owned task and completion.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsConflictPathProfiles(CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperations;
    TransferPlan local;
    local.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity      = FileSystemPathIdentity::OrdinalIgnoreCaseForLocalFileSystem();
    local.destinationEndpoint.pathIdentity = local.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity;
    local.selectedItems.resize(1u);
    TransferPlan remote                                    = local;
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity->acceptedSeparators = L"/";
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity->preferredSeparator = L'/';
    FileOperationPlanGroup plans{local, remote};
    const auto first = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"C:\\Photos\\été.raw", L"D:\\Sauvegarde\\été.raw");
    state.Require(first.sourceName == L"été.raw" && first.sourceParentPath == L"C:\\Photos" && first.destinationParentPath == L"D:\\Sauvegarde",
                  L"A local admitted item must expose filename and parent display facts.");
    // The backslash is part of this provider's filename, not a path separator.
    const auto second = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 1u, L"/photos/été\\original.raw", L"D:\\Sauvegarde\\copie.raw");
    state.Require(second.sourceName == L"été\\original.raw" && second.sourceParentPath == L"/photos" && second.destinationName == L"copie.raw" &&
                      second.destinationParentPath == L"D:\\Sauvegarde",
                  L"Each group item and each endpoint must use its own admitted separator profile.");
    const auto noParts = [](const ConflictPathDisplay& value)
    { return value.sourceName.empty() && value.sourceParentPath.empty() && value.destinationName.empty() && value.destinationParentPath.empty(); };
    state.Require(noParts(ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, std::nullopt, L"/photos/file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw")) &&
                      noParts(ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 2u, L"/photos/file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw")),
                  L"Missing/out-of-range item identity must keep the exact-path fallback.");
    state.Require(noParts(ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"C:\\", L"\\\\server\\share")),
                  L"Namespace roots must not be invented as filename/parent pairs.");
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity.reset();
    remote.destinationEndpoint.pathIdentity->pathTextStableIdentity = false;
    state.Require(noParts(ResolveConflictPathDisplay(FileOperationPlanGroup{remote}, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"/photos/file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw")),
                  L"Unavailable/unadmitted path profiles must not be guessed.");
    DeletePlan deletion;
    deletion.endpoint = local.sourceEndpoint;
    deletion.selectedItems.resize(1u);
    const auto deleted = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(FileOperationPlanGroup{deletion}, FILESYSTEM_DELETE, 0u, L"C:\\Photos\\été.raw", L"D:\\unused.raw");
    state.Require(deleted.sourceName == L"été.raw" && deleted.sourceParentPath == L"C:\\Photos" && deleted.destinationName.empty(),
                  L"A deleting context has only its qualified source location.");
    state.Require(noParts(ResolveConflictPathDisplay(FileOperationPlanGroup{deletion}, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"C:\\file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw")),
                  L"A mismatched operation/plan must not publish display facts.");
    const auto nested =
        ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_MOVE, 1u, L"/photos/sous-dossier/été\\original.raw", L"D:\\Sauvegarde\\sous-dossier\\copie.raw");
    state.Require(nested.sourceParentPath == L"/photos/sous-dossier" && nested.destinationParentPath == L"D:\\Sauvegarde\\sous-dossier",
                  L"Nested collisions must retain the selected item's provider profile and exact nested location.");
    state.Require(! first.inspection && ! second.inspection && ! deleted.inspection,
                  L"Display parsing alone must not invent an admitted provider identity for inspection.");
    const auto qualify = [](QualifiedEndpoint& endpoint, std::wstring_view plugin, std::wstring_view instance, std::wstring_view root)
    {
        endpoint.pluginId               = plugin;
        endpoint.instanceId             = instance;
        endpoint.profileId              = L"test/profile";
        endpoint.rootId                 = root;
        endpoint.cancellationRouteClass = CancellationRouteClass::Bounded;
    };
    qualify(local.sourceEndpoint, L"builtin/file-system", L"default", L"volume/source");
    qualify(local.destinationEndpoint, L"builtin/file-system", L"destination-session", L"volume/destination");
    remote = local;
    qualify(remote.sourceEndpoint, L"test/remote", L"remote-session", L"remote-root");
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity->acceptedSeparators  = L"/";
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity->preferredSeparator  = L'/';
    remote.sourceEndpoint.pathIdentity->componentComparison = FileSystemPathComponentComparison::OrdinalCaseSensitive;
    plans                                                   = {local, remote};
    const auto qualified = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_MOVE, 1u, L"/photos/sous-dossier/été\\original.raw", L"D:\\Sauvegarde\\copie.raw");
    state.Require(qualified.inspection && qualified.inspection->source && qualified.inspection->destination,
                  L"Both qualified endpoints must publish immutable inspection locations.");
    if (qualified.inspection && qualified.inspection->source && qualified.inspection->destination)
    {
        const auto& context  = *qualified.inspection;
        const auto& location = context.source.value();
        state.Require(context.operation == FILESYSTEM_MOVE && context.sourceIndex == 1u && location.endpoint.pluginId == L"test/remote" &&
                          location.endpoint.instanceId == L"remote-session" && location.endpoint.profileId == L"test/profile" &&
                          location.endpoint.rootId == L"remote-root" &&
                          location.endpoint.pathIdentity->componentComparison == FileSystemPathComponentComparison::OrdinalCaseSensitive &&
                          location.providerPath == L"/photos/sous-dossier/été\\original.raw" && location.parentPath == L"/photos/sous-dossier" &&
                          location.itemName == L"été\\original.raw" && context.destination->endpoint.instanceId == L"destination-session",
                      L"Inspection must retain the selected partition, each role's identity and the nested provider spelling.");
        const auto repeated = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_MOVE, 1u, location.providerPath, context.destination->providerPath);
        state.Require(repeated.inspection && repeated.inspection != qualified.inspection,
                      L"A replacement prompt with identical paths must receive a different retained generation token.");
        remote.sourceEndpoint.instanceId = L"changed-session";
        state.Require(location.endpoint.instanceId == L"remote-session", L"Changing an input plan must not mutate an already published inspection context.");
    }
    remote.destinationEndpoint.profileId.clear();
    const auto sourceOnly = ResolveConflictPathDisplay(FileOperationPlanGroup{remote}, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"/photos/file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw");
    state.Require(sourceOnly.inspection && sourceOnly.inspection->source && ! sourceOnly.inspection->destination,
                  L"A missing destination identity must not invalidate an independently qualified source or invent destination qualification.");
    deletion.endpoint = local.sourceEndpoint;
    const auto deleteInspection =
        ResolveConflictPathDisplay(FileOperationPlanGroup{deletion}, FILESYSTEM_DELETE, 0u, L"C:\\Photos\\été.raw", L"D:\\unused.raw");
    state.Require(deleteInspection.inspection && deleteInspection.inspection->source && ! deleteInspection.inspection->destination,
                  L"Delete inspection must never expose a destination role.");
    state.Require(! ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, std::nullopt, L"C:\\file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw").inspection &&
                      ! ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 2u, L"C:\\file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw").inspection &&
                      ! ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_COPY, 0u, L"C:\\", L"D:\\").inspection &&
                      ! ResolveConflictPathDisplay(plans, FILESYSTEM_RENAME, 0u, L"C:\\file.raw", L"D:\\file.raw").inspection,
                  L"Task-wide, out-of-range, root-only and unmapped rename prompts must not publish inspection targets.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsPopupConflictComparisonRows(HWND mainWindow, CaseState& state, uint32_t repeatIndex) noexcept
{
    using namespace FileOperationsPopupInternal;
    // The application has no frame sink on this UI thread. Borrow the library's existing
    // synchronous stage telemetry for this fixture, then remove it before stack teardown.
    std::array<uint64_t, 4> frameMetricCounts{};
    DxUi::SetFrameMetricSink(
        [](void* context, std::wstring_view metric, uint64_t durationUs) noexcept
    {
        auto& counts = *static_cast<std::array<uint64_t, 4>*>(context);
        constexpr std::array<std::wstring_view, 4> names{{L"dxui.frame.total_us", L"dxui.frame.update_us", L"dxui.frame.render_us", L"dxui.frame.present_us"}};
        for (size_t index = 0u; index < names.size(); ++index)
        {
            if (metric == names[index])
                ++counts[index];
        }
        Debug::Perf::EmitDurationUs(metric, durationUs);
    },
        &frameMetricCounts);
    const auto restoreFrameMetrics = wil::scope_exit([]() noexcept { DxUi::SetFrameMetricSink(nullptr, nullptr); });
    Localization::LanguagePreference previousLanguage{};
    if (g_settings.ui.has_value() && ! g_settings.ui->language.empty() && g_settings.ui->language != L"system")
        previousLanguage = {Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, g_settings.ui->language};
    const auto restoreLanguage = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference(previousLanguage)); });
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, L"fr-FR"})),
                  L"Conflict comparison requires the French satellite.");
    const auto previousUi = g_settings.ui;
    const auto restoreUi  = wil::scope_exit([&] { g_settings.ui = previousUi; });
    if (! g_settings.ui)
        g_settings.ui.emplace();
    g_settings.ui->reducedMotion = Common::Settings::ReducedMotionMode::On;
    FileOperationsPopupTestFixture fixture(state);
    if (! InitializeFileOperationsPopupTestFixture(mainWindow, fixture, true))
        return false;
    auto theme                  = ResolveAppTheme(ThemeMode::Dark, L"conflict-comparison-capture");
    theme.reducedMotionOverride = true;
    g_folderWindow.ApplyTheme(theme);
    auto* popupState = reinterpret_cast<FileOperationsPopupState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(fixture.popup, GWLP_USERDATA));
    state.Require(popupState != nullptr, L"Conflict comparison popup state is unavailable.");
    if (! popupState)
        return false;
    // ApplyTheme posts to the popup. Await the actual hosted policy before relying on
    // non-animated controls; blocked capture waits cannot advance a stale focus animation.
    const auto themeReady = RedSalamander::TestSupport::PumpMessagesUntil(
        [&]() noexcept
    {
        PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot snapshot;
        snapshot.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
        return DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, snapshot) && snapshot.hostedReducedMotion;
    },
        {.timeout = SelfTest::Scale(std::chrono::milliseconds(2000)), .operationName = L"hosted reduced-motion theme delivery"});
    state.Require(themeReady.conditionMet, themeReady.timeoutDiagnostic);
    if (! themeReady.conditionMet)
        return false;
    auto task = fixture.taskSnapshot;
    state.Require(task.conflict.sourceName == std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.sourcePath).filename().wstring() &&
                      task.conflict.sourceParentPath == std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.sourcePath).parent_path().wstring() &&
                      task.conflict.destinationName == std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.destinationPath).filename().wstring(),
                  L"The real admitted conflict must publish provider-qualified filename/parent facts before visual decoration.");
    task.conflict.sourcePath      = L"C:\\Photographies\\Archives familiales de plusieurs générations\\Photographie originale de la réunion familiale 📷.raw";
    task.conflict.destinationPath = L"D:\\Sauvegardes\\Collection de photographies déjà présentes\\Photographie déjà présente de la réunion familiale 📷.raw";
    // Synthetic local paths replace both raw and precomputed display facts.
    task.conflict.inspection.reset(); // Decorative paths are not live admitted navigation targets.
    task.conflict.sourceName                    = std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.sourcePath).filename().wstring();
    task.conflict.sourceParentPath              = std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.sourcePath).parent_path().wstring();
    task.conflict.destinationName               = std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.destinationPath).filename().wstring();
    task.conflict.destinationParentPath         = std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.destinationPath).parent_path().wstring();
    task.conflict.sourceMetadata.sizeBytes      = 9876543210123456ull;
    task.conflict.destinationMetadata.sizeBytes = 1234567890123456ull;
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    const auto captureRoot = SelfTest::GetSuiteArtifactPath(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands, L"conflict-comparison");
    const auto captures    = captureRoot.empty() ? std::filesystem::path{} : captureRoot / std::format(L"repeat-{}", repeatIndex);
    state.Require(! captures.empty() && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(captures), L"Conflict capture directory is unavailable.");
    const auto metadataText = [](const auto& metadata)
    { return FormatBytesCompact(metadata.sizeBytes) + L" \u2022 " + DebugFormatFileOperationsConflictTimestamp(metadata.lastWriteTime); };
    const std::array metadataNames{metadataText(task.conflict.sourceMetadata), metadataText(task.conflict.destinationMetadata)};
    const auto redraw = [&] { RedrawWindow(fixture.popup, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW); };
    // Keep each width on the same physical display. Retaining the old top-left near
    // a monitor boundary can trigger WM_DPICHANGED repeatedly during automatic sizing.
    for (size_t warmup = 0u; warmup < 4u; ++warmup)
        redraw();
    const HMONITOR captureMonitor = MonitorFromWindow(fixture.popup, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
    MONITORINFOEXW captureMonitorInfo{};
    captureMonitorInfo.cbSize = sizeof(captureMonitorInfo);
    state.Require(GetMonitorInfoW(captureMonitor, &captureMonitorInfo) != FALSE, L"The capture display work area must be available.");
    const UINT captureDpi               = GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup);
    const auto differentDestinationPath = task.conflict.destinationPath;
    const auto differentDestinationName = task.conflict.destinationName;
    for (const bool sameName : {true, false})
    {
        task.conflict.destinationName = sameName ? task.conflict.sourceName : differentDestinationName;
        task.conflict.destinationPath =
            sameName ? (std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.destinationParentPath) / task.conflict.sourceName).wstring() : differentDestinationPath;
        popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
        for (const int widthDip : {480, 640, 760})
        {
            const UINT dpi = GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup);
            state.Require(SetWindowPos(fixture.popup,
                                       nullptr,
                                       captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.left + MulDiv(16, static_cast<int>(captureDpi), 96),
                                       captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.top + MulDiv(16, static_cast<int>(captureDpi), 96),
                                       MulDiv(widthDip, static_cast<int>(captureDpi), 96),
                                       std::min(MulDiv(840, static_cast<int>(captureDpi), 96),
                                                static_cast<int>(captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.bottom - captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.top) -
                                                    MulDiv(32, static_cast<int>(captureDpi), 96)),
                                       SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE) != FALSE,
                          L"Could not resize conflict fixture.");
            for (size_t warmup = 0u; warmup < 4u; ++warmup)
                redraw();
            for (size_t batch = 0u; batch < 5u; ++batch)
            {
                Debug::Perf::Scope measurement(sameName ? L"FileOps.Popup.CommonName.RedrawBatchUs" : L"FileOps.Popup.ConflictRows.RedrawBatchUs");
                for (size_t frame = 0u; frame < 8u; ++frame)
                {
                    redraw();
                    const UINT frameDpi = GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup);
                    MONITORINFOEXW frameMonitorInfo{};
                    frameMonitorInfo.cbSize = sizeof(frameMonitorInfo);
                    // HMONITOR values can be recreated when Windows refreshes display topology.
                    // Verify the display device and geometry, not the lifetime of that opaque handle.
                    const bool sameMonitor = GetMonitorInfoW(MonitorFromWindow(fixture.popup, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST), &frameMonitorInfo) != FALSE &&
                                             std::wstring_view(frameMonitorInfo.szDevice) == captureMonitorInfo.szDevice &&
                                             EqualRect(&frameMonitorInfo.rcMonitor, &captureMonitorInfo.rcMonitor) != FALSE &&
                                             EqualRect(&frameMonitorInfo.rcWork, &captureMonitorInfo.rcWork) != FALSE;
                    RECT frameRect{};
                    GetWindowRect(fixture.popup, &frameRect);
                    state.Require(frameDpi == captureDpi && sameMonitor,
                                  std::format(L"A measured redraw batch must remain on its declared physical display and DPI: sameName={}, width={}, batch={}, "
                                              L"frame={}, DPI={}/{}, monitor={}, device={}/{}, rect=({},{},{},{}), work=({},{},{},{}).",
                                              sameName,
                                              widthDip,
                                              batch,
                                              frame,
                                              frameDpi,
                                              captureDpi,
                                              sameMonitor,
                                              frameMonitorInfo.szDevice,
                                              captureMonitorInfo.szDevice,
                                              frameRect.left,
                                              frameRect.top,
                                              frameRect.right,
                                              frameRect.bottom,
                                              captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.left,
                                              captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.top,
                                              captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.right,
                                              captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.bottom));
                }
            }
            state.Require(dpi == captureDpi && GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup) == captureDpi, L"Capture filenames must describe the actual settled DPI.");
            const auto name = std::format(L"{}__{}dip__fr-FR__{}dpi.png", sameName ? L"common-name" : L"conflict-comparison", widthDip, dpi);
            state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(fixture.popup, captures / name)), L"Conflict harness capture failed.");
            for (const UINT labelId : {static_cast<UINT>(IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SOURCE), static_cast<UINT>(IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_DESTINATION)})
            {
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> action;
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
                UiaRect bounds{};
                FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot bodyLayout{};
                bodyLayout.taskId = task.taskId;
                state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, bodyLayout), L"Comparison viewport geometry must be available.");
                state.Require(bodyLayout.conflictPromptPrecedesActions && bodyLayout.conflictPromptVisibleBottomPx > 0.0f &&
                                  bodyLayout.conflictActionsTopPx > 0.0f && bodyLayout.conflictPromptVisibleBottomPx <= bodyLayout.conflictActionsTopPx + 0.5f,
                              std::format(L"Visible French comparison must precede its fixed decisions: contentBottom={}, visibleBottom={}, actionsTop={}, "
                                          L"bodyBottom={}, contentHeight={}, offset={}.",
                                          bodyLayout.conflictPromptContentBottomPx,
                                          bodyLayout.conflictPromptVisibleBottomPx,
                                          bodyLayout.conflictActionsTopPx,
                                          bodyLayout.conflictBodyBounds.bottom,
                                          bodyLayout.conflictBodyContentHeightPx,
                                          bodyLayout.conflictBodyOffsetDip));
                state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, labelId), action.put()) &&
                                  action && SUCCEEDED(action.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment && SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&bounds)) &&
                                  bounds.width > static_cast<double>(widthDip) * static_cast<double>(dpi) / 96.0 * 0.8 && bounds.height > 0.0,
                              std::format(L"French inspection controls must have their own full-width accessible button row: label={}, window={} DIP, "
                                          L"provider={}, bounds=({}, {}, {}, {}).",
                                          labelId,
                                          widthDip,
                                          action != nullptr,
                                          bounds.left,
                                          bounds.top,
                                          bounds.width,
                                          bounds.height));
                SelfTest::AppendSuiteTrace(
                    SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands,
                    std::format(L"Comparison layout: same={}, width={}, body=({}, {}, {}, {}), panel=({}, {}, {}, {}), content={}, offset={}",
                                sameName,
                                widthDip,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyBounds.left,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyBounds.top,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyBounds.right,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyBounds.bottom,
                                bodyLayout.conflictPanelBounds.left,
                                bodyLayout.conflictPanelBounds.top,
                                bodyLayout.conflictPanelBounds.right,
                                bodyLayout.conflictPanelBounds.bottom,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyContentHeightPx,
                                bodyLayout.conflictBodyOffsetDip));
            }
            FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot inspectionLayout{};
            inspectionLayout.taskId = task.taskId;
            state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, inspectionLayout) && inspectionLayout.taskInspectionControlCount == 2u &&
                              inspectionLayout.taskInspectionLabelsFit,
                          L"The actual retained button typography plus padding must fit both French inspection labels.");
            state.Require(
                inspectionLayout.conflictMeasuredPrimaryCount == task.conflict.primaryActionCount && inspectionLayout.conflictPrimaryLabelsFit &&
                    inspectionLayout.conflictScopeLabelFits && ! inspectionLayout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap,
                std::format(L"Every primary French conflict action must fit its actual hosted typography and remain separate at {} DIP; measured {} of {}.",
                            widthDip,
                            inspectionLayout.conflictMeasuredPrimaryCount,
                            task.conflict.primaryActionCount));
            // Inspect the real hosted tree: policy flags alone missed the stale painted
            // transfer rows and their mini-progress while a decision was pending.
            for (const auto& ordinaryPath : {std::format(L"{} {}", LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_FROM), task.currentSourcePath),
                                             std::format(L"{} {}", LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_TO), task.destinationFolder.wstring())})
            {
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> staleProvider;
                state.Require(! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, ordinaryPath, staleProvider.put()),
                              L"A pending conflict must not retain the ordinary transfer path rows above its decision context.");
            }
            // Inspect the painted semantic tree, including absence of the old duplicate
            // full paths. The common filename must be a heading before both parent folders.
            std::vector<std::wstring> expectedRows{task.conflict.sourceName, task.conflict.sourceParentPath};
            if (! sameName)
                expectedRows.push_back(task.conflict.destinationName);
            expectedRows.push_back(task.conflict.destinationParentPath);
            std::vector<UiaRect> pathBounds(expectedRows.size());
            for (size_t row = 0u; row < expectedRows.size(); ++row)
            {
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
                const bool found = FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, expectedRows[row], provider.put()) && provider;
                state.Require(found, L"Conflict filename and parent location must have complete semantic rows.");
                if (! found)
                    continue;
                std::wstring id;
                if (sameName && row == 0u)
                    state.Require(TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_AutomationIdPropertyId, id) &&
                                      id == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Conflict.Name", task.taskId),
                                  L"A shared filename must be a single prominent heading, not an Incoming name row.");
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment && SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&pathBounds[row])),
                              L"Conflict filename and parent must have real screen bounds.");
                RECT client{};
                GetClientRect(fixture.popup, &client);
                state.Require(pathBounds[row].width > static_cast<double>(client.right) * 0.8 && pathBounds[row].height > 0.0,
                              L"Each conflict filename and parent must use the full comparison width.");
            }
            for (size_t row = 1u; row < pathBounds.size(); ++row)
                state.Require(pathBounds[row - 1u].top + pathBounds[row - 1u].height <= pathBounds[row].top,
                              L"Filename and stacked parent locations must remain ordered without overlap.");
            {
                // Count actual accessible text, not a boolean derived from the model.
                // Include enclosing tooltip regions to catch hidden repeated filenames.
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragmentRoot> root;
                root.attach(DxUi::CreateWindowHostAccessibilityProvider(fixture.popup));
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
                state.Require(root && SUCCEEDED(root.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment, L"Conflict semantic tree is unavailable.");
                std::vector<wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment>> pending;
                if (fragment)
                    pending.push_back(std::move(fragment));
                size_t visited             = 0u;
                size_t filenameMentions    = 0u;
                size_t destinationMentions = 0u;
                size_t waitingMentions     = 0u;
                const auto waitingText     = LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_STATUS_WAITING_FOR_DECISION);
                while (! pending.empty() && visited < 4096u)
                {
                    auto current = std::move(pending.back());
                    pending.pop_back();
                    ++visited;
                    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> simple;
                    std::wstring text;
                    if (SUCCEEDED(current.query_to(simple.put())) && simple && TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*simple, UIA_NamePropertyId, text))
                    {
                        filenameMentions += text.find(task.conflict.sourceName) != std::wstring::npos ? 1u : 0u;
                        destinationMentions += text.find(task.conflict.destinationName) != std::wstring::npos ? 1u : 0u;
                        waitingMentions += text.find(waitingText) != std::wstring::npos ? 1u : 0u;
                    }
                    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> child;
                    if (SUCCEEDED(current->Navigate(NavigateDirection_FirstChild, child.put())))
                    {
                        size_t siblings = 0u;
                        while (child && siblings++ < 4096u)
                        {
                            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> next;
                            static_cast<void>(child->Navigate(NavigateDirection_NextSibling, next.put()));
                            pending.push_back(std::move(child));
                            child = std::move(next);
                        }
                        state.Require(siblings < 4096u, L"Conflict semantic traversal exceeded its sibling budget.");
                    }
                }
                state.Require(pending.empty() && visited < 4096u, L"Conflict semantic traversal exceeded its node budget.");
                state.Require(filenameMentions == 1u,
                              std::format(L"The source or common filename must occur once in the actual UIA tree; saw {}.", filenameMentions));
                state.Require(destinationMentions == 1u,
                              std::format(L"The destination or common filename must occur once in the actual UIA tree; saw {}.", destinationMentions));
                state.Require(waitingMentions == 1u, std::format(L"The waiting state must occur once in the actual UIA tree; saw {}.", waitingMentions));

                for (const auto& staleText : {std::format(L"{} {}", LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_FROM), task.conflict.sourcePath),
                                              std::format(L"{} {}", LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_TO), task.conflict.destinationPath),
