                                              FormatStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FMT_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_EXISTS_NAMED, task.conflict.destinationName)})
                {
                    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> stale;
                    state.Require(! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, staleText, stale.put()),
                                  L"A shared filename must not be repeated in the question or hidden full-path providers.");
                }
            }
            std::array<UiaRect, 2> metadataBounds{};
            for (size_t index = 0u; index < metadataNames.size(); ++index)
            {
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
                const bool found = FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, metadataNames[index], provider.put()) && provider;
                state.Require(found, L"Each comparison metadata value must have its own complete, unabridged semantic row.");
                if (! found)
                    continue;
                std::wstring id;
                state.Require(TryReadRawProviderStringProperty(*provider, UIA_AutomationIdPropertyId, id) &&
                                  id == std::format(L"FileOperations.Task.{}.Conflict.{}.Metadata", task.taskId, index == 0u ? L"From" : L"To"),
                              L"Comparison metadata must retain a stable role identity.");
                wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment && SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&metadataBounds[index])),
                              L"Comparison metadata must expose actual screen bounds.");
                RECT client{};
                GetClientRect(fixture.popup, &client);
                state.Require(metadataBounds[index].width > static_cast<double>(client.right) * 0.8 && metadataBounds[index].height > 0.0,
                              L"Metadata must use the full comparison width rather than a truncated heading column.");
                POINT origin{};
                ClientToScreen(fixture.popup, &origin);
                std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 1> probes{};
                const auto& bounds              = metadataBounds[index];
                probes[0].clientBounds          = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.left)) - origin.x,
                                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(bounds.top)) - origin.y,
                                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.left + bounds.width)) - origin.x,
                                                   static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(bounds.top + bounds.height)) - origin.y};
                probes[0].expectedColor         = theme.menu.text;
                probes[0].minimumMatchingPixels = 8u;
                const HRESULT capture           = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(fixture.popup, captures / name, probes);
                Debug::Perf::Emit(
                    L"FileOps.Popup.Capture.ConflictMetadataPixels", name.c_str(), index, probes[0].matchedPixels, static_cast<uint64_t>(widthDip), capture);
                state.Require(SUCCEEDED(capture), L"The complete metadata row must contain visible text pixels.");
            }
            state.Require(metadataBounds[0].top + metadataBounds[0].height <= metadataBounds[1].top, L"The incoming item must remain above the existing item.");
        }
    }
    // The dedicated name row must preserve a literal backslash in a slash-only
    // namespace. Unqualified items retain their original exact-path role row,
    // while the question describes the collision without repeating that path.
    for (const bool qualified : {true, false})
    {
        auto providerTask                           = task;
        providerTask.conflict.destinationPath       = L"/photographies/été\\original.raw";
        providerTask.conflict.destinationName       = qualified ? L"été\\original.raw" : L"";
        providerTask.conflict.destinationParentPath = qualified ? L"/photographies" : L"";
        popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({providerTask}, false, true);
        redraw();
        const auto expected = qualified ? providerTask.conflict.destinationName : LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_EXISTS);
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> question;
        state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, expected, question.put()) && question,
                      L"The conflict must expose a dedicated qualified name or a path-free collision question.");
        if (! qualified)
        {
            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> context;
            const auto exactContext = std::format(L"{} {}", LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_LABEL_TO), providerTask.conflict.destinationPath);
            state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, exactContext, context.put()) && context,
                          L"An unknown namespace must expose the complete original path without guessing components.");
            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> repeatedQuestion;
            state.Require(
                ! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup,
                                                    0,
                                                    FormatStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FMT_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_EXISTS_NAMED, providerTask.conflict.destinationPath),
                                                    repeatedQuestion.put()),
                L"The unknown destination path must not also appear in the collision question.");
        }
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(
                          fixture.popup, captures / (qualified ? L"qualified-slash-provider.png" : L"unqualified-exact-path.png"))),
                      L"Provider-path fallback capture failed.");
    }
    // Oversized comparison content must scroll independently of its complete decision controls.
    task.conflict.destinationName = task.conflict.sourceName;
    for (size_t part = 0u; part < 18u; ++part)
    {
        task.conflict.sourceParentPath += L"\\Photographies de la réunion familiale conservées sans modification";
        task.conflict.destinationParentPath += L"\\Sauvegarde complète de plusieurs générations de photographies";
    }
    task.conflict.sourcePath      = (std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.sourceParentPath) / task.conflict.sourceName).wstring();
    task.conflict.destinationPath = (std::filesystem::path(task.conflict.destinationParentPath) / task.conflict.destinationName).wstring();
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({task}, false, true);
    const UINT boundedDpi = GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    state.Require(SetWindowPos(fixture.popup,
                               nullptr,
                               0,
                               0,
                               MulDiv(480, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96),
                               MulDiv(520, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96),
                               SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE) != FALSE,
                  L"Could not establish the short conflict viewport.");
    for (size_t warmup = 0u; warmup < 4u; ++warmup)
        redraw();
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_EXITSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    constexpr std::array<UINT, 4> boundedActionLabels{
        {IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_BTN_KEEP_BOTH, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_BTN_OVERWRITE, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_BTN_SKIP, IDS_FILEOP_BTN_CANCEL}};
    const auto actionBounds = [&]
    {
        std::array<UiaRect, boundedActionLabels.size()> bounds{};
        PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot layout{};
        layout.taskId = task.taskId;
        state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, layout), L"Bounded conflict layout is unavailable.");
        POINT origin{};
        ClientToScreen(fixture.popup, &origin);
        RECT window{};
        state.Require(GetWindowRect(fixture.popup, &window) != FALSE && window.bottom - window.top == MulDiv(520, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96),
                      L"The comparison fixture must retain the explicit short window height.");
        for (size_t index = 0u; index < boundedActionLabels.size(); ++index)
        {
            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
            state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(
                              fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, boundedActionLabels[index]), provider.put()) &&
                              provider && SUCCEEDED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) && fragment &&
                              SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&bounds[index])) && bounds[index].width > 0.0 && bounds[index].height > 0.0 &&
                              bounds[index].top >= static_cast<double>(origin.y) + layout.listViewportRect.top &&
                              bounds[index].top + bounds[index].height <= static_cast<double>(origin.y) + layout.listViewportRect.bottom,
                          L"Every conflict decision must remain fully inside the short task viewport before and after comparison scrolling.");
        }
        state.Require(
            layout.conflictApplyToAllVisible && layout.conflictScopeLabelFits && layout.conflictPrimaryLabelsFit && ! layout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap,
            std::format(L"A bounded conflict must retain its complete scope and action labels: scopeVisible={}, scopeFits={}, actionsFit={}, overlap={}",
                        layout.conflictApplyToAllVisible,
                        layout.conflictScopeLabelFits,
                        layout.conflictPrimaryLabelsFit,
                        layout.taskHasVisibleButtonOverlap));
        return bounds;
    };
    const auto comparisonBounds = [&](std::wstring_view text)
    {
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
        UiaRect bounds{};
        state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, text, provider.put()) && provider && SUCCEEDED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) &&
                          fragment && SUCCEEDED(fragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&bounds)),
                      L"Scrolling must retain complete comparison values in the accessible tree.");
        return bounds;
    };
    const auto beforeParent = comparisonBounds(task.conflict.sourceParentPath);
    const auto beforeName   = comparisonBounds(task.conflict.sourceName);
    const auto beforeScroll = actionBounds();
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(
                      fixture.popup, captures / std::format(L"bounded-body__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi__before.png", boundedDpi))),
                  L"Could not capture the short conflict before scrolling.");
    POINT wheelPoint{MulDiv(240, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96), MulDiv(200, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96)};
    ClientToScreen(fixture.popup, &wheelPoint);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_MOUSEWHEEL, MAKEWPARAM(0, static_cast<WORD>(-WHEEL_DELTA * 3)), MAKELPARAM(wheelPoint.x, wheelPoint.y));
    redraw();
    const auto afterScroll = actionBounds();
    const auto afterParent = comparisonBounds(task.conflict.sourceParentPath);
    const auto afterName   = comparisonBounds(task.conflict.sourceName);
    state.Require(beforeParent.height > 0.0 && afterParent.height > 0.0 && afterParent.top < beforeParent.top,
                  L"The delivered wheel input must move the visible comparison content within its own viewport.");
    state.Require(beforeName.height > 0.0 && afterName.top == beforeName.top && afterName.height == beforeName.height,
                  L"The prominent conflict filename must remain visible while its folder comparison scrolls.");
    for (size_t index = 0u; index < beforeScroll.size(); ++index)
        state.Require(beforeScroll[index].left == afterScroll[index].left && beforeScroll[index].top == afterScroll[index].top &&
                          beforeScroll[index].width == afterScroll[index].width && beforeScroll[index].height == afterScroll[index].height,
                      L"Comparison scrolling must leave all decision control rectangles fixed.");
    state.Require(fixture.fileOps->IsPendingConflictInspectionCurrent(fixture.taskId.value(), fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection),
                  L"Scrolling a comparison must leave the real admitted conflict unresolved.");
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(
                      fixture.popup, captures / std::format(L"bounded-body__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi__after.png", boundedDpi))),
                  L"Could not capture the short conflict after scrolling.");
    // Use the admitted live prompt for keyboard inspection; synthetic paths must not grant navigation authority.
    auto keyboardTask = fixture.taskSnapshot;
    for (size_t line = 0u; line < 20u; ++line)
        keyboardTask.lastDiagnosticMessage += L"Les informations complémentaires concernant cette opération restent consultables. ";
    popupState->DebugSetVisualScenario({keyboardTask}, false, true);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    for (size_t warmup = 0u; warmup < 4u; ++warmup)
        redraw();
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_EXITSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(fixture.popup),
                  L"The keyboard capture must own foreground through the existing warning and desktop lease.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    POINT savedKeyboardCursor{};
    const bool savedKeyboardCursorValid = GetCursorPos(&savedKeyboardCursor) != FALSE;
    const auto restoreKeyboardCursor    = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        if (savedKeyboardCursorValid)
            SetCursorPos(savedKeyboardCursor.x, savedKeyboardCursor.y);
    });
    state.Require(savedKeyboardCursorValid && SetCursorPos(captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.right - 2, captureMonitorInfo.rcWork.top + 2) != FALSE,
                  L"Place the pointer outside the owned keyboard capture and retain it for restoration.");
    PumpPendingMessages();
    redraw();
    POINT keyboardOrigin{};
    ClientToScreen(fixture.popup, &keyboardOrigin);
    const auto questionBounds = comparisonBounds(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_EXISTS));
    std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 1> questionProbe{};
    questionProbe[0].clientBounds          = {static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(questionBounds.left)) - keyboardOrigin.x,
                                              static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(questionBounds.top)) - keyboardOrigin.y,
                                              static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(questionBounds.left + questionBounds.width)) - keyboardOrigin.x,
                                              static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(questionBounds.top + questionBounds.height)) - keyboardOrigin.y};
    questionProbe[0].expectedColor         = theme.menu.text;
    questionProbe[0].minimumMatchingPixels = 80u;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(
                      fixture.popup, captures / std::format(L"bounded-body__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi__keyboard-before.png", boundedDpi), questionProbe)),
                  L"The fixed conflict explanation must contain its text pixels before keyboard inspection.");
    const uint64_t questionPixelsBefore = questionProbe[0].matchedPixels;
    const auto beforeKeyboard           = actionBounds();
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> destinationInspection;
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> destinationFragment;
    state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(
                      fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_DESTINATION), destinationInspection.put()) &&
                      destinationInspection && SUCCEEDED(destinationInspection.query_to(destinationFragment.put())) && destinationFragment &&
                      SUCCEEDED(destinationFragment->SetFocus()),
                  L"The admitted destination inspection must accept keyboard/UIA focus inside a short comparison.");
    redraw();
    UiaRect inspectionBounds{};
    state.Require(destinationFragment && SUCCEEDED(destinationFragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&inspectionBounds)) && inspectionBounds.width > 0.0 &&
                      inspectionBounds.height >= static_cast<double>(MulDiv(24, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96)),
                  L"Focusing an offscreen inspection action must reveal its complete bounds.");
    const auto afterKeyboard = actionBounds();
    for (size_t index = 0u; index < beforeKeyboard.size(); ++index)
        state.Require(beforeKeyboard[index].top == afterKeyboard[index].top && beforeKeyboard[index].height == afterKeyboard[index].height,
                      L"Revealing an inspection by keyboard must leave decisions stationary.");
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYDOWN, VK_TAB, 0);
    redraw();
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> scopeProvider;
    wil::unique_variant focusedScope;
    state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(
                      fixture.popup, UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_APPLY_TO_ALL), scopeProvider.put()) &&
                      scopeProvider && SUCCEEDED(scopeProvider->GetPropertyValue(UIA_HasKeyboardFocusPropertyId, &focusedScope)) &&
                      focusedScope.vt == VT_BOOL && focusedScope.boolVal == VARIANT_TRUE,
                  L"Tab must leave the comparison through the scope checkbox in visual reading order.");
    state.Require(fixture.fileOps->IsPendingConflictInspectionCurrent(fixture.taskId.value(), fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection),
                  L"Keyboard inspection focus must not consent to or resolve the engine conflict.");
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYUP, VK_TAB, 0);
    const auto focusedScopeBounds = comparisonBounds(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_APPLY_TO_ALL));
    std::array<RedSalamander::TestSupport::WindowScreenshotColorProbe, 2> keyboardProbes{};
    keyboardProbes[0]                       = questionProbe[0];
    keyboardProbes[0].minimumMatchingPixels = static_cast<uint32_t>(questionPixelsBefore * 95u / 100u);
    const auto focusColor                   = MakeAppThemeDxPalette(theme).focusStrokeOuter;
    const int focusOutset                   = MulDiv(4, static_cast<int>(boundedDpi), 96);
    const LONG scopeTop                     = static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(focusedScopeBounds.top)) - keyboardOrigin.y;
    keyboardProbes[1].clientBounds  = {static_cast<LONG>(std::ceil(focusedScopeBounds.left)) - keyboardOrigin.x + focusOutset,
                                       scopeTop - focusOutset,
                                       static_cast<LONG>(std::floor(focusedScopeBounds.left + focusedScopeBounds.width)) - keyboardOrigin.x - focusOutset,
                                       scopeTop};
    keyboardProbes[1].expectedColor = ColorToCOLORREF(D2D1::ColorF(focusColor.r, focusColor.g, focusColor.b));
    keyboardProbes[1].minimumMatchingPixels = 60u;
    PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot keyboardStateBefore{};
    keyboardStateBefore.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, keyboardStateBefore), L"Capture keyboard state before WGC.");
    const HWND foregroundAtCapture = GetForegroundWindow();
    state.Require(keyboardStateBefore.hostedFocusedHasFocus && keyboardStateBefore.hostedReducedMotion,
                  L"The captured scope must retain actual control focus and the delivered reduced-motion policy.");
    const HRESULT keyboardCapture = RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(
        fixture.popup, captures / std::format(L"bounded-body__480dip__fr-FR__{}dpi__keyboard.png", boundedDpi), keyboardProbes);
    PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot keyboardStateAfter{};
    keyboardStateAfter.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
    state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, keyboardStateAfter), L"Capture keyboard state after WGC.");
    POINT cursorAfterCapture{};
    const bool cursorAfterValid  = GetCursorPos(&cursorAfterCapture) != FALSE;
    const auto scopeAfterCapture = comparisonBounds(LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_APPLY_TO_ALL));
    Trace(std::format(L"BoundedKeyboardCapture: repeat={} hr=0x{:08X} text={}/{} focusPixels={} modality={}/{} control={}/{} foregroundOwned={}/{} "
                      L"cursorValid={} cursor=({}, {}) scopeTop={}/{}.",
                      repeatIndex,
                      static_cast<unsigned long>(keyboardCapture),
                      keyboardProbes[0].matchedPixels,
                      questionPixelsBefore,
                      keyboardProbes[1].matchedPixels,
                      keyboardStateBefore.hostedKeyboardFocusVisible,
                      keyboardStateAfter.hostedKeyboardFocusVisible,
                      keyboardStateBefore.hostedFocusedAutomationId,
                      keyboardStateAfter.hostedFocusedAutomationId,
                      foregroundAtCapture == fixture.popup,
                      GetForegroundWindow() == fixture.popup,
                      cursorAfterValid,
                      cursorAfterCapture.x,
                      cursorAfterCapture.y,
                      focusedScopeBounds.top,
                      scopeAfterCapture.top));
    Trace(std::format(L"BoundedKeyboardPaintState: repeat={} actualFocus={}/{} reducedMotion={}/{} nativeFocusOwned={}.",
                      repeatIndex,
                      keyboardStateBefore.hostedFocusedHasFocus,
                      keyboardStateAfter.hostedFocusedHasFocus,
                      keyboardStateBefore.hostedReducedMotion,
                      keyboardStateAfter.hostedReducedMotion,
                      GetFocus() == fixture.popup));
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(keyboardCapture),
                  std::format(L"Keyboard capture must retain the fixed explanation and show scope focus: repeat={}, text={}/{}, focus={}, keyboard={}/{}, "
                              L"foreground={}/{}, scopeTop={}/{}.",
                              repeatIndex,
                              keyboardProbes[0].matchedPixels,
                              questionPixelsBefore,
                              keyboardProbes[1].matchedPixels,
                              keyboardStateBefore.hostedKeyboardFocusVisible,
                              keyboardStateAfter.hostedKeyboardFocusVisible,
                              foregroundAtCapture == fixture.popup,
                              GetForegroundWindow() == fixture.popup,
                              focusedScopeBounds.top,
                              scopeAfterCapture.top));
    state.Require(fixture.Cleanup(), L"Conflict comparison fixture must clean its owned real task.");
    state.Require(std::ranges::all_of(frameMetricCounts, [](uint64_t count) noexcept { return count > 0u; }),
                  L"Comparison captures must retain total, update, render and presentation stage metrics.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ValidatePendingConflictInspectionSelection(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    using Status          = FolderView::InspectionSelectionStatus;
    CaseState& state      = fixture.state;
    const auto pane       = FolderWindow::Pane::Left;
    const HWND paneWindow = g_folderWindow.GetFolderViewHwnd(pane);
    auto* view            = reinterpret_cast<FolderView*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(paneWindow, GWLP_USERDATA));
    state.Require(view && view->GetHWND() == paneWindow, L"Inspection selection requires the owned live pane.");
    if (! view)
        return false;
    const auto inspection = fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection;
    FileOperations::ConflictInspectionTarget sourceTarget;
    FileOperations::ConflictInspectionTarget destinationTarget;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(fixture.fileOps->ResolvePendingConflictInspectionTarget(fixture.taskId.value(), inspection, false, sourceTarget)) &&
                      SUCCEEDED(fixture.fileOps->ResolvePendingConflictInspectionTarget(fixture.taskId.value(), inspection, true, destinationTarget)),
                  L"Both pending roles must resolve their admitted live provider independently of current pane location.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    state.Require(sourceTarget.context == inspection && destinationTarget.context == inspection && ! sourceTarget.destination &&
                      destinationTarget.destination && sourceTarget.fileSystem && destinationTarget.fileSystem && sourceTarget.instanceContext.empty() &&
                      destinationTarget.instanceContext.empty(),
                  L"Inspection must retain the role adapter and raw default context, not host/default as a pane context.");
    state.Require(
        SUCCEEDED(fixture.fileOps->ResolvePendingConflictInspectionTarget(fixture.taskId.value(), destinationTarget.context, true, destinationTarget)) &&
            destinationTarget.context == inspection,
        L"Requalifying an output using its own context must retain the token before clearing old output.");
    const auto& originalLocation = inspection->destination.value();
    const auto qualify           = [&](const FileOperations::ConflictInspectionLocation& location, std::wstring_view plugin, std::wstring_view instance)
    { return FileOperations::QualifyConflictInspectionLocation(location, inspection->operation, destinationTarget.fileSystem.get(), plugin, instance); };
    for (unsigned int changed = 0u; changed < 5u; ++changed)
    {
        auto location = originalLocation;
        switch (changed)
        {
            case 0u: location.endpoint.profileId.append(L"-changed"); break;
            case 1u: location.endpoint.rootId.append(L"-changed"); break;
            case 2u: location.endpoint.pathIdentity->componentComparison = FileSystemPathComponentComparison::OrdinalCaseSensitive; break;
            case 3u: location.endpoint.pathIdentity->acceptedSeparators.append(L"!"); break;
            case 4u: location.endpoint.instanceId = L"host/context/changed"; break;
        }
        state.Require(qualify(location, destinationTarget.pluginId, destinationTarget.instanceContext) == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_REVISION_MISMATCH),
                      L"Changed profile/root/path rules/instance must fail closed before pane navigation.");
    }
    auto invalidLocation     = originalLocation;
    invalidLocation.itemName = L"another-item.txt";
    state.Require(
        qualify(invalidLocation, destinationTarget.pluginId, destinationTarget.instanceContext) == E_INVALIDARG &&
            qualify(originalLocation, L"builtin/different-provider", destinationTarget.instanceContext) == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_REVISION_MISMATCH) &&
            qualify(originalLocation, destinationTarget.pluginId, originalLocation.endpoint.instanceId) == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_REVISION_MISMATCH),
        L"Inconsistent item identity, another provider and canonical-as-raw context must not produce a navigable target.");
    invalidLocation = originalLocation;
    invalidLocation.endpoint.pathIdentity.reset();
    state.Require(qualify(invalidLocation, destinationTarget.pluginId, destinationTarget.instanceContext) == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED) &&
                      FileOperations::QualifyConflictInspectionLocation(
                          originalLocation, inspection->operation, nullptr, destinationTarget.pluginId, destinationTarget.instanceContext) ==
                          HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NOT_SUPPORTED),
                  L"Unknown qualification and absent providers must remain unavailable.");
    auto staleContext  = std::make_shared<const FileOperations::ConflictInspectionContext>(*inspection);
    auto clearedTarget = destinationTarget;
    state.Require(fixture.fileOps->ResolvePendingConflictInspectionTarget(fixture.taskId.value(), staleContext, true, clearedTarget) == E_ABORT &&
                      ! clearedTarget.context && ! clearedTarget.fileSystem && clearedTarget.pluginId.empty(),
                  L"A stale equal-path prompt must clear previously qualified output without navigation.");
    const auto identity     = FileSystemPathIdentity::OrdinalIgnoreCaseForLocalFileSystem();
    size_t settledSuccesses = 0u;
    size_t settledFailures  = 0u;
    FolderView::InspectionSelectionResult selectionFromEvent{};
    bool eventOnUiThread       = true;
    bool failureReceiptMatches = true;
    const DWORD owningThread   = GetCurrentThreadId();
    g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationSettledCallback(pane,
                                                     [&](const FolderView::InspectionVisit& eventVisit, const std::filesystem::path& folder, HRESULT status)
    {
        eventOnUiThread = eventOnUiThread && GetCurrentThreadId() == owningThread;
        if (FAILED(status))
        {
            ++settledFailures;
            const auto failed     = view->SelectItemForInspection(eventVisit, identity, fixture.dest.native());
            failureReceiptMatches = failureReceiptMatches && failed.status == Status::EnumerationFailed && failed.enumerationStatus == status;
        }
        else
        {
            ++settledSuccesses;
            if (folder == fixture.destDir)
                selectionFromEvent = view->SelectItemForInspection(eventVisit, identity, fixture.dest.native());
        }
    });
    const auto unsubscribe   = wil::scope_exit([&] { g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationSettledCallback(pane, {}); });
    const auto prompt        = fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection;
    const auto originalVisit = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.destDir);
    auto visit = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    state.Require(originalVisit && visit, L"Both navigation visits must be capturable on the owning UI thread.");
    if (! originalVisit || ! visit)
        return false;
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(originalVisit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Stale,
                  L"Navigation must invalidate the previous visit before any late inspection selection.");
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Pending,
                  L"Navigation acceptance must not claim enumeration/selection completion.");
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.dest.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)),
                  L"Inspection destination did not enumerate its conflict item.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    state.Require(settledSuccesses > 0u && selectionFromEvent.status == Status::Selected && eventOnUiThread,
                  L"The owning pane settlement event must support exact selection after all enumeration work without polling.");
    // Capture the settled visit; a legitimate cache refresh can supersede the requested enumeration.
    visit = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    if (! visit)
        return false;
    const HWND focusBefore      = GetFocus();
    const HWND foregroundBefore = GetForegroundWindow();
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Selected,
                  L"Inspection must select the exact conflicting item after enumeration settles.");
    state.Require(view->GetFocusedPath() == fixture.dest && view->GetSelectedPaths() == std::vector<std::filesystem::path>{fixture.dest},
                  L"Inspection must establish both one selected item and the exact current item.");
    state.Require(GetFocus() == focusBefore && GetForegroundWindow() == foregroundBefore,
                  L"Selecting an inspection item must not activate a window or acquire OS focus.");
    visit = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    if (! visit)
        return false;
    const auto selectedBefore = view->GetSelectedPaths();
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, (fixture.destDir / L"missing-inspection-item.txt").native()).status ==
                      Status::MissingOrFiltered,
                  L"A missing/filtered conflict item must not select a neighbor.");
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, fixture.source.native()).status == Status::Unavailable,
                  L"An item in another parent must not be selected by its coincident leaf name.");
    auto unstable                   = identity;
    unstable.pathTextStableIdentity = false;
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), unstable, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Unavailable,
                  L"Unknown path identity must fail before changing pane selection.");
    std::wstring upperName = fixture.dest.filename().wstring();
    CharUpperBuffW(upperName.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(upperName.size()));
    auto sensitive                = identity;
    sensitive.componentComparison = FileSystemPathComponentComparison::OrdinalCaseSensitive;
    state.Require(upperName != fixture.dest.filename().native(), L"Case fixture must differ from the existing exact spelling.");
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), sensitive, (fixture.destDir / upperName).native()).status == Status::MissingOrFiltered,
                  L"Inspection must not use case-insensitive fallback under a case-sensitive provider contract.");
    state.Require(view->GetSelectedPaths() == selectedBefore && view->GetFocusedPath() == fixture.dest,
                  L"Rejected targets must leave current and selection unchanged.");
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, (fixture.destDir / upperName).native()).status == Status::Selected,
                  L"A uniquely matching local case-insensitive identity should resolve its actual item spelling.");
    const HWND otherWindow = g_folderWindow.GetFolderViewHwnd(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    auto* other            = reinterpret_cast<FolderView*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(otherWindow, GWLP_USERDATA));
    state.Require(other && other->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Stale,
                  L"A visit token from the other pane must never authorize selection.");
    view->ForceRefresh();
    state.Require(view->SelectItemForInspection(visit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Stale,
                  L"A newer same-folder enumeration must supersede an inspection completion.");
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.dest.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Refreshed conflict destination did not settle.");
    const auto beforeFailure = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.destDir / L"missing-inspection-directory");
    const auto failedVisit = view->CaptureInspectionVisit();
    FolderView::InspectionSelectionResult result{};
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
    if (failedVisit)
    {
        do
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            result = view->SelectItemForInspection(failedVisit.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native());
            if (result.status != Status::Pending)
                break;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
        } while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline);
    }
    state.Require(failedVisit && result.status == Status::EnumerationFailed && FAILED(result.enumerationStatus),
                  L"Failed navigation must expose its enumeration failure instead of selecting the retained old model.");
    state.Require(settledFailures == 1u && failureReceiptMatches && eventOnUiThread,
                  L"The owner must receive one failed settlement with the exact status and no old-model selection.");
    const size_t successesBeforeRestore = settledSuccesses;
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.destDir);
    // A failed enumeration retains the old item model. Finding that same leaf
    // cannot establish that this new navigation has settled. Wait on the existing
    // owner completion event before installing the reentrant-selection witness.
    const auto restoreDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(3000ms);
    while ((settledSuccesses == successesBeforeRestore || ! view->IsCurrentFolderEnumerated()) && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < restoreDeadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(20ms);
    }
    state.Require(settledSuccesses > successesBeforeRestore && view->IsCurrentFolderEnumerated() && selectionFromEvent.status == Status::Selected,
                  L"Restoring the conflict folder must complete a new successful enumeration and exact selection, not reuse retained cached items.");
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.dest.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Restored conflict folder did not enumerate.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    state.Require(beforeFailure && view->SelectItemForInspection(beforeFailure.value(), identity, fixture.dest.native()).status == Status::Stale,
                  L"Returning to identical path text must not resurrect an earlier navigation visit.");
    state.Require(fixture.fileOps->IsPendingConflictInspectionCurrent(fixture.taskId.value(), prompt),
                  L"Pane inspection selection must leave the same prompt generation pending.");
    state.Require(TextFileEqualsForFileOpsPrompt(fixture.dest, "existing payload"),
                  L"Inspection must preserve the exact original destination bytes while the conflict remains pending.");
    state.Require(view->DebugValidateInspectionSelectionReentryForSelfTest(identity, fixture.dest.native()),
                  L"Reentrant owner navigation must invalidate selection without reusing its old item index.");
    size_t selfUnsubscribeCount = 0u;
    g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationSettledCallback(pane,
                                                     [&](const FolderView::InspectionVisit&, const std::filesystem::path&, HRESULT)
    {
        g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationSettledCallback(pane, {});
        ++selfUnsubscribeCount;
    });
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.sourceDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.source.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Inspection fixture must restore its source pane.");
    view->ForceRefresh();
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.source.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)) && selfUnsubscribeCount == 1u,
                  L"A settled callback must safely unsubscribe itself and receive no later event.");
    size_t obsoleteSettlements        = 0u;
    size_t currentSettlements         = 0u;
    bool redirectedFromLegacyCallback = false;
    g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationSettledCallback(pane,
                                                     [&](const FolderView::InspectionVisit&, const std::filesystem::path& folder, HRESULT)
    {
        if (folder == fixture.destDir)
            ++obsoleteSettlements;
        if (folder == fixture.sourceDir)
            ++currentSettlements;
    });
    g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationCompletedCallback(pane,
                                                       [&](const std::filesystem::path& folder)
    {
        if (folder == fixture.destDir && ! redirectedFromLegacyCallback)
        {
            redirectedFromLegacyCallback = true;
            g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.sourceDir);
        }
    });
    const auto restoreCompletedCallback = wil::scope_exit([&] { g_folderWindow.SetPaneEnumerationCompletedCallback(pane, {}); });
    g_folderWindow.SetFolderPath(pane, fixture.destDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPanePath(pane, fixture.sourceDir, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)) &&
                      WaitForPaneItems(pane, {fixture.source.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)) && redirectedFromLegacyCallback &&
                      obsoleteSettlements == 0u && currentSettlements > 0u,
                  L"Reentrant navigation from legacy completion must suppress the old settlement and notify only the new visit.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ValidatePendingConflictInspectionNavigation(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    using Status          = FileOperations::ConflictInspectionNavigationStatus;
    using Result          = FileOperations::ConflictInspectionNavigationResult;
    auto& owner           = g_folderWindow;
    auto& state           = fixture.state;
    auto* operations      = fixture.fileOps;
    const auto context    = fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection;
    const auto activePane = owner.GetActivePane();
    const auto leftPath   = owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const auto rightPath  = owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    const auto restore    = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        operations->CancelPendingConflictInspection();
        if (leftPath)
            owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftPath.value());
        if (rightPath)
            owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightPath.value());
        owner.SetActivePane(activePane);
    });
    std::optional<Result> result;
    const auto complete = [&](const Result& value) { result = value; };
    const auto wait     = [&]()
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(4000ms);
        while (! result && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        return result.has_value();
    };
    for (const auto pane : {FolderWindow::Pane::Left, FolderWindow::Pane::Right})
    {
        owner.SetActivePane(pane);
        const bool destination = pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left;
        result.reset();
        const HWND focus      = GetFocus();
        const HWND foreground = GetForegroundWindow();
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), context, destination, complete)) && wait() &&
                          result->status == Status::Selected,
                      L"Inspection must navigate the captured active pane and select the exact pending role item.");
        const auto expected = destination ? fixture.dest : fixture.source;
        const auto selected = owner.DebugGetPaneCommandTargetPathsForSelfTest(pane);
        state.Require(owner.GetCurrentPath(pane) == expected.parent_path() && selected.size() == 1u && selected.front() == expected && GetFocus() == focus &&
                          GetForegroundWindow() == foreground,
                      L"The non-activating owner transaction must select only its exact item and preserve OS focus.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
    }
    owner.SetActivePane(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    result.reset();
    size_t completionCount = 0u;
    HRESULT chainedStatus  = E_PENDING;
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(),
                                                                       context,
                                                                       true,
                                                                       [&](const Result& first)
    {
        ++completionCount;
        state.Require(first.status == Status::Selected, L"First chained inspection must select its target.");
        chainedStatus = operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(),
                                                                   context,
                                                                   false,
                                                                   [&](const Result& second)
        {
            ++completionCount;
            complete(second);
        });
    })) && wait() && SUCCEEDED(chainedStatus) &&
                      completionCount == 2u && result->status == Status::Selected,
                  L"A completion callback must be able to begin the next inspection without retaining the old request or listener.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    result.reset();
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), context, true, complete)),
                  L"Inspection cancellation fixture must begin navigation.");
    if (! result)
    {
        state.Require(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), context, false, complete) == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_BUSY),
                      L"An in-flight inspection must reject a duplicate without replacing its completion owner.");
        operations->CancelPendingConflictInspection();
        state.Require(result && result->status == Status::Stale, L"Explicit cancellation must complete once as stale without waiting for enumeration.");
    }
    PumpPendingMessages();
    owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, fixture.sourceDir);
    state.Require(WaitForPaneItems(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, {fixture.source.filename().wstring()}, SelfTest::Scale(3000ms)), L"Source reset failed.");
    result.reset();
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), context, true, complete)), L"Superseded inspection must start.");
    owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, fixture.sourceDir);
    state.Require(wait() && result->status == Status::Stale && owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left) == fixture.sourceDir,
                  L"A newer pane navigation must supersede an earlier inspection without pulling the pane back.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;

    // Change only immutable display-inspection facts in the blocked harness prompt.
    // Engine paths, bindings, decision, repeat scope and source/destination bytes stay unchanged.
    auto* task = operations->FindTask(fixture.taskId.value());
    if (! task)
        return false;
    const auto restoreContext = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
        task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = context;
    });
    for (const bool missingParent : {false, true})
    {
        auto altered                       = std::make_shared<FileOperations::ConflictInspectionContext>(*context);
        const auto parent                  = missingParent ? fixture.destDir / L"inspection-parent-that-does-not-exist" : fixture.destDir;
        const auto path                    = parent / L"inspection-item-that-does-not-exist.txt";
        altered->destination->parentPath   = parent.native();
        altered->destination->providerPath = path.native();
        altered->destination->itemName     = path.filename().native();
        {
            std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
            task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = altered;
        }
        const auto previous = owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
        result.reset();
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), altered, true, complete)) && wait(),
                      L"Missing inspection target must produce an event-driven terminal result.");
        state.Require(result && result->status == (missingParent ? Status::NavigationFailed : Status::MissingOrFiltered),
                      L"Missing file and failed folder enumeration must have distinct truthful inspection outcomes.");
        if (missingParent)
            state.Require(owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left) == previous,
                          L"Failed folder navigation must settle restoration of the previous pane location.");
        if (! state.failure.empty())
            return false;
    }
    {
        std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
        task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = context;
    }
    result.reset();
    auto stale = std::make_shared<const FileOperations::ConflictInspectionContext>(*context);
    state.Require(operations->BeginPendingConflictInspection(fixture.taskId.value(), stale, true, complete) == E_ABORT && ! result,
                  L"A stale equal-path prompt must be refused before navigation and must not claim request acceptance.");
    state.Require(operations->IsPendingConflictInspectionCurrent(fixture.taskId.value(), context),
                  L"Inspection must leave the original engine prompt unresolved and current.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool ValidatePendingConflictInspectionControls(FileOperationsPopupTestFixture& fixture) noexcept
{
    using namespace std::chrono_literals;
    Localization::LanguagePreference previousLanguage{};
    if (g_settings.ui.has_value() && ! g_settings.ui->language.empty() && g_settings.ui->language != L"system")
        previousLanguage = {Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, g_settings.ui->language};
    const auto restoreLanguage = wil::scope_exit([&] { static_cast<void>(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference(previousLanguage)); });
    fixture.state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, L"fr-FR"})),
                          L"Inspection results require the French satellite.");
    // ApplyCurrentSettingsToRunningApp routes a live language change through
    // WM_THEMECHANGED; exercise that same notification on the existing popup.
    std::wstring englishTitle;
    for (const auto* culture : {L"en-US", L"fr-FR"})
    {
        fixture.state.Require(SUCCEEDED(Localization::ApplyLanguagePreference({Localization::LanguagePreferenceKind::Culture, culture})),
                              L"Live caption coverage requires the English and French satellites.");
        SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_THEMECHANGED, 0, 0);
        const std::wstring title = Win32Text::GetWindowTextString(fixture.popup);
        fixture.state.Require(title == LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_POPUP_TITLE),
                              L"An existing popup must refresh its native title when the language changes.");
        if (englishTitle.empty())
            englishTitle = title;
        else
            fixture.state.Require(title != englishTitle, L"The French popup title must differ from the English title.");
    }
    const auto captures = SelfTest::GetSuiteArtifactPath(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands, L"conflict-inspection");
    fixture.state.Require(! captures.empty() && SelfTest::EnsureDirectory(captures), L"Inspection capture directory is unavailable.");
    auto& state                   = fixture.state;
    auto& owner                   = g_folderWindow;
    const auto activePane         = owner.GetActivePane();
    const auto leftPath           = owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left);
    const auto rightPath          = owner.GetCurrentPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right);
    const auto context            = fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.inspection;
    const HWND originalForeground = GetForegroundWindow();
    const HWND originalFocus      = GetFocus();
    POINT originalCursor{};
    const bool cursorSaved = GetCursorPos(&originalCursor) != FALSE;
    const auto restore     = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        fixture.fileOps->CancelPendingConflictInspection();
        if (leftPath)
            owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Left, leftPath.value());
        if (rightPath)
            owner.SetFolderPath(FolderWindow::Pane::Right, rightPath.value());
        owner.SetActivePane(activePane);
        if (cursorSaved)
            SetCursorPos(originalCursor.x, originalCursor.y);
        if (originalForeground && IsWindow(originalForeground))
            SetForegroundWindow(originalForeground);
        if (originalFocus && IsWindow(originalFocus))
            SetFocus(originalFocus);
    });
    const auto redraw      = [&] { RedrawWindow(fixture.popup, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN); };
    const auto capture     = [&](std::wstring_view name, UINT messageId)
    {
        redraw();
        FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot layout{};
        layout.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
        state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, layout) && layout.taskInspectionResultFits,
                      L"The painted inspection result must retain its complete wrapped text height, not just a nonempty clipped semantic rectangle.");
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> resultProvider;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> scopeProvider;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> resultFragment;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> scopeFragment;
        UiaRect resultBounds{};
        UiaRect scopeBounds{};
        state.Require(FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, LoadStringResource(nullptr, messageId), resultProvider.put()) &&
                          FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(
                              fixture.popup, UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_APPLY_TO_ALL), scopeProvider.put()) &&
                          resultProvider && scopeProvider && SUCCEEDED(resultProvider.query_to(resultFragment.put())) &&
                          SUCCEEDED(scopeProvider.query_to(scopeFragment.put())) && SUCCEEDED(resultFragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&resultBounds)) &&
                          SUCCEEDED(scopeFragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&scopeBounds)) && resultBounds.height > 0.0 &&
                          resultBounds.top + resultBounds.height <= scopeBounds.top,
                      L"An inspection result must fit before the decision scope immediately, without waiting for an expanding card animation.");
        state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(fixture.popup, captures / std::filesystem::path(name))),
                      L"The harness must retain the actual inspection result surface.");
    };
    const auto invoke = [&](UINT labelId)
    {
        redraw();
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IUnknown> pattern;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IInvokeProvider> action;
        return FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, labelId), provider.put()) && provider &&
               SUCCEEDED(provider->GetPatternProvider(UIA_InvokePatternId, pattern.put())) && pattern && SUCCEEDED(pattern.query_to(action.put())) && action &&
               SUCCEEDED(action->Invoke());
    };
    const auto invokeKey = [&](UINT labelId, UINT key)
    {
        redraw();
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
        wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> fragment;
        if (! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, labelId), provider.put()) || ! provider ||
            FAILED(provider.query_to(fragment.put())) || ! fragment || FAILED(fragment->SetFocus()))
            return false;
        SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYDOWN, key, 0);
        SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYUP, key, 0);
        return true;
    };
    const auto waitForMessage = [&](UINT messageId)
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(4000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            redraw();
            wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> provider;
            if (FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, LoadStringResource(nullptr, messageId), provider.put()))
                return true;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        return false;
    };
    const auto waitForHidden = [&]
    {
        const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(4000ms);
        while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        {
            PumpPendingMessages();
            if (! IsWindowVisible(fixture.popup))
                return true;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
        }
        return false;
    };
    // Each role must work in either last-active pane through UIA and both button
    // activation keys; tying a role or key to one pane misses navigation defects.
    for (const UINT activationKey : std::array<UINT, 3>{{0u, VK_SPACE, VK_RETURN}})
        for (const auto pane : {FolderWindow::Pane::Left, FolderWindow::Pane::Right})
            for (const bool destination : {true, false})
            {
                const bool keyboard = activationKey != 0u;
                if (activationKey == VK_SPACE && pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left && destination)
                    state.Require(
                        DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(
                            fixture.popup, {FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictToggleApplyToAll, fixture.taskId.value(), 0u}),
                        L"Inspect with the repeat-scope checkbox checked as well as unchecked.");
                owner.SetActivePane(pane);
                const auto hitKind = destination ? FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictInspectDestination
                                                 : FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictInspectSource;
                state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(fixture.popup),
                              L"Inspection focus acceptance requires the existing warning and owned foreground.");
                if (! state.failure.empty())
                    return false;
                redraw();
                FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot before{};
                before.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
                state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, before), L"Capture the pre-inspection decision state.");
                const UINT labelId = destination ? IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_DESTINATION : IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SOURCE;
                LASTINPUTINFO inputBefore{sizeof(LASTINPUTINFO)};
                const bool inputBeforeKnown = GetLastInputInfo(&inputBefore) != FALSE;
                const HWND focusBefore      = GetFocus();
                const HWND foregroundBefore = GetForegroundWindow();
                const bool invoked          = keyboard ? invokeKey(labelId, activationKey) : invoke(labelId);
                const bool hidden           = invoked && waitForHidden();
                LASTINPUTINFO inputAfter{sizeof(LASTINPUTINFO)};
                const bool inputAfterKnown = GetLastInputInfo(&inputAfter) != FALSE;
                DWORD foregroundProcess    = 0u;
                GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), &foregroundProcess);
                SelfTest::AppendSuiteTrace(
                    SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands,
                    std::format(L"Inspection handoff: keyboard={}, key={}, pane={}, destination={}, invoked={}, hidden={}, inputKnown={}/{}, inputTick={}/{}, "
                                L"focus={}/{}, foreground={}/{}, foregroundProcess={}, ownProcess={}",
                                keyboard,
                                activationKey,
                                pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left ? L"left" : L"right",
                                destination,
                                invoked,
                                hidden,
                                inputBeforeKnown,
                                inputAfterKnown,
                                inputBefore.dwTime,
                                inputAfter.dwTime,
                                reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(focusBefore),
                                reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetFocus()),
                                reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(foregroundBefore),
                                reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetForegroundWindow()),
                                foregroundProcess,
                                GetCurrentProcessId()));
                state.Require(hidden,
                              L"Successful foreground UIA inspection must hide the popup through its existing lifecycle; see Inspection handoff witnesses.");
                const auto expected = destination ? fixture.dest : fixture.source;
                const auto selected = owner.DebugGetPaneCommandTargetPathsForSelfTest(pane);
                state.Require(selected.size() == 1u && selected.front() == expected && owner.GetCurrentPath(pane) == expected.parent_path() &&
                                  GetFocus() == owner.GetFolderViewHwnd(pane) && GetForegroundWindow() == GetAncestor(owner.GetHwnd(), GA_ROOT) &&
                                  fixture.fileOps->IsPendingConflictInspectionCurrent(fixture.taskId.value(), context),
                              L"Inspection must focus the exact selected role item while leaving its decision unresolved.");
                if (! state.failure.empty())
                    return false;
                const HWND inspectionWindow = GetAncestor(owner.GetHwnd(), GA_ROOT);
                RedrawWindow(inspectionWindow, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_UPDATENOW | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
                state.Require(
                    SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(inspectionWindow,
                                                                               captures / std::format(L"selected__{}__{}__{}__fr-FR.png",
                                                                                                      pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left ? L"left" : L"right",
                                                                                                      destination ? L"destination" : L"source",
                                                                                                      activationKey == 0u         ? L"uia"
                                                                                                      : activationKey == VK_SPACE ? L"space"
                                                                                                                                  : L"enter"))),
                    L"The harness must retain the exact selected item in the active pane before returning to the unresolved decision.");
                if (! state.failure.empty())
                    return false;
                const HWND samePopup = fixture.popup;
                SendMessageW(GetAncestor(owner.GetHwnd(), GA_ROOT), WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDM_VIEW_FILE_OPERATIONS, 0), 0);
                PumpPendingMessages();
                redraw();
                FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot returned{};
                returned.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
                state.Require(DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(samePopup, returned), L"Returned popup geometry must be available.");
                SelfTest::AppendSuiteTrace(SelfTest::SelfTestSuite::Commands,
                                           std::format(L"Inspection return: keyboard={}, key={}, pane={}, destination={}, foreground={}, focus={}, visible={}, "
                                                       L"same={}, footerOnly={}, taskFound={}",
                                                       keyboard,
                                                       activationKey,
                                                       pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left ? L"left" : L"right",
                                                       destination,
                                                       GetForegroundWindow() == samePopup,
                                                       GetFocus() == samePopup,
                                                       IsWindowVisible(samePopup) != FALSE,
                                                       fixture.fileOps->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest() == samePopup,
                                                       returned.footerOnly,
                                                       returned.found));
                state.Require(
                    fixture.fileOps->GetPopupHwndForSelfTest() == samePopup && IsWindowVisible(samePopup) && GetForegroundWindow() == samePopup &&
                        GetFocus() == samePopup && DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(samePopup, returned) &&
                        returned.hostedFocusedAutomationId ==
                            std::format(L"FileOperations.Action.{}.{}.0", static_cast<uint32_t>(hitKind), fixture.taskId.value()) &&
                        returned.hostedDefaultAutomationId == before.hostedDefaultAutomationId &&
                        returned.hostedCancelAutomationId == before.hostedCancelAutomationId &&
                        returned.conflictApplyToAllChecked == before.conflictApplyToAllChecked && returned.taskCollapsed == before.taskCollapsed &&
                        returned.scrollPosition == before.scrollPosition,
                    std::format(L"The ordinary File Operations command must restore the same invoking control, scroll, scope and engine default/cancel "
                                L"actions. Focus= {}, expected={}, scroll={}->{}, scope={}->{}, default={}->{}, cancel={}->{}, collapsed={}->{}",
                                returned.hostedFocusedAutomationId,
                                std::format(L"FileOperations.Action.{}.{}.0", static_cast<uint32_t>(hitKind), fixture.taskId.value()),
                                before.scrollPosition,
                                returned.scrollPosition,
                                before.conflictApplyToAllChecked,
                                returned.conflictApplyToAllChecked,
                                before.hostedDefaultAutomationId,
                                returned.hostedDefaultAutomationId,
                                before.hostedCancelAutomationId,
                                returned.hostedCancelAutomationId,
                                before.taskCollapsed,
                                returned.taskCollapsed));
                if (! state.failure.empty())
                    return false;
                capture(std::format(L"returned__{}__{}__{}__fr-FR.png",
                                    pane == FolderWindow::Pane::Left ? L"left" : L"right",
                                    destination ? L"destination" : L"source",
                                    activationKey == 0u         ? L"uia"
                                    : activationKey == VK_SPACE ? L"space"
                                                                : L"enter"),
                        IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SELECTED);
                if (! state.failure.empty())
                    return false;
            }
    capture(L"returned__fr-FR.png", IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SELECTED);
    // A short viewport must clip retained task controls above the footer, and
    // ordinary keyboard traversal must reveal the next action instead of focusing
    // an invisible control under that footer. No action is invoked here.
    RECT savedRect{};
    GetWindowRect(fixture.popup, &savedRect);
    const UINT popupDpi = GetDpiForWindow(fixture.popup);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    SetWindowPos(fixture.popup,
                 nullptr,
                 0,
                 0,
                 savedRect.right - savedRect.left,
                 MulDiv(420, static_cast<int>(popupDpi), 96),
                 SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
    redraw();
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_EXITSIZEMOVE, 0, 0);
    redraw();
    RECT shortRect{};
    GetWindowRect(fixture.popup, &shortRect);
    state.Require(shortRect.bottom - shortRect.top == MulDiv(420, static_cast<int>(popupDpi), 96),
                  L"A settled user resize must preserve the requested short height.");
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_VSCROLL, SB_BOTTOM, 0);
    redraw();
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> cancelProvider;
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> cancelFragment;
    state.Require(
        FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOP_BTN_CANCEL), cancelProvider.put()) &&
            cancelProvider && SUCCEEDED(cancelProvider.query_to(cancelFragment.put())) && cancelFragment && SUCCEEDED(cancelFragment->SetFocus()),
        L"The bottom conflict action must remain reachable by scrolling the short body.");
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_VSCROLL, SB_TOP, 0);
    redraw();
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYDOWN, VK_TAB, 0);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_KEYUP, VK_TAB, 0);
    PumpPendingMessages();
    redraw();
    FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupLayoutDebugSnapshot shortLayout{};
    shortLayout.taskId = fixture.taskId.value();
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> moreProvider;
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderFragment> moreFragment;
    UiaRect moreBounds{};
    POINT origin{};
    ClientToScreen(fixture.popup, &origin);
    state.Require(
        DebugGetFileOperationsPopupLayoutSnapshot(fixture.popup, shortLayout) && shortLayout.scrollPosition > 0 &&
            shortLayout.hostedFocusedAutomationId == std::format(L"FileOperations.Action.{}.{}.{}",
                                                                 static_cast<uint32_t>(FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictMore),
                                                                 fixture.taskId.value(),
                                                                 fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.overflowActionCount) &&
            FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(
                fixture.popup, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_CONFLICT_BTN_MORE), moreProvider.put()) &&
            moreProvider && SUCCEEDED(moreProvider.query_to(moreFragment.put())) && SUCCEEDED(moreFragment->get_BoundingRectangle(&moreBounds)) &&
            moreBounds.height > 0 && moreBounds.top >= static_cast<double>(origin.y) + shortLayout.listViewportRect.top &&
            moreBounds.top + moreBounds.height + 3.0 * static_cast<double>(popupDpi) / 96.0 <=
                static_cast<double>(origin.y) + shortLayout.listViewportRect.bottom,
        std::format(L"Tab must reveal the overflow action above the footer: scroll={} focused={} overflowCount={} boundsTop={} height={} viewportBottom={}.",
                    shortLayout.scrollPosition,
                    shortLayout.hostedFocusedAutomationId,
                    fixture.taskSnapshot.conflict.overflowActionCount,
                    moreBounds.top,
                    moreBounds.height,
                    shortLayout.listViewportRect.bottom));
    state.Require(SUCCEEDED(RedSalamander::TestSupport::SaveWindowScreenshot(fixture.popup, captures / L"short-body-focused-action__fr-FR.png")),
                  L"Capture the short viewport through the application harness.");
    SetWindowPos(fixture.popup,
                 nullptr,
                 savedRect.left,
                 savedRect.top,
                 savedRect.right - savedRect.left,
                 savedRect.bottom - savedRect.top,
                 SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
    SendMessageW(fixture.popup, WM_VSCROLL, SB_TOP, 0);
    redraw();
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    // A background/non-focus dispatch may select, but may not hide or activate.
    state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(GetAncestor(owner.GetHwnd(), GA_ROOT)), L"Restore owned pane foreground.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    SetFocus(owner.GetFolderViewHwnd(owner.GetActivePane()));
    const HWND dispatchFocus      = GetFocus();
    const HWND dispatchForeground = GetForegroundWindow();
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(
                      fixture.popup, {FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictInspectDestination, fixture.taskId.value(), 0u}) &&
                      waitForMessage(IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SELECTED) && GetFocus() == dispatchFocus && GetForegroundWindow() == dispatchForeground &&
                      IsWindowVisible(fixture.popup),
                  L"Background inspection dispatch must preserve OS focus and leave the popup available.");
    capture(L"selected__fr-FR.png", IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_SELECTED);
    // Exercise the one-shot guard directly: exact selection alone never grants a
    // later caller permission to steal focus after the user has moved elsewhere.
    state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(fixture.popup), L"Own popup foreground for the final focus witness.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    SetFocus(fixture.popup);
    std::optional<FileOperations::ConflictInspectionNavigationResult> ready;
    const auto ticketStatus = fixture.fileOps->BeginPendingConflictInspection(
        fixture.taskId.value(), context, true, [&](const FileOperations::ConflictInspectionNavigationResult& result) { ready = result; });
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + SelfTest::Scale(4000ms);
    while (! ready && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
    {
        PumpPendingMessages();
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(10ms);
    }
    state.Require((SUCCEEDED(ticketStatus) || ready) && ready && ready->focusTicket != 0u, L"An unchanged selection must issue one guarded focus ticket.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    state.Require(DirectedSelfTestInputWarning::TryActivateOwnedWindow(GetAncestor(owner.GetHwnd(), GA_ROOT)), L"Simulate an intervening owned focus change.");
    if (! state.failure.empty())
        return false;
    SetFocus(owner.GetFolderViewHwnd(owner.GetActivePane()));
    const HWND movedFocus = GetFocus();
    state.Require(! fixture.fileOps->CommitPendingConflictInspectionFocus(ready->focusTicket, fixture.popup) && GetFocus() == movedFocus &&
                      ! fixture.fileOps->CommitPendingConflictInspectionFocus(ready->focusTicket, fixture.popup),
                  L"A changed focus witness must refuse and consume the ticket, including replay, without stealing focus.");
    auto* task = fixture.fileOps->FindTask(fixture.taskId.value());
    if (! task)
        return false;
    const auto restoreContext          = wil::scope_exit([&]
    {
        std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
        task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = context;
    });
    auto missing                       = std::make_shared<FileOperations::ConflictInspectionContext>(*context);
    const auto absent                  = fixture.destDir / L"inspection-control-item-that-does-not-exist.txt";
    missing->destination->providerPath = absent.native();
    missing->destination->itemName     = absent.filename().native();
    {
        std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
        task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = missing;
    }
    state.Require(invoke(IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_DESTINATION) && waitForMessage(IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_MISSING),
                  L"A missing inspected item must expose a truthful accessible result through the real action path.");
    capture(L"missing-item__fr-FR.png", IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_MISSING);
    {
        std::scoped_lock lock(task->_conflictArbiter.mutex);
        task->_conflictArbiter.prompt.pathDisplay.inspection = context;
    }
    // The painted button still belongs to 'missing'. A same-task replacement
    // must be refused before navigation even before the next frame is painted.
    const auto previous = owner.GetCurrentPath(owner.GetActivePane());
    state.Require(DebugInvokeFileOperationsPopup(fixture.popup,
                                                 {FileOperationsPopupInternal::PopupHitTest::Kind::TaskConflictInspectSource, fixture.taskId.value(), 0u}) &&
                      owner.GetCurrentPath(owner.GetActivePane()) == previous,
                  L"A stale painted inspection button must not navigate a replacement prompt.");
    redraw();
    wil::com_ptr_nothrow<IRawElementProviderSimple> stale;
    state.Require(! FindMatchingWindowHostRawProvider(fixture.popup, 0, LoadStringResource(nullptr, IDS_FILEOPS_INSPECTION_MISSING), stale.put()),
                  L"A replacement prompt must not inherit the previous inspection result.");
    state.Require(TextFileEqualsForFileOpsPrompt(fixture.dest, "existing payload"), L"Inspection controls must never overwrite destination bytes.");
    return state.failure.empty();
}

[[nodiscard]] bool TestFileOperationsConflictResultRetention(CaseState& state) noexcept
{
    using State      = FolderWindow::FileOperationState;
    auto* operations = g_folderWindow.DebugGetFileOperationState();
    state.Require(operations != nullptr, L"File operations state is required.");
    if (! operations)
        return false;
    const auto previous = g_settings.fileOperations;
    auto restore        = wil::scope_exit([&] { g_settings.fileOperations = previous; });
    if (! g_settings.fileOperations)
        g_settings.fileOperations.emplace();
    g_settings.fileOperations->diagnosticsInfoEnabled = false;
    State::Task task(*operations);
    task._taskId = 0xF500000000000000ull + GetTickCount64();
    task._sourcePaths.resize(5u);
    auto cleanup = wil::scope_exit([&] { operations->DebugRemoveDiagnosticsForTask(task._taskId); });
    FileOperations::FileOperationItemResult result{};
    result.completion          = FileOperations::ItemCompletion::Completed;
